// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\core\backup\backup_service.dart ===

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/hive_database.dart';

class BackupResult {
  final bool success;
  final String? message;
  final String? backupJson;
  final String? checksum;

  BackupResult({
    required this.success,
    this.message,
    this.backupJson,
    this.checksum,
  });
}

class RestoreValidationResult {
  final bool isValid;
  final String? error;
  final Map<String, dynamic>? data;

  RestoreValidationResult({required this.isValid, this.error, this.data});
}

class BackupService {
  final HiveDatabase database;

  BackupService(this.database);

  static const int currentSchemaVersion = 1;

  /// Creates a complete JSON backup with metadata, timestamp, and SHA-256 integrity hash
  Future<BackupResult> createBackup() async {
    try {
      final exportData = await database.exportAllData();
      final tablesJson = jsonEncode(exportData['tables']);
      final checksum = sha256.convert(utf8.encode(tablesJson)).toString();

      final fullBackup = <String, dynamic>{
        'app': 'Fatoura',
        'schemaVersion': currentSchemaVersion,
        'createdAt': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
        'checksum': checksum,
        'data': exportData,
      };

      final jsonString = jsonEncode(fullBackup);
      return BackupResult(
        success: true,
        backupJson: jsonString,
        checksum: checksum,
        message: 'Backup generated successfully',
      );
    } catch (e) {
      return BackupResult(
        success: false,
        message: 'Failed to create backup: $e',
      );
    }
  }

  /// Validates a backup string before applying it
  RestoreValidationResult validateBackup(String rawJson) {
    try {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(rawJson) as Map<String, dynamic>?;
      if (decoded == null) {
        return RestoreValidationResult(
          isValid: false,
          error: 'Empty or invalid JSON',
        );
      }

      if (decoded['app'] != 'Fatoura') {
        return RestoreValidationResult(
          isValid: false,
          error: 'Unsupported backup archive: app identifier is not Fatoura',
        );
      }

      final schemaVersion = decoded['schemaVersion'] as int?;
      if (schemaVersion == null || schemaVersion > currentSchemaVersion) {
        return RestoreValidationResult(
          isValid: false,
          error:
              'Incompatible schema version: $schemaVersion (supported: <= $currentSchemaVersion)',
        );
      }

      final checksum = decoded['checksum'] as String?;
      final data = decoded['data'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
      if (data == null || data['tables'] == null) {
        return RestoreValidationResult(
          isValid: false,
          error: 'Backup payload is missing tables data',
        );
      }

      final tablesJson = jsonEncode(data['tables']);
      final calculatedChecksum = sha256
          .convert(utf8.encode(tablesJson))
          .toString();

      if (checksum != null && checksum != calculatedChecksum) {
        return RestoreValidationResult(
          isValid: false,
          error: 'Integrity check failed: checksum mismatch (corrupted data)',
        );
      }

      return RestoreValidationResult(isValid: true, data: data);
    } catch (e) {
      return RestoreValidationResult(
        isValid: false,
        error: 'JSON parsing failed: $e',
      );
    }
  }

  /// Restores verified backup into live database with a pre-restore rollback snapshot
  Future<BackupResult> restoreBackup(String rawJson) async {
    final validation = validateBackup(rawJson);
    if (!validation.isValid || validation.data == null) {
      return BackupResult(
        success: false,
        message: validation.error ?? 'Validation failed',
      );
    }

    // 1. Take safety snapshot of current live state
    Map<String, dynamic>? rollbackSnapshot;
    try {
      rollbackSnapshot = await database.exportAllData();
    } catch (_) {
      // In case export fails on an empty db
    }

    // 2. Perform restoration
    try {
      await database.restoreAllData(validation.data!);
      return BackupResult(success: true, message: 'Data restored successfully');
    } catch (e) {
      // Rollback if something failed
      if (rollbackSnapshot != null) {
        try {
          await database.restoreAllData(rollbackSnapshot);
        } catch (_) {}
      }
      return BackupResult(
        success: false,
        message: 'Restoration failed and rolled back: $e',
      );
    }
  }
}

      isArabic ? 'بيانات المتجر والنشاط' : 'Shop Profile';
  String get settingsTaxConfig =>
      isArabic ? 'إعدادات الضريبة الافتراضية' : 'Default Tax Configuration';
  String get taxEnabledLabel =>
      isArabic ? 'تفعيل الضريبة افتراضياً' : 'Enable Tax by Default';
  String get settingsInvoicePrefix =>
      isArabic ? 'نظام ترقيم الفواتير' : 'Invoice Numbering';

      ? 'تنسيق الترقيم اليومي المتسلسل: Ftr-DD.MM.YYYY-NNNN'
      : 'Daily sequential numbering format: Ftr-DD.MM.YYYY-NNNN';

  // Common Actions

  String get cancelBtn => isArabic ? 'إلغاء' : 'Cancel';
  String get deleteBtn => isArabic ? 'حذف' : 'Delete';

  String get allLabel => isArabic ? 'الكل' : 'All';
  String get detailsBtn => isArabic ? 'التفاصيل' : 'Details';
  String get closeBtn => isArabic ? 'إغلاق' : 'Close';
  String get successMsg =>
      isArabic ? 'تمت العملية بنجاح' : 'Operation completed successfully';

}

class _AppLocalizationsDelegate
    extends LocalizationsDelegate<AppLocalizations> {
  const _AppLocalizationsDelegate();

  @override
  bool isSupported(Locale locale) => ['ar', 'en'].contains(locale.languageCode);

  @override
  Future<AppLocalizations> load(Locale locale) {
    return SynchronousFuture<AppLocalizations>(AppLocalizations(locale));
  }

  @override
  bool shouldReload(_AppLocalizationsDelegate old) => false;
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\core\pdf\invoice_pdf_generator.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:pdf/pdf.dart';
import 'package:pdf/widgets.dart' as pw;

import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency_formatter.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';

class InvoicePdfGenerator {
  InvoicePdfGenerator._();

  static pw.Font? _regularFont;
  static pw.Font? _boldFont;

  static Future<void> _loadFonts() async {
    if (_regularFont == null || _boldFont == null) {
      final regData = await rootBundle.load('assets/fonts/AppFont-Regular.ttf');
      final boldData = await rootBundle.load('assets/fonts/AppFont-Bold.ttf');
      _regularFont = pw.Font.ttf(regData);
      _boldFont = pw.Font.ttf(boldData);
    }
  }

  /// Generates a professional, print-ready PDF for an invoice
  static Future<Uint8List> generateInvoicePdf({
    required Invoice invoice,
    required bool isArabic,
  }) async {
    await _loadFonts();

    final pdf = pw.Document(
      theme: pw.ThemeData.withFont(base: _regularFont!, bold: _boldFont!),
    );

    final textDirection = isArabic
        ? pw.TextDirection.rtl
        : pw.TextDirection.ltr;
    final alignment = isArabic ? pw.Alignment.topRight : pw.Alignment.topLeft;
    final dateFormat = DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm');
    final formattedDate = dateFormat.format(invoice.issueDate);

    // Labels
    final title = isArabic ? 'فاتورة مبيعات' : 'SALES INVOICE';
    final invNumLabel = isArabic ? 'رقم الفاتورة:' : 'Invoice No:';
    final dateLabel = isArabic ? 'تاريخ الإصدار:' : 'Date:';
    final billToLabel = isArabic ? 'بيانات العميل:' : 'Bill To:';
    final cashSaleLabel = isArabic
        ? 'عميل نقدي / مبيعات عامة'
        : 'Cash / Anonymous Sale';
    final taxNoLabel = isArabic ? 'الرقم الضريبي:' : 'Tax No:';
    final descHeader = isArabic ? 'البيان / الصنف' : 'Description';
    final qtyHeader = isArabic ? 'الكمية' : 'Qty';
    final priceHeader = isArabic ? 'سعر الوحدة' : 'Unit Price';
    final totalHeader = isArabic ? 'الإجمالي' : 'Total';
    final subtotalLabel = isArabic ? 'المجموع الفرعي:' : 'Subtotal:';
    final discountLabel = isArabic ? 'الخصم:' : 'Discount:';
    final taxableBaseLabel = isArabic ? 'الوعاء الضريبي:' : 'Taxable Base:';
    final taxLabel = isArabic
        ? 'ضريبة القيمة المضافة (${invoice.taxRate}%):'
        : 'Tax (${invoice.taxRate}%):';
    final finalTotalLabel = isArabic ? 'الإجمالي النهائي:' : 'Total Payable:';
    final paidLabel = isArabic ? 'المبلغ المسدد:' : 'Amount Paid:';
    final balanceLabel = isArabic ? 'الرصيد المتبقي:' : 'Remaining Balance:';
    final statusCancelled = isArabic ? 'ملغاة' : 'CANCELLED';

    pdf.addPage(
      pw.Page(
        pageFormat: PdfPageFormat.a4,
        textDirection: textDirection,
        margin: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(32),
        build: (context) {
          return pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              // Header Banner
              pw.Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  pw.Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: isArabic
                        ? pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start
                        : pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      pw.Text(
                        invoice.shopName.isNotEmpty
                            ? invoice.shopName
                            : (isArabic ? 'متجر فاتورة' : 'Fatoura Shop'),
                        style: pw.TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 20,
                          fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                          color: PdfColors.blue800,
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (invoice.shopPhone.isNotEmpty)
                        pw.Text(
                          invoice.shopPhone,
                          style: const pw.TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 10,
                            color: PdfColors.grey700,
                          ),
                        ),
                      if (invoice.shopAddress.isNotEmpty)
                        pw.Text(
                          invoice.shopAddress,
                          style: const pw.TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 10,
                            color: PdfColors.grey700,
                          ),
                        ),
                      if (invoice.shopTaxNumber.isNotEmpty)
                        pw.Text(
                          '$taxNoLabel ${invoice.shopTaxNumber}',
                          style: const pw.TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 10,
                            color: PdfColors.grey700,
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  pw.Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: isArabic
                        ? pw.CrossAxisAlignment.end
                        : pw.CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                    children: [
                      pw.Text(
                        title,
                        style: pw.TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 18,
                          fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                          color: invoice.isCancelled
                              ? PdfColors.red800
                              : PdfColors.blue900,
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (invoice.isCancelled)
                        pw.Container(
                          padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                            horizontal: 8,
                            vertical: 2,
                          ),
                          decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
                            color: PdfColors.red50,
                            borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(4),
                            border: pw.Border.all(color: PdfColors.red800),
                          ),
                          child: pw.Text(
                            statusCancelled,
                            style: pw.TextStyle(
                              color: PdfColors.red800,
                              fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                              fontSize: 12,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      pw.SizedBox(height: 6),
                      pw.Text(
                        '$invNumLabel ${invoice.invoiceNumber}',
                        style: pw.TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11,
                          fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                          color: PdfColors.grey900,
                        ),
                      ),
                      pw.Text(
                        '$dateLabel $formattedDate',
                        style: const pw.TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10,
                          color: PdfColors.grey700,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 16),
              pw.Divider(color: PdfColors.grey300),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 8),

              // Customer / Bill-To Section
              pw.Container(
                padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(10),
                decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
                  color: PdfColors.grey100,
                  borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(6),
                ),
                child: pw.Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                  children: [
                    pw.Text(
                      billToLabel,
                      style: pw.TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 10,
                        fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                        color: PdfColors.blue800,
                      ),
                    ),
                    pw.SizedBox(height: 4),
                    pw.Text(
                      invoice.customerName.isNotEmpty
                          ? invoice.customerName
                          : cashSaleLabel,
                      style: pw.TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (invoice.customerPhone.isNotEmpty)
                      pw.Text(
                        invoice.customerPhone,
                        style: const pw.TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10,
                          color: PdfColors.grey700,
                        ),
                      ),
                    if (invoice.customerAddress.isNotEmpty)
                      pw.Text(
                        invoice.customerAddress,
                        style: const pw.TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10,
                          color: PdfColors.grey700,
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 16),

              // Line Items Table
              pw.Table(
                border: pw.TableBorder.all(
                  color: PdfColors.grey300,
                  width: 0.5,
                ),
                columnWidths: {
                  0: const pw.FlexColumnWidth(4),
                  1: const pw.FlexColumnWidth(1.2),
                  2: const pw.FlexColumnWidth(2),
                  3: const pw.FlexColumnWidth(2),
                },
                children: [
                  // Table Header
                  pw.TableRow(
                    decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(color: PdfColors.blue50),
                    children: [
                      _buildTableCell(
                        descHeader,
                        isHeader: true,
                        align: alignment,
                      ),
                      _buildTableCell(
                        qtyHeader,
                        isHeader: true,
                        align: pw.Alignment.center,
                      ),
                      _buildTableCell(
                        priceHeader,
                        isHeader: true,
                        align: pw.Alignment.center,
                      ),
                      _buildTableCell(
                        totalHeader,
                        isHeader: true,
                        align: pw.Alignment.center,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  // Items
                  ...invoice.items.map(
                    (item) => pw.TableRow(
                      children: [
                        _buildTableCell(item.description, align: alignment),
                        _buildTableCell(
                          item.quantity.toString(),
                          align: pw.Alignment.center,
                        ),
                        _buildTableCell(
                          CurrencyFormatter.format(
                            item.unitPrice,
                            currency: invoice.currency,
                            isArabic: isArabic,
                          ),
                          align: pw.Alignment.center,
                        ),
                        _buildTableCell(
                          CurrencyFormatter.format(
                            item.lineTotal,
                            currency: invoice.currency,
                            isArabic: isArabic,
                          ),
                          align: pw.Alignment.center,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 16),

              // Summary Calculations Box
              pw.Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  // Notes Area
                  pw.Expanded(
                    flex: 5,
                    child: pw.Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        if (invoice.notes.isNotEmpty) ...[
                          pw.Text(
                            isArabic ? 'ملاحظات:' : 'Notes:',
                            style: pw.TextStyle(
                              fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                              fontSize: 10,
                              color: PdfColors.blue800,
                            ),
                          ),
                          pw.SizedBox(height: 4),
                          pw.Text(
                            invoice.notes,
                            style: const pw.TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 9,
                              color: PdfColors.grey800,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                        if (invoice.isCancelled &&
                            invoice.cancellationReason != null) ...[
                          pw.SizedBox(height: 8),
                          pw.Text(
                            isArabic ? 'سبب الإلغاء:' : 'Cancellation Reason:',
                            style: pw.TextStyle(
                              fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold,
                              fontSize: 10,
                              color: PdfColors.red800,
                            ),
                          ),
                          pw.Text(
                            invoice.cancellationReason!,
                            style: const pw.TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 9,
                              color: PdfColors.red700,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  pw.SizedBox(width: 24),

                  // Financial Totals
                  pw.Expanded(
                    flex: 5,
                    child: pw.Column(
                      children: [
                        _buildSummaryRow(
                          subtotalLabel,
                          CurrencyFormatter.format(
                            invoice.subtotal,
                            currency: invoice.currency,
                            isArabic: isArabic,
                          ),
                        ),
                        if (invoice.discountAmount > 0)
                          _buildSummaryRow(
                            discountLabel,
                            '-${CurrencyFormatter.format(invoice.discountAmount, currency: invoice.currency, isArabic: isArabic)}',
                            color: PdfColors.red700,
                          ),
                        if (invoice.taxAmount > 0) ...[
                          _buildSummaryRow(
                            taxableBaseLabel,
                            CurrencyFormatter.format(
                              invoice.taxableBase,
                              currency: invoice.currency,
                              isArabic: isArabic,
                            ),
                          ),
                          _buildSummaryRow(
                            taxLabel,
                            '+${CurrencyFormatter.format(invoice.taxAmount, currency: invoice.currency, isArabic: isArabic)}',
                          ),
                        ],
                        pw.Divider(color: PdfColors.blue800, thickness: 1),
                        _buildSummaryRow(
                          finalTotalLabel,
                          CurrencyFormatter.format(
                            invoice.finalTotal,
                            currency: invoice.currency,
                            isArabic: isArabic,
                          ),
                          isBold: true,
                          fontSize: 12,
                          color: PdfColors.blue900,
                        ),
                        _buildSummaryRow(
                          paidLabel,
                          CurrencyFormatter.format(
                            invoice.totalPaid,
                            currency: invoice.currency,
                            isArabic: isArabic,
                          ),
                          color: PdfColors.green800,
                        ),
                        _buildSummaryRow(
                          balanceLabel,
                          CurrencyFormatter.format(
                            invoice.remainingBalance,
                            currency: invoice.currency,
                            isArabic: isArabic,
                          ),
                          isBold: true,
                          color: invoice.remainingBalance > 0
                              ? PdfColors.red800
                              : PdfColors.green800,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              pw.Spacer(),

              // Footer Note
              pw.Center(
                child: pw.Text(
                  isArabic
                      ? 'تم إصدار هذه الوثيقة إلكترونياً بواسطة نظام فاتورة'
                      : 'Generated electronically via Fatoura Invoicing System',
                  style: const pw.TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 8,
                    color: PdfColors.grey500,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    );

    return pdf.save();
  }

  static pw.Widget _buildTableCell(
    String text, {
    bool isHeader = false,
    pw.Alignment align = pw.Alignment.centerLeft,
  }) {
    return pw.Container(
      alignment: align,
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 6),
      child: pw.Text(
        text,
        style: pw.TextStyle(
          fontSize: isHeader ? 9 : 9,
          fontWeight: isHeader ? pw.FontWeight.bold : pw.FontWeight.normal,
          color: isHeader ? PdfColors.blue900 : PdfColors.black,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  static pw.Widget _buildSummaryRow(
    String label,
    String value, {
    bool isBold = false,
    double fontSize = 9,
    PdfColor color = PdfColors.black,
  }) {
    return pw.Padding(
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
      child: pw.Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: pw.MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          pw.Text(
            label,
            style: pw.TextStyle(
              fontSize: fontSize,
              fontWeight: isBold ? pw.FontWeight.bold : pw.FontWeight.normal,
              color: color,
            ),
          ),
          pw.Text(
            value,
            style: pw.TextStyle(
              fontSize: fontSize,
              fontWeight: isBold ? pw.FontWeight.bold : pw.FontWeight.normal,
              color: color,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\core\precision\currency.dart ===

import 'package:decimal/decimal.dart';

/// Metadata definition for a supported currency in Fatoura.
class CurrencyInfo {
  final String code; // ISO 4217 code (e.g. EGP, SAR, USD)
  final String symbolAr; // Arabic symbol (e.g. ج.م, ر.س, $)
  final String symbolEn; // English symbol (e.g. EGP, SAR, USD)
  final String nameAr; // Arabic full name (e.g. الجنيه المصري)
  final String nameEn; // English full name (e.g. Egyptian Pound)
  final String majorUnitAr; // e.g. جنيه
  final String majorUnitEn; // e.g. Pound
  final String minorUnitAr; // e.g. قرش
  final String minorUnitEn; // e.g. Piastre
  final int minorUnitFactor; // e.g. 100 (1 EGP = 100 piastres)
  final int decimalDigits; // e.g. 2

  const CurrencyInfo({
    required this.code,
    required this.symbolAr,
    required this.symbolEn,
    required this.nameAr,
    required this.nameEn,
    required this.majorUnitAr,
    required this.majorUnitEn,
    required this.minorUnitAr,
    required this.minorUnitEn,
    this.minorUnitFactor = 100,
    this.decimalDigits = 2,
  });

  /// Converts a major decimal amount to integer minor units (e.g. 100.00 EGP -> 10000 piastres)
  int toMinorUnits(Decimal majorAmount) {
    return (majorAmount * Decimal.fromInt(minorUnitFactor)).toBigInt().toInt();
  }

  /// Converts integer minor units to major decimal amount (e.g. 10000 piastres -> 100.00 EGP)
  Decimal fromMinorUnits(int minorUnits) {
    return (Decimal.fromInt(minorUnits) / Decimal.fromInt(minorUnitFactor))
        .toDecimal(scaleOnInfinitePrecision: decimalDigits);
  }
}

/// Authoritative currency registry for Fatoura.
///
/// **Default Currency:** Egyptian Pound (`EGP`)
/// Major unit: Pound (جنيه)
/// Minor unit: Piastre (قرش)
/// Conversion: `1 EGP = 100 piastres`
///
/// Piastres are a subdivision of the Egyptian pound, not a separate currency.
class AppCurrencies {
  AppCurrencies._();

  static const String defaultCurrency = 'EGP';

  /// Egyptian Pound (Default)
  static const CurrencyInfo egp = CurrencyInfo(
    code: 'EGP',
    symbolAr: 'ج.م',
    symbolEn: 'EGP',
    nameAr: 'الجنيه المصري',
    nameEn: 'Egyptian Pound',
    majorUnitAr: 'جنيه',
    majorUnitEn: 'Pound',
    minorUnitAr: 'قرش',
    minorUnitEn: 'Piastre',
    minorUnitFactor: 100,
    decimalDigits: 2,
  );

  /// Saudi Riyal
  static const CurrencyInfo sar = CurrencyInfo(
    code: 'SAR',
    symbolAr: 'ر.س',
    symbolEn: 'SAR',
    nameAr: 'الريال السعودي',
    nameEn: 'Saudi Riyal',
    majorUnitAr: 'ريال',
    majorUnitEn: 'Riyal',
    minorUnitAr: 'هللة',
    minorUnitEn: 'Halala',
    minorUnitFactor: 100,
    decimalDigits: 2,
  );

  /// UAE Dirham
  static const CurrencyInfo aed = CurrencyInfo(
    code: 'AED',
    symbolAr: 'د.إ',
    symbolEn: 'AED',
    nameAr: 'الدرهم الإماراتي',
    nameEn: 'UAE Dirham',
    majorUnitAr: 'درهم',
    majorUnitEn: 'Dirham',
    minorUnitAr: 'فلس',
    minorUnitEn: 'Fils',
    minorUnitFactor: 100,
    decimalDigits: 2,
  );

  /// US Dollar
  static const CurrencyInfo usd = CurrencyInfo(
    code: 'USD',
    symbolAr: '\$',
    symbolEn: 'USD',
    nameAr: 'الدولار الأمريكي',
    nameEn: 'US Dollar',
    majorUnitAr: 'دولار',
    majorUnitEn: 'Dollar',
    minorUnitAr: 'سنت',
    minorUnitEn: 'Cent',
    minorUnitFactor: 100,
    decimalDigits: 2,
  );

  /// Euro
  static const CurrencyInfo eur = CurrencyInfo(
    code: 'EUR',
    symbolAr: '€',
    symbolEn: 'EUR',
    nameAr: 'اليورو',
    nameEn: 'Euro',
    majorUnitAr: 'يورو',
    majorUnitEn: 'Euro',
    minorUnitAr: 'سنت',
    minorUnitEn: 'Cent',
    minorUnitFactor: 100,
    decimalDigits: 2,
  );

  /// Kuwaiti Dinar
  static const CurrencyInfo kwd = CurrencyInfo(
    code: 'KWD',
    symbolAr: 'د.ك',
    symbolEn: 'KWD',
    nameAr: 'الدينار الكويتي',
    nameEn: 'Kuwaiti Dinar',
    majorUnitAr: 'دينار',
    majorUnitEn: 'Dinar',
    minorUnitAr: 'فلس',
    minorUnitEn: 'Fils',
    minorUnitFactor: 1000,
    decimalDigits: 3,
  );

  /// Qatari Riyal
  static const CurrencyInfo qar = CurrencyInfo(
    code: 'QAR',
    symbolAr: 'ر.ق',
    symbolEn: 'QAR',
    nameAr: 'الريال القطري',
    nameEn: 'Qatari Riyal',
    majorUnitAr: 'ريال',
    majorUnitEn: 'Riyal',
    minorUnitAr: 'درهم',
    minorUnitEn: 'Dirham',
    minorUnitFactor: 100,
    decimalDigits: 2,
  );

  static const List<CurrencyInfo> supported = [
    egp,
    sar,
    aed,
    usd,
    eur,
    kwd,
    qar,
  ];

  static List<String> get supportedCurrencyCodes =>
      supported.map((c) => c.code).toList();

  static final Map<String, CurrencyInfo> _byCode = {
    for (final c in supported) c.code.toUpperCase(): c,
  };

  /// Retrieves currency metadata by ISO code. Defaults to `EGP` if unrecognized.
  static CurrencyInfo get(String? code) {
    if (code == null) return egp;
    return _byCode[code.trim().toUpperCase()] ?? egp;
  }

  /// Converts piastres to EGP Decimal amount (e.g. 2550 -> 25.50)
  static Decimal piastresToEgp(int piastres) {
    return egp.fromMinorUnits(piastres);
  }

  /// Converts EGP Decimal amount to integer piastres (e.g. 25.50 -> 2550)
  static int egpToPiastres(Decimal egpAmount) {
    return egp.toMinorUnits(egpAmount);
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\core\precision\currency_formatter.dart ===

import 'package:decimal/decimal.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/money.dart';

/// Centralized, authoritative currency formatter for Fatoura.
/// Ensures standard formatting across Dashboard, Invoices, Payments, Reports, and PDFs.
class CurrencyFormatter {
  CurrencyFormatter._();

  /// Formats a numeric value (e.g. 100.00) with currency code/symbol.
  ///
  /// Examples:
  /// - Arabic: `100.00 ج.م`
  /// - English: `EGP 100.00`
  static String format(
    num amount, {
    String currency = AppCurrencies.defaultCurrency,
    bool isArabic = false,
    bool useFullName = false,
  }) {
    final info = AppCurrencies.get(currency);
    final formattedNumber = amount.toStringAsFixed(info.decimalDigits);

    if (isArabic) {
      final symbol = useFullName ? info.nameAr : info.symbolAr;
      return '$formattedNumber $symbol';
    } else {
      if (useFullName) {
        return '$formattedNumber ${info.nameEn}';
      }
      return '${info.symbolEn} $formattedNumber';
    }
  }

  /// Formats an exact Decimal amount.
  static String formatDecimal(
    Decimal amount, {
    String currency = AppCurrencies.defaultCurrency,
    bool isArabic = false,
    bool useFullName = false,
  }) {
    final info = AppCurrencies.get(currency);
    final formattedNumber = amount.toStringAsFixed(info.decimalDigits);

    if (isArabic) {
      final symbol = useFullName ? info.nameAr : info.symbolAr;
      return '$formattedNumber $symbol';
    } else {
      if (useFullName) {
        return '$formattedNumber ${info.nameEn}';
      }
      return '${info.symbolEn} $formattedNumber';
    }
  }

  /// Formats an immutable Money object.
  static String formatMoney(
    Money money, {
    bool isArabic = false,
    bool useFullName = false,
  }) {
    return formatDecimal(
      money.value,
      currency: money.currency,
      isArabic: isArabic,
      useFullName: useFullName,
    );
  }

  /// Formats integer minor units (e.g. 2550 piastres -> `25.50 ج.م` / `EGP 25.50`).
  static String formatMinorUnits(
    int minorUnits, {
    String currency = AppCurrencies.defaultCurrency,
    bool isArabic = false,
    bool useFullName = false,
  }) {
    final info = AppCurrencies.get(currency);
    final decimal = info.fromMinorUnits(minorUnits);
    return formatDecimal(
      decimal,
      currency: currency,
      isArabic: isArabic,
      useFullName: useFullName,
    );
  }

  /// Localized currency display name (e.g. "الجنيه المصري (EGP)" or "Egyptian Pound (EGP)").
  static String getLocalizedDisplayName(String currency, {bool isArabic = false}) {
    final info = AppCurrencies.get(currency);
    if (isArabic) {
      return '${info.nameAr} (${info.code})';
    } else {
      return '${info.nameEn} (${info.code})';
    }
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\core\precision\financial_calculator.dart ===

import 'package:decimal/decimal.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency.dart';

import 'money.dart';

enum DiscountType { fixed, percentage }

class LineItemCalculation {
  final Decimal quantity;
  final Money unitPrice;
  final Money lineTotal;

  LineItemCalculation({
    required this.quantity,
    required this.unitPrice,
    required this.lineTotal,
  });
}

class InvoiceCalculationResult {
  final List<LineItemCalculation> items;
  final Money subtotal;
  final DiscountType discountType;
  final Decimal discountInput;
  final Money discountAmount;
  final Money taxableBase;
  final Decimal taxRate;
  final Money taxAmount;
  final Money finalTotal;

  InvoiceCalculationResult({
    required this.items,
    required this.subtotal,
    required this.discountType,
    required this.discountInput,
    required this.discountAmount,
    required this.taxableBase,
    required this.taxRate,
    required this.taxAmount,
    required this.finalTotal,
  });
}

class FinancialCalculator {
  FinancialCalculator._();

  /// Calculates a single line item total: quantity * unitPrice
  static Money calculateLineTotal({
    required Decimal quantity,
    required Money unitPrice,
  }) {
    if (quantity <= Decimal.zero) {
      return Money.zero(unitPrice.currency);
    }
    return unitPrice * quantity;
  }

  /// Authoritative calculation engine for Invoices (Sales or Purchases)
  static InvoiceCalculationResult calculateInvoice({
    required List<({Decimal quantity, Money unitPrice})> rawItems,
    DiscountType discountType = DiscountType.fixed,
    Decimal? discountValue,
    Decimal? taxRatePercent,
    String currency = AppCurrencies.defaultCurrency,
  }) {
    final validTaxRate = taxRatePercent ?? Decimal.zero;
    final validDiscountInput = discountValue ?? Decimal.zero;

    // 1. Calculate each line total
    final lineItems = <LineItemCalculation>[];
    var subtotalDecimal = Decimal.zero;

    for (final item in rawItems) {
      final total = calculateLineTotal(
        quantity: item.quantity,
        unitPrice: item.unitPrice,
      );
      lineItems.add(
        LineItemCalculation(
          quantity: item.quantity,
          unitPrice: item.unitPrice,
          lineTotal: total,
        ),
      );
      subtotalDecimal += total.value;
    }

    final subtotal = Money.fromDecimal(subtotalDecimal, currency);

    // 2. Calculate discount amount
    Decimal discountDecimal = Decimal.zero;
    if (validDiscountInput > Decimal.zero && subtotalDecimal > Decimal.zero) {
      if (discountType == DiscountType.fixed) {
        discountDecimal = validDiscountInput > subtotalDecimal
            ? subtotalDecimal
            : validDiscountInput;
      } else {
        // Percentage discount
        final rate = (validDiscountInput / Decimal.fromInt(100)).toDecimal(
          scaleOnInfinitePrecision: 4,
        );
        final calc = subtotalDecimal * rate;
        discountDecimal = calc > subtotalDecimal ? subtotalDecimal : calc;
      }
    }
    final discountAmount = Money.fromDecimal(discountDecimal, currency);

    // 3. Taxable Base = subtotal - discount
    final taxableBaseDecimal = subtotalDecimal > discountDecimal
        ? subtotalDecimal - discountDecimal
        : Decimal.zero;
    final taxableBase = Money.fromDecimal(taxableBaseDecimal, currency);

    // 4. Tax amount = taxableBase * (taxRate / 100)
    Decimal taxDecimal = Decimal.zero;
    if (validTaxRate > Decimal.zero && taxableBaseDecimal > Decimal.zero) {
      final taxMultiplier = (validTaxRate / Decimal.fromInt(100)).toDecimal(
        scaleOnInfinitePrecision: 4,
      );
      taxDecimal = taxableBaseDecimal * taxMultiplier;
    }
    final taxAmount = Money.fromDecimal(taxDecimal, currency);

    // 5. Final Total = taxableBase + taxAmount
    final finalTotalDecimal = taxableBaseDecimal + taxDecimal;
    final finalTotal = Money.fromDecimal(finalTotalDecimal, currency);

    return InvoiceCalculationResult(
      items: lineItems,
      subtotal: subtotal,
      discountType: discountType,
      discountInput: validDiscountInput,
      discountAmount: discountAmount,
      taxableBase: taxableBase,
      taxRate: validTaxRate,
      taxAmount: taxAmount,
      finalTotal: finalTotal,
    );
  }

  /// Calculates remaining balance given final total, payments, and return credits
  static Money calculateRemainingBalance({
    required Money finalTotal,
    required Money totalPaid,
    Money? totalReturnCredits,
  }) {
    final credits = totalReturnCredits ?? Money.zero(finalTotal.currency);
    final totalDeductions = totalPaid + credits;

    if (totalDeductions.value >= finalTotal.value) {
      return Money.zero(finalTotal.currency);
    }
    return finalTotal - totalDeductions;
  }

  /// Computes return refund amount for returned items proportional to original net invoice
  static Money calculateReturnRefundAmount({
    required Decimal returnedQuantity,
    required Decimal originalSoldQuantity,
    required Money originalLineTotal,
    required Money originalInvoiceSubtotal,
    required Money originalInvoiceFinalTotal,
  }) {
    if (returnedQuantity <= Decimal.zero ||
        originalSoldQuantity <= Decimal.zero) {
      return Money.zero(originalLineTotal.currency);
    }

    // Proportion of this line being returned
    final lineRatio = (returnedQuantity / originalSoldQuantity).toDecimal(
      scaleOnInfinitePrecision: 6,
    );
    final returnedLineGross = originalLineTotal * lineRatio;

    if (originalInvoiceSubtotal.isZero) {
      return Money.zero(originalLineTotal.currency);
    }

    // Proportional net value based on original invoice finalTotal vs subtotal
    final netRatio =
        (originalInvoiceFinalTotal.value / originalInvoiceSubtotal.value)
            .toDecimal(scaleOnInfinitePrecision: 6);
    final refundDecimal = returnedLineGross.value * netRatio;

    return Money.fromDecimal(refundDecimal, originalLineTotal.currency);
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\core\precision\money.dart ===

import 'package:decimal/decimal.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency_formatter.dart';

/// Immutable high-precision Money value object.
/// Eliminates binary floating-point representation bugs (e.g. 0.1 + 0.2 = 0.30000000000000004).
///
/// **Default Currency:** Egyptian Pound (`EGP`), where `1 EGP = 100 piastres`.
class Money implements Comparable<Money> {
  final Decimal value;
  final String currency;

  const Money._(this.value, this.currency);

  factory Money.fromDecimal(
    Decimal value, [
    String currency = AppCurrencies.defaultCurrency,
  ]) {
    return Money._(value, currency);
  }

  factory Money.fromNum(
    num amount, [
    String currency = AppCurrencies.defaultCurrency,
  ]) {
    return Money._(Decimal.parse(amount.toString()), currency);
  }

  factory Money.fromString(
    String amount, [
    String currency = AppCurrencies.defaultCurrency,
  ]) {
    try {
      final sanitized = amount.trim().replaceAll(',', '');
      if (sanitized.isEmpty) {
        return Money._(Decimal.zero, currency);
      }
      return Money._(Decimal.parse(sanitized), currency);
    } catch (_) {
      return Money._(Decimal.zero, currency);
    }
  }

  /// Instantiates a Money object from integer minor units (e.g. 10000 piastres -> 100.00 EGP).
  factory Money.fromMinorUnits(
    int minorUnits, [
    String currency = AppCurrencies.defaultCurrency,
  ]) {
    final info = AppCurrencies.get(currency);
    return Money._(info.fromMinorUnits(minorUnits), currency);
  }

  /// Instantiates a Money object specifically from Egyptian piastres (1 EGP = 100 piastres).
  factory Money.fromPiastres(int piastres) {
    return Money.fromMinorUnits(piastres, 'EGP');
  }

  static Money zero([String currency = AppCurrencies.defaultCurrency]) =>
      Money._(Decimal.zero, currency);

  Money copyWith({Decimal? value, String? currency}) {
    return Money._(value ?? this.value, currency ?? this.currency);
  }

  Money operator +(Money other) {
    _assertSameCurrency(other);
    return Money._(value + other.value, currency);
  }

  Money operator -(Money other) {
    _assertSameCurrency(other);
    return Money._(value - other.value, currency);
  }

  Money operator *(Decimal multiplier) {
    return Money._(value * multiplier, currency);
  }

  Money operator /(Decimal divisor) {
    if (divisor == Decimal.zero) {
      throw ArgumentError('Division by zero in Money calculation.');
    }
    final info = AppCurrencies.get(currency);
    final res = (value / divisor).toDecimal(
      scaleOnInfinitePrecision: info.decimalDigits + 2,
    );
    return Money._(res, currency);
  }

  bool get isZero => value == Decimal.zero;
  bool get isNegative => value < Decimal.zero;
  bool get isPositive => value > Decimal.zero;

  double toDouble() => value.toDouble();

  /// Converts this amount into integer minor units (e.g. 100.00 EGP -> 10000 piastres).
  int toMinorUnits() {
    final info = AppCurrencies.get(currency);
    return info.toMinorUnits(value);
  }

  /// Converts this amount into Egyptian piastres if currency is EGP.
  int toPiastres() {
    return toMinorUnits();
  }

  /// Formats the money to fixed decimal places (default based on currency)
  String toStringAsFixed([int? fractionDigits]) {
    final digits = fractionDigits ?? AppCurrencies.get(currency).decimalDigits;
    return value.toStringAsFixed(digits);
  }

  /// Formats according to centralized currency standards.
  String format({bool isArabic = false, bool useFullName = false}) {
    return CurrencyFormatter.formatMoney(
      this,
      isArabic: isArabic,
      useFullName: useFullName,
    );
  }

  /// Legacy display with symbol/code
  String formatDisplay([int? fractionDigits]) {
    return format();
  }

  void _assertSameCurrency(Money other) {
    if (currency != other.currency) {
      throw ArgumentError(
        'Cannot perform arithmetic on different currencies: $currency vs ${other.currency}',
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  int compareTo(Money other) {
    _assertSameCurrency(other);
    return value.compareTo(other.value);
  }

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) =>
      identical(this, other) ||
      other is Money &&
          runtimeType == other.runtimeType &&
          value == other.value &&
          currency == other.currency;

  @override
  int get hashCode => value.hashCode ^ currency.hashCode;

  @override
  String toString() => '$value $currency';
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\core\responsive\responsive_layout.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

enum DeviceScreenType { mobile, tablet, desktop }

class ResponsiveLayout extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget mobile;
  final Widget? tablet;
  final Widget? desktop;

  const ResponsiveLayout({
    super.key,
    required this.mobile,
    this.tablet,
    this.desktop,
  });

  static const double mobileBreakpoint = 600;
  static const double tabletBreakpoint = 1024;

  static DeviceScreenType getDeviceType(BuildContext context) {
    final width = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width;
    if (width >= tabletBreakpoint) {
      return DeviceScreenType.desktop;
    } else if (width >= mobileBreakpoint) {
      return DeviceScreenType.tablet;
    }
    return DeviceScreenType.mobile;
  }

  static bool isMobile(BuildContext context) =>
      getDeviceType(context) == DeviceScreenType.mobile;

  static bool isTablet(BuildContext context) =>
      getDeviceType(context) == DeviceScreenType.tablet;

  static bool isDesktop(BuildContext context) =>
      getDeviceType(context) == DeviceScreenType.desktop;

  static bool isWide(BuildContext context) =>
      MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width >= mobileBreakpoint;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        if (constraints.maxWidth >= tabletBreakpoint) {
          return desktop ?? tablet ?? mobile;
        }
        if (constraints.maxWidth >= mobileBreakpoint) {
          return tablet ?? mobile;
        }
        return mobile;
      },
    );
  }
}

/// Helper container that caps max width on desktop displays so content doesn't stretch awkwardly.
class ResponsiveContentContainer extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final double maxWidth;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;

  const ResponsiveContentContainer({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.maxWidth = 1200,
    this.padding = const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: ConstrainedBox(
        constraints: BoxConstraints(maxWidth: maxWidth),
        child: Padding(padding: padding, child: child),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\core\settings\app_settings_cubit.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

class AppSettingsState extends Equatable {
  final ThemeMode themeMode;
  final Locale locale;
  final String defaultCurrency;

  const AppSettingsState({
    this.themeMode = ThemeMode.system,
    this.locale = const Locale('ar'),
    this.defaultCurrency = 'EGP',
  });

  AppSettingsState copyWith({
    ThemeMode? themeMode,
    Locale? locale,
    String? defaultCurrency,
  }) {
    return AppSettingsState(
      themeMode: themeMode ?? this.themeMode,
      locale: locale ?? this.locale,
      defaultCurrency: defaultCurrency ?? this.defaultCurrency,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [themeMode, locale, defaultCurrency];
}

class AppSettingsCubit extends Cubit<AppSettingsState> {
  final SharedPreferences prefs;

  AppSettingsCubit(this.prefs) : super(const AppSettingsState()) {
    _loadSettings();
  }

  static const String _keyThemeMode = 'app_theme_mode';
  static const String _keyLanguage = 'app_language';
  static const String _keyCurrency = 'app_default_currency';

  void _loadSettings() {
    final themeStr = prefs.getString(_keyThemeMode);
    final themeMode = switch (themeStr) {
      'light' => ThemeMode.light,
      'dark' => ThemeMode.dark,
      _ => ThemeMode.system,
    };

    final langStr = prefs.getString(_keyLanguage) ?? 'ar';
    final locale = Locale(langStr);

    final currency = prefs.getString(_keyCurrency) ?? 'EGP';

    emit(
      AppSettingsState(
        themeMode: themeMode,
        locale: locale,
        defaultCurrency: currency,
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> setThemeMode(ThemeMode mode) async {
    final str = switch (mode) {
      ThemeMode.light => 'light',
      ThemeMode.dark => 'dark',
      ThemeMode.system => 'system',
    };
    await prefs.setString(_keyThemeMode, str);
    emit(state.copyWith(themeMode: mode));
  }

  Future<void> setLocale(Locale locale) async {
    await prefs.setString(_keyLanguage, locale.languageCode);
    emit(state.copyWith(locale: locale));
  }

  Future<void> setDefaultCurrency(String currency) async {
    await prefs.setString(_keyCurrency, currency);
    emit(state.copyWith(defaultCurrency: currency));
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\core\storage\hive_database.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:hive_flutter/hive_flutter.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';

class HiveBoxes {
  HiveBoxes._();

  static const String shopProfile = 'box_shop_profile';
  static const String parties = 'box_parties';
  static const String products = 'box_products';
  static const String invoices = 'box_invoices';
  static const String payments = 'box_payments';
  static const String salesReturns = 'box_sales_returns';
  static const String purchases = 'box_purchases';
  static const String auditLogs = 'box_audit_logs';
  static const String sequenceCounters = 'box_sequence_counters';

  static const List<String> allBoxes = [
    shopProfile,
    parties,
    products,
    invoices,
    payments,
    salesReturns,
    purchases,
    auditLogs,
    sequenceCounters,
  ];
}

class HiveDatabase {
  static final HiveDatabase instance = HiveDatabase._internal();

  HiveDatabase._internal();

  bool _isInitialized = false;

  bool get isInitialized => _isInitialized;

  Future<void> init({String? customPath}) async {
    if (_isInitialized) return;

    if (customPath != null) {
      Hive.init(customPath);
    } else {
      if (!kIsWeb) {
        final appDocDir = await getApplicationDocumentsDirectory();
        final path = '${appDocDir.path}/fatoura_data';
        await Hive.initFlutter(path);
      } else {
        await Hive.initFlutter();
      }
    }

    // Open all core boxes
    for (final boxName in HiveBoxes.allBoxes) {
      if (!Hive.isBoxOpen(boxName)) {
        await Hive.openBox<Map>(boxName);
      }
    }

    _isInitialized = true;
  }

  Box<Map> getBox(String boxName) {
    if (!Hive.isBoxOpen(boxName)) {
      throw StateError(
        'Hive box $boxName is not open. Ensure HiveDatabase.init() was called.',
      );
    }
    return Hive.box<Map>(boxName);
  }

  Box<Map> get shopProfileBox => getBox(HiveBoxes.shopProfile);
  Box<Map> get partiesBox => getBox(HiveBoxes.parties);
  Box<Map> get productsBox => getBox(HiveBoxes.products);
  Box<Map> get invoicesBox => getBox(HiveBoxes.invoices);
  Box<Map> get paymentsBox => getBox(HiveBoxes.payments);
  Box<Map> get salesReturnsBox => getBox(HiveBoxes.salesReturns);
  Box<Map> get purchasesBox => getBox(HiveBoxes.purchases);
  Box<Map> get auditLogsBox => getBox(HiveBoxes.auditLogs);
  Box<Map> get sequenceCountersBox => getBox(HiveBoxes.sequenceCounters);

  /// Dumps all database boxes into a JSON-serializable Map for backup
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> exportAllData() async {
    final export = <String, dynamic>{
      'version': 1,
      'exportedAt': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
      'tables': <String, dynamic>{},
    };

    final tables = <String, dynamic>{};
    for (final boxName in HiveBoxes.allBoxes) {
      final box = getBox(boxName);
      final boxData = <String, dynamic>{};
      for (final key in box.keys) {
        final val = box.get(key);
        if (val != null) {
          boxData[key.toString()] = Map<String, dynamic>.from(val);
        }
      }
      tables[boxName] = boxData;
    }

    export['tables'] = tables;
    return export;
  }

  /// Restores all database boxes from a verified backup payload
  Future<void> restoreAllData(Map<String, dynamic> backupData) async {
    final tables = backupData['tables'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
    if (tables == null) {
      throw ArgumentError('Malformed backup data: missing tables map');
    }

    for (final boxName in HiveBoxes.allBoxes) {
      final box = getBox(boxName);
      await box.clear();

      final boxData = tables[boxName] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
      if (boxData != null) {
        for (final entry in boxData.entries) {
          await box.put(
            entry.key,
            Map<String, dynamic>.from(entry.value as Map),
          );
        }
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> clearAll() async {
    for (final boxName in HiveBoxes.allBoxes) {
      final box = getBox(boxName);
      await box.clear();
    }
  }

  Future<void> close() async {
    await Hive.close();
    _isInitialized = false;
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\core\storage\sequence_manager.dart ===

import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

import 'hive_database.dart';

class SequenceManager {
  final HiveDatabase database;

  SequenceManager(this.database);

  /// Generates the next atomic sequential number for a given document prefix.
  /// Format: `[PREFIX]-DD.MM.YYYY-NNNN` (e.g. `Ftr-19.10.2026-0001`)
  Future<String> getNextNumber({
    required String prefix,
    DateTime? date,
    required String targetBoxName,
    required String numberFieldName,
  }) async {
    final localDate = date ?? DateTime.now();
    final dateKey = DateFormat('dd.MM.yyyy').format(localDate);
    final counterKey = '${prefix}_$dateKey';

    final counterBox = database.sequenceCountersBox;
    final currentCounterMap = counterBox.get(counterKey);
    var nextIndex = 1;

    if (currentCounterMap != null && currentCounterMap['lastNumber'] != null) {
      nextIndex = (currentCounterMap['lastNumber'] as int) + 1;
    }

    // Safety reconciliation: Inspect the target box to ensure no collision exists
    final targetBox = database.getBox(targetBoxName);
    while (true) {
      final formattedNumber =
          '$prefix-$dateKey-${nextIndex.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}';

      bool conflict = false;
      for (final key in targetBox.keys) {
        final doc = targetBox.get(key);
        if (doc != null && doc[numberFieldName] == formattedNumber) {
          conflict = true;
          break;
        }
      }

      if (!conflict) {
        // Save the allocated index
        await counterBox.put(counterKey, {
          'date': dateKey,
          'prefix': prefix,
          'lastNumber': nextIndex,
          'updatedAt': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
        });
        return formattedNumber;
      }

      // If collision was found in existing records, increment and re-check
      nextIndex++;
    }
  }

  Future<String> getNextInvoiceNumber([DateTime? date]) {
    return getNextNumber(
      prefix: 'Ftr',
      date: date,
      targetBoxName: HiveBoxes.invoices,
      numberFieldName: 'invoiceNumber',
    );
  }

  Future<String> getNextPurchaseNumber([DateTime? date]) {
    return getNextNumber(
      prefix: 'Pur',
      date: date,
      targetBoxName: HiveBoxes.purchases,
      numberFieldName: 'purchaseNumber',
    );
  }

  Future<String> getNextReturnNumber([DateTime? date]) {
    return getNextNumber(
      prefix: 'Ret',
      date: date,
      targetBoxName: HiveBoxes.salesReturns,
      numberFieldName: 'returnNumber',
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Central brand tokens as specified in Fatoura requirements.
class AppColors {
  AppColors._();

  // Brand Palette
  static const Color primary = Color(0xFF2563EB); // #2563EB
  static const Color secondary = Color(0xFF3B82F6); // #3B82F6
  static const Color lightBackground = Color(0xFFF1F5F9); // Light neutral bg
  static const Color lightSurface = Color(0xFFFFFFFF); // Pure white cards
  static const Color lightSurfaceSubtle = Color(0xFFF8FAFC);
  static const Color lightBorder = Color(0xFFE2E8F0);
  static const Color borderLight = lightBorder;
  static const Color primaryLight = secondary;

  // Dark Palette
  static const Color darkBackground = Color(0xFF0B1220); // #0B1220
  static const Color darkSurface = Color(0xFF1E293B); // #1E293B
  static const Color darkSurfaceSubtle = Color(0xFF172033);
  static const Color darkBorder = Color(0xFF334155);

  // Text Tokens
  static const Color textPrimaryLight = Color(0xFF0F172A);
  static const Color textSecondaryLight = Color(0xFF64748B);
  static const Color textPrimaryDark = Color(0xFFF8FAFC);
  static const Color textSecondaryDark = Color(0xFF94A3B8);

  // Semantic Indicators
  static const Color success = Color(0xFF10B981); // Paid, positive
  static const Color successBackground = Color(0xFFECFDF5);
  static const Color error = Color(0xFFEF4444); // Failures, overdue, cancel
  static const Color errorBackground = Color(0xFFFEF2F2);
  static const Color warning = Color(0xFFF59E0B); // Pending, caution
  static const Color warningBackground = Color(0xFFFFFBEB);
  static const Color info = Color(0xFF0284C7);
  static const Color infoBackground = Color(0xFFF0F9FF);
}

class AppDimensions {
  AppDimensions._();

  static const double radiusSmall = 8.0;
  static const double radiusMedium = 12.0;
  static const double radiusLarge = 16.0;

  static const double paddingSmall = 8.0;
  static const double paddingMedium = 16.0;
  static const double paddingLarge = 24.0;
  static const double paddingXLarge = 32.0;

  static const double buttonHeight = 48.0;
  static const double inputHeight = 50.0;
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\core\theme\app_theme.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

import 'app_colors.dart';

class AppTheme {
  AppTheme._();

  static const String fontFamily = 'AppFont';

  static ThemeData get lightTheme {
    final colorScheme = const ColorScheme.light(
      primary: AppColors.primary,
      onPrimary: Colors.white,
      secondary: AppColors.secondary,
      onSecondary: Colors.white,
      surface: AppColors.lightSurface,
      onSurface: AppColors.textPrimaryLight,
      error: AppColors.error,
      onError: Colors.white,
    );

    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      fontFamily: fontFamily,
      colorScheme: colorScheme,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: AppColors.lightBackground,
      canvasColor: AppColors.lightSurface,
      dividerColor: AppColors.lightBorder,
      appBarTheme: const AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.lightSurface,
        foregroundColor: AppColors.textPrimaryLight,
        elevation: 0,
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0.5,
        centerTitle: false,
        systemOverlayStyle: SystemUiOverlayStyle.dark,
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
          fontSize: 18,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
          color: AppColors.textPrimaryLight,
        ),
      ),
      cardTheme: CardThemeData(
        color: AppColors.lightSurface,
        elevation: 0,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.lightBorder, width: 1),
        ),
        margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      ),
      elevatedButtonTheme: ElevatedButtonThemeData(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          minimumSize: const Size(64, AppDimensions.buttonHeight),
          elevation: 0,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          ),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontFamily: fontFamily,
            fontSize: 15,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      outlinedButtonTheme: OutlinedButtonThemeData(
        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: AppColors.primary,
          minimumSize: const Size(64, AppDimensions.buttonHeight),
          side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary, width: 1.2),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          ),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontFamily: fontFamily,
            fontSize: 15,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      textButtonTheme: TextButtonThemeData(
        style: TextButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: AppColors.primary,
          textStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontFamily: fontFamily,
            fontSize: 14,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: AppColors.lightSurface,
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: 16,
          vertical: 14,
        ),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.lightBorder),
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.lightBorder),
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.primary, width: 1.5),
        ),
        errorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.error),
        ),
        focusedErrorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.error, width: 1.5),
        ),
        hintStyle: const TextStyle(
          color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
          fontSize: 14,
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
        ),
        labelStyle: const TextStyle(
          color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
          fontSize: 14,
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
        ),
      ),
      dividerTheme: const DividerThemeData(
        color: AppColors.lightBorder,
        thickness: 1,
        space: 1,
      ),
      navigationRailTheme: const NavigationRailThemeData(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.lightSurface,
        selectedIconTheme: IconThemeData(color: AppColors.primary),
        unselectedIconTheme: IconThemeData(color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight),
        selectedLabelTextStyle: TextStyle(
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
          color: AppColors.primary,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          fontSize: 12,
        ),
        unselectedLabelTextStyle: TextStyle(
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
          color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
          fontSize: 12,
        ),
      ),
      bottomNavigationBarTheme: const BottomNavigationBarThemeData(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.lightSurface,
        selectedItemColor: AppColors.primary,
        unselectedItemColor: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
        type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
        elevation: 8,
      ),
    );
  }

  static ThemeData get darkTheme {
    final colorScheme = const ColorScheme.dark(
      primary: AppColors.primary,
      onPrimary: Colors.white,
      secondary: AppColors.secondary,
      onSecondary: Colors.white,
      surface: AppColors.darkSurface,
      onSurface: AppColors.textPrimaryDark,
      error: AppColors.error,
      onError: Colors.white,
    );

    return ThemeData(
      useMaterial3: true,
      fontFamily: fontFamily,
      colorScheme: colorScheme,
      scaffoldBackgroundColor: AppColors.darkBackground,
      canvasColor: AppColors.darkSurface,
      dividerColor: AppColors.darkBorder,
      appBarTheme: const AppBarTheme(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.darkSurface,
        foregroundColor: AppColors.textPrimaryDark,
        elevation: 0,
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0.5,
        centerTitle: false,
        systemOverlayStyle: SystemUiOverlayStyle.light,
        titleTextStyle: TextStyle(
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
          fontSize: 18,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
          color: AppColors.textPrimaryDark,
        ),
      ),
      cardTheme: CardThemeData(
        color: AppColors.darkSurface,
        elevation: 0,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.darkBorder, width: 1),
        ),
        margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      ),
      elevatedButtonTheme: ElevatedButtonThemeData(
        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
          foregroundColor: Colors.white,
          minimumSize: const Size(64, AppDimensions.buttonHeight),
          elevation: 0,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          ),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontFamily: fontFamily,
            fontSize: 15,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      outlinedButtonTheme: OutlinedButtonThemeData(
        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: AppColors.secondary,
          minimumSize: const Size(64, AppDimensions.buttonHeight),
          side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.secondary, width: 1.2),
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          ),
          textStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontFamily: fontFamily,
            fontSize: 15,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      textButtonTheme: TextButtonThemeData(
        style: TextButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: AppColors.secondary,
          textStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontFamily: fontFamily,
            fontSize: 14,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      inputDecorationTheme: InputDecorationTheme(
        filled: true,
        fillColor: AppColors.darkSurfaceSubtle,
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: 16,
          vertical: 14,
        ),
        border: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.darkBorder),
        ),
        enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.darkBorder),
        ),
        focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.secondary, width: 1.5),
        ),
        errorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.error),
        ),
        focusedErrorBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppDimensions.radiusMedium),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.error, width: 1.5),
        ),
        hintStyle: const TextStyle(
          color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
          fontSize: 14,
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
        ),
        labelStyle: const TextStyle(
          color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
          fontSize: 14,
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
        ),
      ),
      dividerTheme: const DividerThemeData(
        color: AppColors.darkBorder,
        thickness: 1,
        space: 1,
      ),
      navigationRailTheme: const NavigationRailThemeData(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.darkSurface,
        selectedIconTheme: IconThemeData(color: AppColors.secondary),
        unselectedIconTheme: IconThemeData(color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark),
        selectedLabelTextStyle: TextStyle(
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
          color: AppColors.secondary,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
          fontSize: 12,
        ),
        unselectedLabelTextStyle: TextStyle(
          fontFamily: fontFamily,
          color: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
          fontSize: 12,
        ),
      ),
      bottomNavigationBarTheme: const BottomNavigationBarThemeData(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.darkSurface,
        selectedItemColor: AppColors.secondary,
        unselectedItemColor: AppColors.textSecondaryDark,
        type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
        elevation: 8,
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/features/backup/presentation/pages/backup_page.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/presentation/pages/parties_page.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/products/presentation/pages/products_page.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/features/settings/presentation/pages/developer_info_page.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/responsive/responsive_layout.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/theme/app_colors.dart';

class AppScaffold extends StatefulWidget {
  final int initialIndex;

  const AppScaffold({super.key, this.initialIndex = 0});

  @override
  State<AppScaffold> createState() => AppScaffoldState();
}

class AppScaffoldState extends State<AppScaffold> {
  late int _currentIndex;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _currentIndex = widget.initialIndex;
  }

  void navigateToTab(int index) {
    if (index >= 0 && index < _pages.length) {
      setState(() {
        _currentIndex = index;
      });
    }
  }

  final List<Widget> _pages = const [
    DashboardPage(),
    InvoicesListPage(),
    PurchasesPage(),
    PartiesPage(),
    ProductsPage(),
    ReturnsPage(),
    ReportsPage(),
    BackupPage(),
    SettingsPage(),
  ];

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final isDesktopOrTablet = ResponsiveLayout.isWide(context);

    if (isDesktopOrTablet) {
      return Scaffold(
        body: Row(
          children: [
            LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (context, constraint) {
                return SingleChildScrollView(
                  child: ConstrainedBox(
                    constraints:
                        BoxConstraints(minHeight: constraint.maxHeight),
                    child: IntrinsicHeight(
                      child: NavigationRail(
                        selectedIndex: _currentIndex,
                        onDestinationSelected: (idx) =>
                            setState(() => _currentIndex = idx),
                        labelType: NavigationRailLabelType.all,
                        leading: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16.0),
                          child: Column(
                            children: [
                              ClipRRect(
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                                child: Image.asset(
                                  'assets/images/logo.png',
                                  width: 44,
                                  height: 44,
                                  errorBuilder:
                                      (context, error, stackTrace) =>
                                          Container(
                                    width: 44,
                                    height: 44,
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: AppColors.primary,
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                                    ),
                                    child: const Icon(
                                      Icons.receipt_long,
                                      color: Colors.white,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 6),
                              Text(
                                l10n.appName,
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  fontSize: 13,
                                  color: AppColors.primary,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        destinations: [
                          NavigationRailDestination(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.dashboard_outlined),
                            selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.dashboard),
                            label: Text(l10n.navDashboard),
                          ),
                          NavigationRailDestination(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.receipt_long_outlined),
                            selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.receipt_long),
                            label: Text(l10n.navInvoices),
                          ),
                          NavigationRailDestination(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.shopping_cart_outlined),
                            selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.shopping_cart),
                            label: Text(l10n.navPurchases),
                          ),
                          NavigationRailDestination(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.people_outline),
                            selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.people),
                            label: Text(l10n.navCustomers),
                          ),
                          NavigationRailDestination(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.inventory_2_outlined),
                            selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.inventory_2),
                            label: Text(l10n.navProducts),
                          ),
                          NavigationRailDestination(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.assignment_return_outlined),
                            selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.assignment_return),
                            label: Text(l10n.navReturns),
                          ),
                          NavigationRailDestination(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.bar_chart_outlined),
                            selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.bar_chart),
                            label: Text(l10n.navReports),
                          ),
                          NavigationRailDestination(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.cloud_sync_outlined),
                            selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.cloud_sync),
                            label: Text(l10n.navBackup),
                          ),
                          NavigationRailDestination(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.settings_outlined),
                            selectedIcon: const Icon(Icons.settings),
                            label: Text(l10n.navSettings),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
            const VerticalDivider(width: 1, thickness: 1),
            Expanded(
              child: IndexedStack(index: _currentIndex, children: _pages),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    // Mobile Layout: Bottom navigation for primary 5 actions + drawer for secondary
    return Scaffold(
      drawer: Drawer(
        child: ListView(
          padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          children: [
            DrawerHeader(
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.primary),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    child: Image.asset(
                      'assets/images/logo.png',
                      width: 48,
                      height: 48,
                      errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => const Icon(
                        Icons.receipt_long,
                        color: Colors.white,
                        size: 40,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text(
                    l10n.appName,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      fontSize: 20,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Text(
                    l10n.appTagline,
                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 11),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.dashboard_outlined),
              title: Text(l10n.navDashboard),
              selected: _currentIndex == 0,
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                setState(() => _currentIndex = 0);
              },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.receipt_long_outlined),
              title: Text(l10n.navInvoices),
              selected: _currentIndex == 1,
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                setState(() => _currentIndex = 1);
              },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.shopping_cart_outlined),
              title: Text(l10n.navPurchases),
              selected: _currentIndex == 2,
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                setState(() => _currentIndex = 2);
              },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.people_outline),
              title: Text(l10n.navCustomers),
              selected: _currentIndex == 3,
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                setState(() => _currentIndex = 3);
              },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.inventory_2_outlined),
              title: Text(l10n.navProducts),
              selected: _currentIndex == 4,
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                setState(() => _currentIndex = 4);
              },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.assignment_return_outlined),
              title: Text(l10n.navReturns),
              selected: _currentIndex == 5,
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                setState(() => _currentIndex = 5);
              },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.bar_chart_outlined),
              title: Text(l10n.navReports),
              selected: _currentIndex == 6,
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                setState(() => _currentIndex = 6);

            ListTile(
