              selected: _currentIndex == 7,
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                setState(() => _currentIndex = 7);
              },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.settings_outlined),
              title: Text(l10n.navSettings),
              selected: _currentIndex == 8,
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                setState(() => _currentIndex = 8);
              },
            ),
            const Divider(),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.verified_outlined, color: AppColors.primary),
              title: Text(l10n.isArabic ? 'عن فاتورة' : 'About Fatoura'),
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                Navigator.push(
                  context,
                  MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const AboutPage()),
                );
              },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.code_outlined, color: AppColors.primary),
              title: Text(l10n.isArabic ? 'معلومات المطور' : 'Developer Information'),
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(context);
                Navigator.push(
                  context,
                  MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const DeveloperInfoPage()),
                );
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: IndexedStack(index: _currentIndex, children: _pages),
      bottomNavigationBar: BottomNavigationBar(
        currentIndex: _currentIndex >= 4 ? 4 : _currentIndex,
        onTap: (idx) {
          if (idx == 4) {
            _showMoreMenu(context);
          } else {
            setState(() => _currentIndex = idx);
          }
        },
        items: [
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.dashboard_outlined),

            label: l10n.navPurchases,
          ),
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.people_outline),
            activeIcon: const Icon(Icons.people),
            label: l10n.navCustomers,

            icon: const Icon(Icons.more_horiz),
            label: l10n.allLabel,
          ),
        ],
      ),

  void _showMoreMenu(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final isAr = l10n.isArabic;

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      showDragHandle: true,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(16)),
      ),
      builder: (ctx) {
        return SafeArea(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.inventory_2_outlined,
                      color: AppColors.primary),
                  title: Text(l10n.navProducts),

                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.pop(ctx);
                    setState(() => _currentIndex = 4);
                  },
                ),
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.assignment_return_outlined,
                      color: AppColors.primary),
                  title: Text(l10n.navReturns),
                  selected: _currentIndex == 5,
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.pop(ctx);
                    setState(() => _currentIndex = 5);
                  },
                ),
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.bar_chart_outlined,
                      color: AppColors.primary),
                  title: Text(l10n.navReports),
                  selected: _currentIndex == 6,
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.pop(ctx);
                    setState(() => _currentIndex = 6);
                  },
                ),
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.cloud_sync_outlined,
                      color: AppColors.primary),
                  title: Text(l10n.navBackup),
                  selected: _currentIndex == 7,
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.pop(ctx);
                    setState(() => _currentIndex = 7);
                  },
                ),
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.settings_outlined,
                      color: AppColors.primary),
                  title: Text(l10n.navSettings),
                  selected: _currentIndex == 8,
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.pop(ctx);
                    setState(() => _currentIndex = 8);
                  },
                ),
                const Divider(),
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.verified_outlined,
                      color: AppColors.primary),
                  title: Text(isAr ? 'عن فاتورة' : 'About Fatoura'),
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.pop(ctx);
                    Navigator.push(
                      context,
                      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const AboutPage()),
                    );
                  },
                ),
                ListTile(
                  leading: const Icon(Icons.code_outlined,
                      color: AppColors.primary),
                  title: Text(
                    isAr ? 'معلومات المطور' : 'Developer Information',
                  ),
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.pop(ctx);
                    Navigator.push(
                      context,
                      MaterialPageRoute(
                        builder: (_) => const DeveloperInfoPage(),
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\audit\data\repositories\audit_repository_impl.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/hive_database.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/audit/domain/entities/audit_log.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/audit/domain/repositories/audit_repository.dart';

class AuditRepositoryImpl implements AuditRepository {
  final HiveDatabase database;

  AuditRepositoryImpl(this.database);

  @override
  Future<void> logAction(AuditLog log) async {
    final box = database.auditLogsBox;
    await box.put(log.id, log.toMap());
  }

  @override
  Future<List<AuditLog>> getLogs({
    String? documentType,
    String? documentId,
    int limit = 50,
  }) async {
    final box = database.auditLogsBox;
    final results = <AuditLog>[];

    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final log = AuditLog.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
        if (documentType != null && log.documentType != documentType) {
          continue;
        }
        if (documentId != null && log.documentId != documentId) {
          continue;
        }
        results.add(log);
      }
    }

    results.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp.compareTo(a.timestamp));
    if (results.length > limit) {
      return results.sublist(0, limit);
    }
    return results;
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\audit\domain\entities\audit_log.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

class AuditLog extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String action;
  final String documentType;
  final String documentId;
  final String description;
  final String actor;
  final DateTime timestamp;
  final Map<String, dynamic> metadata;

  const AuditLog({
    required this.id,
    required this.action,
    required this.documentType,
    required this.documentId,
    required this.description,
    this.actor = 'Local User',
    required this.timestamp,
    this.metadata = const {},
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'action': action,
      'documentType': documentType,
      'documentId': documentId,
      'description': description,
      'actor': actor,
      'timestamp': timestamp.toIso8601String(),
      'metadata': metadata,
    };
  }

  factory AuditLog.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return AuditLog(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? '',
      action: map['action'] as String? ?? '',
      documentType: map['documentType'] as String? ?? '',
      documentId: map['documentId'] as String? ?? '',
      description: map['description'] as String? ?? '',
      actor: map['actor'] as String? ?? 'Local User',
      timestamp:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['timestamp'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      metadata: Map<String, dynamic>.from(map['metadata'] as Map? ?? {}),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [id, action, documentType, documentId, timestamp];
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\audit\domain\repositories\audit_repository.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/features/audit/domain/entities/audit_log.dart';

abstract class AuditRepository {
  Future<void> logAction(AuditLog log);
  Future<List<AuditLog>> getLogs({
    String? documentType,
    String? documentId,
    int limit = 50,
  });
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\backup\presentation\cubit\backup_cubit.dart ===

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:file_picker/file_picker.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/backup/backup_service.dart';

abstract class BackupState extends Equatable {
  const BackupState();
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class BackupInitial extends BackupState {}

class BackupLoading extends BackupState {}

class BackupCreatedSuccess extends BackupState {
  final String filePath;
  final String checksum;

  const BackupCreatedSuccess({required this.filePath, required this.checksum});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [filePath, checksum];
}

class RestoreSuccess extends BackupState {
  final String message;

  const RestoreSuccess(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class BackupError extends BackupState {
  final String message;

  const BackupError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class BackupCubit extends Cubit<BackupState> {
  final BackupService backupService;

  BackupCubit(this.backupService) : super(BackupInitial());

  Future<void> createBackupToFile() async {
    emit(BackupLoading());
    try {
      final res = await backupService.createBackup();
      if (!res.success || res.backupJson == null) {
        emit(BackupError(res.message ?? 'Failed to create backup'));
        return;
      }

      final dateStr = DateFormat('yyyy-MM-dd_HH-mm').format(DateTime.now());
      final filename = 'Fatoura_Backup_$dateStr.fatoura.json';

      // Save to application documents or downloads folder
      final directory = await getApplicationDocumentsDirectory();
      final backupDir = Directory('${directory.path}/FatouraBackups');
      if (!await backupDir.exists()) {
        await backupDir.create(recursive: true);
      }

      final file = File('${backupDir.path}/$filename');
      await file.writeAsString(res.backupJson!);

      emit(
        BackupCreatedSuccess(filePath: file.path, checksum: res.checksum ?? ''),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      emit(BackupError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> restoreBackupFromFile() async {
    try {
      final files = await FilePicker.pickFiles(
        type: FileType.custom,
        allowedExtensions: ['json'],
      );

      if (files.isEmpty || files.first.path == null) {
        return; // User cancelled
      }

      emit(BackupLoading());
      final file = File(files.first.path!);
      final rawJson = await file.readAsString();

      final restoreRes = await backupService.restoreBackup(rawJson);
      if (restoreRes.success) {
        emit(
          RestoreSuccess(restoreRes.message ?? 'Backup restored successfully.'),
        );
      } else {
        emit(BackupError(restoreRes.message ?? 'Failed to restore backup'));
      }
    } catch (e) {
      emit(BackupError('Restoration error: $e'));
    }
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

class BackupPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const BackupPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.backupSectionTitle)),
      body: ResponsiveContentContainer(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: BlocConsumer<BackupCubit, BackupState>(
          listener: (context, state) {
            if (state is BackupCreatedSuccess) {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text('${l10n.backupSuccess}\n${state.filePath}'),
                  backgroundColor: AppColors.success,
                  duration: const Duration(seconds: 4),
                ),
              );
            } else if (state is RestoreSuccess) {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text(state.message),
                  backgroundColor: AppColors.success,
                ),
              );
            } else if (state is BackupError) {
              ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                SnackBar(
                  content: Text(state.message),
                  backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
                  duration: const Duration(seconds: 4),
                ),
              );
            }
          },
          builder: (context, state) {
            return SingleChildScrollView(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  Card(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
                      child: Column(
                        children: [
                          const Icon(
                            Icons.cloud_sync_outlined,
                            size: 64,
                            color: AppColors.primary,
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 16),
                          Text(
                            l10n.backupSectionTitle,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 18,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 8),
                          Text(
                            l10n.backupNotice,
                            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 13,
                              color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 32),
                          ElevatedButton.icon(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.download),
                            label: Text(l10n.createBackupBtn),
                            onPressed: state is BackupLoading
                                ? null
                                : () => context
                                      .read<BackupCubit>()
                                      .createBackupToFile(),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 16),
                          OutlinedButton.icon(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.upload),
                            label: Text(l10n.restoreBackupBtn),
                            onPressed: state is BackupLoading
                                ? null
                                : () async {
                                    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
                                      context: context,
                                      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
                                        title: Text(l10n.restoreBackupBtn),
                                        content: Text(l10n.restoreWarning),
                                        actions: [
                                          TextButton(
                                            onPressed: () =>
                                                Navigator.pop(ctx, false),
                                            child: Text(l10n.cancelBtn),
                                          ),
                                          ElevatedButton(
                                            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                                              backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
                                            ),
                                            onPressed: () =>
                                                Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
                                            child: Text(l10n.restoreBackupBtn),
                                          ),
                                        ],
                                      ),
                                    );

                                    if (confirmed == true && context.mounted) {
                                      context
                                          .read<BackupCubit>()
                                          .restoreBackupFromFile();
                                    }
                                  },
                          ),
                          if (state is BackupLoading) ...[
                            const SizedBox(height: 24),
                            const CircularProgressIndicator(),
                          ],
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\dashboard\presentation\cubit\dashboard_cubit.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/repositories/invoice_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/repositories/party_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/payments/domain/repositories/payment_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/purchases/domain/repositories/purchase_repository.dart';

class DailySalePoint extends Equatable {
  final DateTime date;
  final double amount;

  const DailySalePoint({required this.date, required this.amount});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [date, amount];
}

class DashboardData extends Equatable {
  final double totalSales;
  final double totalCollected;
  final double customerDebts;
  final double supplierPayables;
  final double totalPurchases;
  final List<Invoice> recentInvoices;
  final List<DailySalePoint> salesTrend;
  final String currency;

  const DashboardData({
    required this.totalSales,
    required this.totalCollected,
    required this.customerDebts,
    required this.supplierPayables,
    required this.totalPurchases,
    required this.recentInvoices,
    required this.salesTrend,
    required this.currency,
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    totalSales,
    totalCollected,
    customerDebts,
    supplierPayables,
    totalPurchases,
    recentInvoices,
    salesTrend,
    currency,
  ];
}

abstract class DashboardState extends Equatable {
  const DashboardState();
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class DashboardInitial extends DashboardState {}

class DashboardLoading extends DashboardState {}

class DashboardLoaded extends DashboardState {
  final DashboardData data;

  const DashboardLoaded(this.data);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [data];
}

class DashboardError extends DashboardState {
  final String message;

  const DashboardError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class DashboardCubit extends Cubit<DashboardState> {
  final InvoiceRepository invoiceRepository;
  final PurchaseRepository purchaseRepository;
  final PaymentRepository paymentRepository;
  final PartyRepository partyRepository;

  DashboardCubit({
    required this.invoiceRepository,
    required this.purchaseRepository,
    required this.paymentRepository,
    required this.partyRepository,
  }) : super(DashboardInitial());

  Future<void> loadDashboardMetrics([String defaultCurrency = 'EGP']) async {
    emit(DashboardLoading());
    try {
      // 1. Fetch issued sales invoices
      final invoices = await invoiceRepository.getInvoices();
      final activeInvoices = invoices.where((i) => i.isIssued).toList();

      var totalSales = 0.0;
      var totalReceivables = 0.0;
      for (final inv in activeInvoices) {
        totalSales += inv.finalTotal;
        totalReceivables += inv.remainingBalance;
      }

      // 2. Fetch customer payments
      final payments = await paymentRepository.getPayments(
        isCustomerPayment: true,
      );
      final totalCollected = payments.fold(0.0, (sum, p) => sum + p.amount);

      // 3. Fetch purchases
      final purchases = await purchaseRepository.getPurchases();
      final activePurchases = purchases
          .where((p) => p.documentStatus == DocumentStatus.issued)
          .toList();
      var totalPurchases = 0.0;
      var totalPayables = 0.0;
      for (final pur in activePurchases) {
        totalPurchases += pur.finalTotal;
        totalPayables += pur.remainingPayable;
      }

      // 4. Sales Trend (Last 7 Days)
      final now = DateTime.now();
      final trendPoints = <DailySalePoint>[];
      for (int i = 6; i >= 0; i--) {
        final day = DateTime(
          now.year,
          now.month,
          now.day,
        ).subtract(Duration(days: i));
        final nextDay = day.add(const Duration(days: 1));

        final daySales = activeInvoices
            .where(
              (inv) =>
                  inv.issueDate.isAfter(
                    day.subtract(const Duration(seconds: 1)),
                  ) &&
                  inv.issueDate.isBefore(nextDay),
            )
            .fold(0.0, (sum, inv) => sum + inv.finalTotal);

        trendPoints.add(DailySalePoint(date: day, amount: daySales));
      }

      final recent = invoices.take(5).toList();

      emit(
        DashboardLoaded(
          DashboardData(
            totalSales: totalSales,
            totalCollected: totalCollected,
            customerDebts: totalReceivables,
            supplierPayables: totalPayables,
            totalPurchases: totalPurchases,
            recentInvoices: recent,
            salesTrend: trendPoints,
            currency: defaultCurrency,
          ),
        ),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      emit(DashboardError(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

                                ),

                                  final cubit = context.read<DashboardCubit>();
                                  Navigator.push(
                                    context,
                                    MaterialPageRoute(
                                      builder: (_) => InvoiceDetailPage(
                                        invoiceId: invoice.id,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ).then((_) {
                                    cubit.loadDashboardMetrics();
                                  });
                                },
                              );
                            },
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            );
          }
          return const SizedBox.shrink();
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMetricsGrid(BuildContext context, DashboardData data) {

            (constraints.maxWidth - (crossAxisCount - 1) * 12) / crossAxisCount;

                  data.totalCollected,

  Widget _buildSalesTrendChart(List<DailySalePoint> points, String currency) {
    if (points.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();

    final maxVal = points.fold(
      1.0,
      (max, p) => p.amount > max ? p.amount : max,
    );

    return LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        final barWidth = (constraints.maxWidth / points.length) * 0.5;

        return Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
          children: points.map((p) {
            final ratio = maxVal > 0
                ? (p.amount / maxVal).clamp(0.05, 1.0)
                : 0.05;
            final dayName = DateFormat('E').format(p.date);

            return SizedBox(
              height: constraints.maxHeight,
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                children: [
                  SizedBox(
                    height: 16,
                    child: Center(
                      child: Text(
                        p.amount > 0 ? p.amount.toStringAsFixed(0) : '',
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Align(
                      alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                      child: Container(
                        width: barWidth.clamp(16.0, 36.0),
                        height: ((constraints.maxHeight - 48) * ratio)
                            .clamp(4.0, (constraints.maxHeight - 48).clamp(4.0, double.infinity)),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: p.amount > 0
                              ? AppColors.primary
                              : AppColors.lightBorder,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  SizedBox(
                    height: 16,
                    child: Center(
                      child: Text(
                        dayName,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11,
                          color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }).toList(),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\invoices\data\repositories\invoice_repository_impl.dart ===

import 'package:decimal/decimal.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/financial_calculator.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/money.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/hive_database.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/repositories/invoice_repository.dart';

class InvoiceRepositoryImpl implements InvoiceRepository {
  final HiveDatabase database;

  InvoiceRepositoryImpl(this.database);

  @override
  Future<List<Invoice>> getInvoices({
    String? customerId,
    DocumentStatus? documentStatus,
    PaymentStatus? paymentStatus,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
    String? query,
  }) async {
    final box = database.invoicesBox;
    final results = <Invoice>[];

    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final invoice = Invoice.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));

        // Filters
        if (customerId != null && invoice.customerId != customerId) {
          continue;
        }
        if (documentStatus != null &&
            invoice.documentStatus != documentStatus) {
          continue;
        }
        if (paymentStatus != null && invoice.paymentStatus != paymentStatus) {
          continue;
        }
        if (fromDate != null &&
            invoice.issueDate.isBefore(
              DateTime(fromDate.year, fromDate.month, fromDate.day),
            )) {
          continue;
        }
        if (toDate != null &&
            invoice.issueDate.isAfter(
              DateTime(toDate.year, toDate.month, toDate.day, 23, 59, 59),
            )) {
          continue;
        }
        if (query != null && query.trim().isNotEmpty) {
          final q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
          final matchesNumber = invoice.invoiceNumber.toLowerCase().contains(q);
          final matchesCustomer = invoice.customerName.toLowerCase().contains(
            q,
          );
          final matchesItem = invoice.items.any(
            (item) => item.description.toLowerCase().contains(q),
          );
          if (!matchesNumber && !matchesCustomer && !matchesItem) {
            continue;
          }
        }

        results.add(invoice);
      }
    }

    // Sort by issueDate descending
    results.sort((a, b) => b.issueDate.compareTo(a.issueDate));
    return results;
  }

  @override
  Future<Invoice?> getInvoiceById(String id) async {
    final box = database.invoicesBox;
    final map = box.get(id);
    if (map == null) return null;
    return Invoice.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
  }

  @override
  Future<Invoice?> getInvoiceByNumber(String invoiceNumber) async {
    final box = database.invoicesBox;
    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null && map['invoiceNumber'] == invoiceNumber) {
        return Invoice.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  @override
  Future<void> saveInvoice(Invoice invoice) async {
    final box = database.invoicesBox;
    await box.put(invoice.id, invoice.toMap());
  }

  @override
  Future<void> cancelInvoice(String invoiceId, String reason) async {
    final invoice = await getInvoiceById(invoiceId);
    if (invoice == null) {
      throw ArgumentError('Invoice $invoiceId does not exist.');
    }
    if (invoice.isCancelled) {
      return; // Already cancelled
    }

    final cancelled = invoice.copyWith(
      documentStatus: DocumentStatus.cancelled,
      cancelledAt: DateTime.now(),
      cancellationReason: reason.trim(),
      updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
    );
    await saveInvoice(cancelled);
  }

  @override
  Future<void> recordPaymentOnInvoice({
    required String invoiceId,
    required double paymentAmount,
  }) async {
    final invoice = await getInvoiceById(invoiceId);
    if (invoice == null) {
      throw ArgumentError('Invoice $invoiceId does not exist.');
    }

    final newTotalPaidDecimal =
        Decimal.parse(invoice.totalPaid.toString()) +
        Decimal.parse(paymentAmount.toString());

    final finalTotal = Money.fromNum(invoice.finalTotal, invoice.currency);
    final totalPaid = Money.fromDecimal(newTotalPaidDecimal, invoice.currency);

    final newRemaining = FinancialCalculator.calculateRemainingBalance(
      finalTotal: finalTotal,
      totalPaid: totalPaid,
    );

    final PaymentStatus newPaymentStatus;
    if (newRemaining.isZero) {
      newPaymentStatus = PaymentStatus.fullyPaid;
    } else if (newTotalPaidDecimal > Decimal.zero) {
      newPaymentStatus = PaymentStatus.partiallyPaid;
    } else {
      newPaymentStatus = PaymentStatus.unpaid;
    }

    final updated = invoice.copyWith(
      totalPaid: totalPaid.toDouble(),
      remainingBalance: newRemaining.toDouble(),
      paymentStatus: newPaymentStatus,
      updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
    );
    await saveInvoice(updated);
  }

  @override
  Future<void> recordReturnCreditOnInvoice({
    required String invoiceId,
    required double creditAmount,
  }) async {
    final invoice = await getInvoiceById(invoiceId);
    if (invoice == null) return;

    final credit = Money.fromNum(creditAmount, invoice.currency);
    final finalTotal = Money.fromNum(invoice.finalTotal, invoice.currency);
    final totalPaid = Money.fromNum(invoice.totalPaid, invoice.currency);

    final newRemaining = FinancialCalculator.calculateRemainingBalance(
      finalTotal: finalTotal,
      totalPaid: totalPaid,
      totalReturnCredits: credit,
    );

    final PaymentStatus newPaymentStatus;
    if (newRemaining.isZero && (invoice.totalPaid > 0 || creditAmount > 0)) {
      newPaymentStatus = PaymentStatus.refunded;
    } else if (invoice.totalPaid > 0) {
      newPaymentStatus = PaymentStatus.partiallyPaid;
    } else {
      newPaymentStatus = PaymentStatus.unpaid;
    }

    final updated = invoice.copyWith(
      remainingBalance: newRemaining.toDouble(),
      paymentStatus: newPaymentStatus,
      updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
    );
    await saveInvoice(updated);
  }

  @override
  Future<void> deleteDraftInvoice(String invoiceId) async {
    final invoice = await getInvoiceById(invoiceId);
    if (invoice != null && invoice.isDraft) {
      final box = database.invoicesBox;
      await box.delete(invoiceId);
    }
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\invoices\domain\entities\invoice.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

enum DocumentStatus { draft, issued, cancelled }

enum PaymentStatus { unpaid, partiallyPaid, fullyPaid, refunded }

class InvoiceItem extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String? productId;
  final String description;
  final double quantity;
  final double unitPrice;
  final double lineTotal;

  const InvoiceItem({
    required this.id,
    this.productId,
    required this.description,
    required this.quantity,
    required this.unitPrice,
    required this.lineTotal,
  });

  InvoiceItem copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? productId,
    String? description,
    double? quantity,
    double? unitPrice,
    double? lineTotal,
  }) {
    return InvoiceItem(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      productId: productId ?? this.productId,
      description: description ?? this.description,
      quantity: quantity ?? this.quantity,
      unitPrice: unitPrice ?? this.unitPrice,
      lineTotal: lineTotal ?? this.lineTotal,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'productId': productId,
      'description': description,
      'quantity': quantity,
      'unitPrice': unitPrice,
      'lineTotal': lineTotal,
    };
  }

  factory InvoiceItem.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return InvoiceItem(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? '',
      productId: map['productId'] as String?,
      description: map['description'] as String? ?? '',
      quantity: (map['quantity'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 1.0,
      unitPrice: (map['unitPrice'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      lineTotal: (map['lineTotal'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    id,
    productId,
    description,
    quantity,
    unitPrice,
    lineTotal,
  ];
}

class Invoice extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String invoiceNumber; // e.g. Ftr-19.10.2026-0001
  final DateTime issueDate;

  // Shop snapshot
  final String shopName;
  final String shopPhone;
  final String shopAddress;
  final String shopTaxNumber;

  // Customer snapshot (Optional for cash sales)
  final String? customerId;
  final String customerName;
  final String customerPhone;
  final String customerAddress;

  final List<InvoiceItem> items;

  // Financials
  final double subtotal;
  final String discountType; // 'fixed' or 'percentage'
  final double discountInput;
  final double discountAmount;
  final double taxableBase;
  final double taxRate;
  final double taxAmount;
  final double finalTotal;
  final double totalPaid;
  final double remainingBalance;

  final DocumentStatus documentStatus;
  final PaymentStatus paymentStatus;

  // Cancellation metadata
  final DateTime? cancelledAt;
  final String? cancellationReason;

  final String currency;
  final String notes;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  const Invoice({
    required this.id,
    required this.invoiceNumber,
    required this.issueDate,
    required this.shopName,
    this.shopPhone = '',
    this.shopAddress = '',
    this.shopTaxNumber = '',
    this.customerId,
    this.customerName = '',
    this.customerPhone = '',
    this.customerAddress = '',
    required this.items,
    required this.subtotal,
    this.discountType = 'fixed',
    this.discountInput = 0.0,
    this.discountAmount = 0.0,
    required this.taxableBase,
    this.taxRate = 0.0,
    this.taxAmount = 0.0,
    required this.finalTotal,
    this.totalPaid = 0.0,
    required this.remainingBalance,
    this.documentStatus = DocumentStatus.issued,
    this.paymentStatus = PaymentStatus.unpaid,
    this.cancelledAt,
    this.cancellationReason,
    this.currency = 'EGP',
    this.notes = '',
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  bool get isDraft => documentStatus == DocumentStatus.draft;
  bool get isIssued => documentStatus == DocumentStatus.issued;
  bool get isCancelled => documentStatus == DocumentStatus.cancelled;
  bool get isCashSale => customerId == null || customerId!.isEmpty;

  Invoice copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? invoiceNumber,
    DateTime? issueDate,
    String? shopName,
    String? shopPhone,
    String? shopAddress,
    String? shopTaxNumber,
    String? customerId,
    String? customerName,
    String? customerPhone,
    String? customerAddress,
    List<InvoiceItem>? items,
    double? subtotal,
    String? discountType,
    double? discountInput,
    double? discountAmount,
    double? taxableBase,
    double? taxRate,
    double? taxAmount,
    double? finalTotal,
    double? totalPaid,
    double? remainingBalance,
    DocumentStatus? documentStatus,
    PaymentStatus? paymentStatus,
    DateTime? cancelledAt,
    String? cancellationReason,
    String? currency,
    String? notes,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return Invoice(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      invoiceNumber: invoiceNumber ?? this.invoiceNumber,
      issueDate: issueDate ?? this.issueDate,
      shopName: shopName ?? this.shopName,
      shopPhone: shopPhone ?? this.shopPhone,
      shopAddress: shopAddress ?? this.shopAddress,
      shopTaxNumber: shopTaxNumber ?? this.shopTaxNumber,
      customerId: customerId ?? this.customerId,
      customerName: customerName ?? this.customerName,
      customerPhone: customerPhone ?? this.customerPhone,
      customerAddress: customerAddress ?? this.customerAddress,
      items: items ?? this.items,
      subtotal: subtotal ?? this.subtotal,
      discountType: discountType ?? this.discountType,
      discountInput: discountInput ?? this.discountInput,
      discountAmount: discountAmount ?? this.discountAmount,
      taxableBase: taxableBase ?? this.taxableBase,
      taxRate: taxRate ?? this.taxRate,
      taxAmount: taxAmount ?? this.taxAmount,
      finalTotal: finalTotal ?? this.finalTotal,
      totalPaid: totalPaid ?? this.totalPaid,
      remainingBalance: remainingBalance ?? this.remainingBalance,
      documentStatus: documentStatus ?? this.documentStatus,
      paymentStatus: paymentStatus ?? this.paymentStatus,
      cancelledAt: cancelledAt ?? this.cancelledAt,
      cancellationReason: cancellationReason ?? this.cancellationReason,
      currency: currency ?? this.currency,
      notes: notes ?? this.notes,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'invoiceNumber': invoiceNumber,
      'issueDate': issueDate.toIso8601String(),
      'shopName': shopName,
      'shopPhone': shopPhone,
      'shopAddress': shopAddress,
      'shopTaxNumber': shopTaxNumber,
      'customerId': customerId,
      'customerName': customerName,
      'customerPhone': customerPhone,
      'customerAddress': customerAddress,
      'items': items.map((x) => x.toMap()).toList(),
      'subtotal': subtotal,
      'discountType': discountType,
      'discountInput': discountInput,
      'discountAmount': discountAmount,
      'taxableBase': taxableBase,
      'taxRate': taxRate,
      'taxAmount': taxAmount,
      'finalTotal': finalTotal,
      'totalPaid': totalPaid,
      'remainingBalance': remainingBalance,
      'documentStatus': documentStatus.name,
      'paymentStatus': paymentStatus.name,
      'cancelledAt': cancelledAt?.toIso8601String(),
      'cancellationReason': cancellationReason,
      'currency': currency,
      'notes': notes,
      'createdAt': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updatedAt': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory Invoice.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return Invoice(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? '',
      invoiceNumber: map['invoiceNumber'] as String? ?? '',
      issueDate:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['issueDate'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      shopName: map['shopName'] as String? ?? '',
      shopPhone: map['shopPhone'] as String? ?? '',
      shopAddress: map['shopAddress'] as String? ?? '',
      shopTaxNumber: map['shopTaxNumber'] as String? ?? '',
      customerId: map['customerId'] as String?,
      customerName: map['customerName'] as String? ?? '',
      customerPhone: map['customerPhone'] as String? ?? '',
      customerAddress: map['customerAddress'] as String? ?? '',
      items:
          (map['items'] as List<dynamic>?)
              ?.map(
                (x) => InvoiceItem.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(x as Map)),
              )
              .toList() ??
          [],
      subtotal: (map['subtotal'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      discountType: map['discountType'] as String? ?? 'fixed',
      discountInput: (map['discountInput'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      discountAmount: (map['discountAmount'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      taxableBase: (map['taxableBase'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      taxRate: (map['taxRate'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      taxAmount: (map['taxAmount'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      finalTotal: (map['finalTotal'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      totalPaid: (map['totalPaid'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      remainingBalance: (map['remainingBalance'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      documentStatus: DocumentStatus.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == map['documentStatus'],
        orElse: () => DocumentStatus.issued,
      ),
      paymentStatus: PaymentStatus.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == map['paymentStatus'],
        orElse: () => PaymentStatus.unpaid,
      ),
      cancelledAt: map['cancelledAt'] != null
          ? DateTime.tryParse(map['cancelledAt'] as String)
          : null,
      cancellationReason: map['cancellationReason'] as String?,
      currency: map['currency'] as String? ?? 'EGP',
      notes: map['notes'] as String? ?? '',
      createdAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['createdAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['updatedAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    id,
    invoiceNumber,
    issueDate,
    customerId,
    customerName,
    items,
    finalTotal,
    totalPaid,
    remainingBalance,
    documentStatus,
    paymentStatus,
    cancelledAt,
    createdAt,
  ];
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\invoices\domain\repositories\invoice_repository.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';

abstract class InvoiceRepository {
  Future<List<Invoice>> getInvoices({
    String? customerId,
    DocumentStatus? documentStatus,
    PaymentStatus? paymentStatus,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
    String? query,
  });
  Future<Invoice?> getInvoiceById(String id);
  Future<Invoice?> getInvoiceByNumber(String invoiceNumber);
  Future<void> saveInvoice(Invoice invoice);
  Future<void> cancelInvoice(String invoiceId, String reason);
  Future<void> recordPaymentOnInvoice({
    required String invoiceId,
    required double paymentAmount,
  });
  Future<void> recordReturnCreditOnInvoice({
    required String invoiceId,
    required double creditAmount,
  });
  Future<void> deleteDraftInvoice(String invoiceId);
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\invoices\presentation\cubit\invoices_cubit.dart ===

import 'package:decimal/decimal.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/financial_calculator.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/money.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/sequence_manager.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/audit/domain/entities/audit_log.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/audit/domain/repositories/audit_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/repositories/invoice_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/shop_setup/domain/repositories/shop_profile_repository.dart';

abstract class InvoicesState extends Equatable {
  const InvoicesState();
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class InvoicesInitial extends InvoicesState {}

class InvoicesLoading extends InvoicesState {}

class InvoicesLoaded extends InvoicesState {
  final List<Invoice> invoices;

  const InvoicesLoaded(this.invoices);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [invoices];
}

class InvoiceOperationSuccess extends InvoicesState {
  final String message;
  final Invoice? invoice;

  const InvoiceOperationSuccess(this.message, {this.invoice});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message, invoice];
}

class InvoicesError extends InvoicesState {
  final String message;

  const InvoicesError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class InvoicesCubit extends Cubit<InvoicesState> {
  final InvoiceRepository invoiceRepository;
  final ShopProfileRepository shopProfileRepository;
  final SequenceManager sequenceManager;
  final AuditRepository auditRepository;

  InvoicesCubit({
    required this.invoiceRepository,
    required this.shopProfileRepository,
    required this.sequenceManager,
    required this.auditRepository,
  }) : super(InvoicesInitial());

  Future<void> loadInvoices({
    String? customerId,
    DocumentStatus? documentStatus,
    PaymentStatus? paymentStatus,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
    String? query,
  }) async {
    emit(InvoicesLoading());
    try {
      final list = await invoiceRepository.getInvoices(
        customerId: customerId,
        documentStatus: documentStatus,
        paymentStatus: paymentStatus,
        fromDate: fromDate,
        toDate: toDate,
        query: query,
      );
      emit(InvoicesLoaded(list));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(InvoicesError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<Invoice?> createInvoice({
    String? customerId,
    String customerName = '',
    String customerPhone = '',
    String customerAddress = '',
    required List<
      ({
        String? productId,
        String description,
        double quantity,
        double unitPrice,
      })
    >
    rawItems,
    DiscountType discountType = DiscountType.fixed,
    double discountValue = 0.0,
    double taxRate = 0.0,
    String notes = '',
    bool isDraft = false,
  }) async {
    if (rawItems.isEmpty) {
      emit(const InvoicesError('Invoice must contain at least one line item.'));
      return null;
    }

    emit(InvoicesLoading());
    try {
      final shopProfile = await shopProfileRepository.getProfile();
      final currency = shopProfile?.defaultCurrency ?? 'EGP';

      // 1. Authoritative calculation via FinancialCalculator
      final calcItems = rawItems
          .map(
            (item) => (
              quantity: Decimal.parse(item.quantity.toString()),
              unitPrice: Money.fromNum(item.unitPrice, currency),
            ),
          )
          .toList();

      final calcResult = FinancialCalculator.calculateInvoice(
        rawItems: calcItems,
        discountType: discountType,
        discountValue: Decimal.parse(discountValue.toString()),
        taxRatePercent: Decimal.parse(taxRate.toString()),
        currency: currency,
      );

      // 2. Generate unique atomic daily invoice number
      final now = DateTime.now();
      final invoiceNumber = await sequenceManager.getNextInvoiceNumber(now);
      final invoiceId = const Uuid().v4();

      // 3. Build line items with snapshots
      final items = <InvoiceItem>[];
      for (int i = 0; i < rawItems.length; i++) {
        final raw = rawItems[i];
        final calc = calcResult.items[i];
        items.add(
          InvoiceItem(
            id: const Uuid().v4(),
            productId: raw.productId,
            description: raw.description.trim(),
            quantity: calc.quantity.toDouble(),
            unitPrice: calc.unitPrice.toDouble(),
            lineTotal: calc.lineTotal.toDouble(),
          ),
        );
      }

      final invoice = Invoice(
        id: invoiceId,
        invoiceNumber: invoiceNumber,
        issueDate: now,
        shopName: shopProfile?.name ?? '',
        shopPhone: shopProfile?.phone ?? '',
        shopAddress: shopProfile?.address ?? '',
        shopTaxNumber: shopProfile?.taxNumber ?? '',
        customerId: customerId,
        customerName: customerName.trim(),
        customerPhone: customerPhone.trim(),
        customerAddress: customerAddress.trim(),
        items: items,
        subtotal: calcResult.subtotal.toDouble(),
        discountType: discountType == DiscountType.fixed
            ? 'fixed'
            : 'percentage',
        discountInput: discountValue,
        discountAmount: calcResult.discountAmount.toDouble(),
        taxableBase: calcResult.taxableBase.toDouble(),
        taxRate: taxRate,
        taxAmount: calcResult.taxAmount.toDouble(),
        finalTotal: calcResult.finalTotal.toDouble(),
        totalPaid: 0.0,
        remainingBalance: calcResult.finalTotal.toDouble(),
        documentStatus: isDraft ? DocumentStatus.draft : DocumentStatus.issued,
        paymentStatus: PaymentStatus.unpaid,
        currency: currency,
        notes: notes.trim(),
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      );

      await invoiceRepository.saveInvoice(invoice);

      // Audit log
      await auditRepository.logAction(
        AuditLog(
          id: const Uuid().v4(),
          action: isDraft ? 'create_draft' : 'issue_invoice',
          documentType: 'sales_invoice',
          documentId: invoice.id,
          description:
              'Issued invoice ${invoice.invoiceNumber} for amount ${invoice.finalTotal} $currency',
          timestamp: now,
        ),
      );

      emit(
        InvoiceOperationSuccess(
          isDraft ? 'Draft saved' : 'Invoice issued successfully',
          invoice: invoice,
        ),
      );
      await loadInvoices();
      return invoice;
    } catch (e) {
      emit(InvoicesError(e.toString()));
      return null;
    }
  }

  Future<void> cancelInvoice(String invoiceId, String reason) async {
    if (reason.trim().isEmpty) {
      emit(const InvoicesError('Cancellation reason is required.'));
      return;
    }

    emit(InvoicesLoading());
    try {
      await invoiceRepository.cancelInvoice(invoiceId, reason.trim());

      await auditRepository.logAction(
        AuditLog(
          id: const Uuid().v4(),
          action: 'cancel_invoice',
          documentType: 'sales_invoice',
          documentId: invoiceId,
          description: 'Cancelled invoice with reason: $reason',
          timestamp: DateTime.now(),
        ),
      );

      emit(const InvoiceOperationSuccess('Invoice cancelled successfully'));
      await loadInvoices();
    } catch (e) {
      emit(InvoicesError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> deleteDraft(String invoiceId) async {
    emit(InvoicesLoading());
    try {
      await invoiceRepository.deleteDraftInvoice(invoiceId);
      emit(const InvoiceOperationSuccess('Draft deleted'));
      await loadInvoices();
    } catch (e) {
      emit(InvoicesError(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\invoices\presentation\pages\create_invoice_page.dart ===

import 'package:decimal/decimal.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/localization/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/financial_calculator.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/money.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/responsive/responsive_layout.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/entities/party.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/presentation/cubit/parties_cubit.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/products/domain/entities/product.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/products/presentation/cubit/products_cubit.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/shop_setup/presentation/cubit/shop_setup_cubit.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/presentation/cubit/invoices_cubit.dart';

class _EditableItem {
  String? productId;
  final TextEditingController descCtrl;
  final TextEditingController qtyCtrl;
  final TextEditingController priceCtrl;

  _EditableItem({
    this.productId,
    String description = '',
    String quantity = '1',
    String unitPrice = '0.0',
  }) : descCtrl = TextEditingController(text: description),
       qtyCtrl = TextEditingController(text: quantity),
       priceCtrl = TextEditingController(text: unitPrice);

  void dispose() {
    descCtrl.dispose();
    qtyCtrl.dispose();
    priceCtrl.dispose();
  }
}

class CreateInvoicePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const CreateInvoicePage({super.key});

  @override
  State<CreateInvoicePage> createState() => _CreateInvoicePageState();
}

class _CreateInvoicePageState extends State<CreateInvoicePage> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  Party? _selectedCustomer;
  final _items = <_EditableItem>[];

  DiscountType _discountType = DiscountType.fixed;
  final _discountController = TextEditingController(text: '0.0');
  final _taxRateController = TextEditingController(text: '0.0');
  final _notesController = TextEditingController();

  String _currency = 'EGP';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // Preload customers and products
    context.read<PartiesCubit>().loadCustomers();
    context.read<ProductsCubit>().loadProducts();

    // Check shop profile for default tax and currency
    final shopState = context.read<ShopSetupCubit>().state;
    if (shopState is ShopSetupLoaded && shopState.profile != null) {
      final p = shopState.profile!;
      _currency = p.defaultCurrency;
      if (p.defaultTaxEnabled) {
        _taxRateController.text = p.defaultTaxRate.toString();
      }
    }

    // Start with 1 blank item
    _addItem();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    for (final item in _items) {
      item.dispose();
    }
    _discountController.dispose();
    _taxRateController.dispose();
    _notesController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _addItem([Product? product]) {
    setState(() {
      _items.add(
        _EditableItem(
          productId: product?.id,
          description: product?.name ?? '',
          quantity: '1',
          unitPrice: product?.defaultUnitPrice.toString() ?? '0.0',
        ),
      );
    });
  }

  void _removeItem(int index) {
    if (_items.length > 1) {
      setState(() {
        final item = _items.removeAt(index);
        item.dispose();
      });
    }
  }

  InvoiceCalculationResult _calculateCurrentTotals() {
    final raw = <({Decimal quantity, Money unitPrice})>[];
    for (final item in _items) {
      final qty = Decimal.tryParse(item.qtyCtrl.text.trim()) ?? Decimal.zero;
      final price =
          Decimal.tryParse(item.priceCtrl.text.trim()) ?? Decimal.zero;
      raw.add((quantity: qty, unitPrice: Money.fromDecimal(price, _currency)));
    }

    final discountVal =
        Decimal.tryParse(_discountController.text.trim()) ?? Decimal.zero;
    final taxRateVal =
        Decimal.tryParse(_taxRateController.text.trim()) ?? Decimal.zero;

    return FinancialCalculator.calculateInvoice(
      rawItems: raw,
      discountType: _discountType,
      discountValue: discountVal,
      taxRatePercent: taxRateVal,
      currency: _currency,
    );
  }

  void _submit({required bool isDraft}) {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    final rawItems =
        <
          ({
            String? productId,
            String description,
            double quantity,
            double unitPrice,
          })
        >[];
    for (final item in _items) {
      final desc = item.descCtrl.text.trim();
      final qty = double.tryParse(item.qtyCtrl.text.trim()) ?? 1.0;
      final price = double.tryParse(item.priceCtrl.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;
      if (desc.isNotEmpty && qty > 0) {
        rawItems.add((
          productId: item.productId,
          description: desc,
          quantity: qty,
          unitPrice: price,
        ));
      }
    }

    if (rawItems.isEmpty) {
      final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(l10n.setupRequiredField),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    final discountVal = double.tryParse(_discountController.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;
    final taxRateVal = double.tryParse(_taxRateController.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;

    context.read<InvoicesCubit>().createInvoice(
      customerId: _selectedCustomer?.id,
      customerName: _selectedCustomer?.name ?? '',
      customerPhone: _selectedCustomer?.phone ?? '',
      customerAddress: _selectedCustomer?.address ?? '',
      rawItems: rawItems,
      discountType: _discountType,
      discountValue: discountVal,
      taxRate: taxRateVal,
      notes: _notesController.text.trim(),
      isDraft: isDraft,
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final totals = _calculateCurrentTotals();

    return BlocListener<InvoicesCubit, InvoicesState>(
      listener: (context, state) {
        if (state is InvoiceOperationSuccess) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
              .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(state.message)));
          Navigator.pop(context);
        } else if (state is InvoicesError) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            SnackBar(
              content: Text(state.message),
              backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
            ),
          );
        }
      },
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.actionNewInvoice)),
        body: ResponsiveContentContainer(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
          child: Form(
            key: _formKey,
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  // Customer Selection Card
                  Card(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            l10n.customerLabel,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              fontSize: 15,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 12),
                          BlocBuilder<PartiesCubit, PartiesState>(
                            builder: (context, state) {
                              List<Party> customers = [];
                              if (state is CustomersLoaded) {
                                customers = state.customers;
                              }
                              return DropdownButtonFormField<Party?>(
                                initialValue: _selectedCustomer,
                                decoration: InputDecoration(
                                  labelText: l10n.customerLabel,
                                  prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.person_outline),
                                ),
                                hint: Text(l10n.cashCustomer),
                                items: [
                                  DropdownMenuItem<Party?>(
                                    value: null,
                                    child: Text(l10n.cashCustomer),
                                  ),
                                  ...customers.map(
                                    (c) => DropdownMenuItem(
                                      value: c,
                                      child: Text('${c.name} (${c.phone})'),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                                onChanged: (val) =>
                                    setState(() => _selectedCustomer = val),
                              );
                            },
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),

                  // Line Items Section
                  Card(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Row(
                            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                l10n.itemsSection,
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  fontSize: 15,
                                ),
                              ),
                              TextButton.icon(
                                icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                                label: Text(l10n.addItemBtn),
                                onPressed: () => _addItem(),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                          const Divider(),
                          const SizedBox(height: 8),

                          // Dynamic Items list
                          ..._items.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                            final idx = entry.key;
                            final item = entry.value;

                            return Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12.0),
                              child: Row(
                                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                children: [
                                  Expanded(
                                    flex: 4,
                                    child: TextFormField(
                                      controller: item.descCtrl,
                                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                                        labelText: l10n.itemDescription,
                                        isDense: true,
                                      ),
                                      onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                                      validator: (v) =>
                                          v == null || v.trim().isEmpty
                                          ? l10n.setupRequiredField
                                          : null,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                  Expanded(
                                    flex: 2,
                                    child: TextFormField(
                                      controller: item.qtyCtrl,
                                      keyboardType:
                                          const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                                            decimal: true,
                                          ),
                                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                                        labelText: l10n.itemQuantity,
                                        isDense: true,
                                      ),
                                      onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                  Expanded(
                                    flex: 2,
                                    child: TextFormField(
                                      controller: item.priceCtrl,
                                      keyboardType:
                                          const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                                            decimal: true,
                                          ),

                                    }
