                                    isDense: true,
                                  ),
                                  onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: 12),
                              Expanded(
                                flex: 2,
                                child: TextFormField(
                                  controller: _taxRateController,
                                  keyboardType:
                                      const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                                        decimal: true,
                                      ),
                                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                                    labelText: l10n.taxRateLabel,
                                    isDense: true,
                                  ),
                                  onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 12),
                          TextFormField(
                            controller: _notesController,
                            maxLines: 2,
                            decoration: InputDecoration(
                              labelText: l10n.notesLabel,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),

                  // Live Summary Box
                  Card(
                    color: const Color(0xFFF8FAFC),
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                      side: const BorderSide(
                        color: AppColors.primary,
                        width: 1.2,
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                      child: Column(
                        children: [
                          _buildSummaryLine(
                            l10n.subtotalLabel,
                            '${totals.subtotal.toStringAsFixed(2)} $_currency',
                          ),
                          if (totals.discountAmount.isPositive)
                            _buildSummaryLine(
                              l10n.discountLabel,
                              '-${totals.discountAmount.toStringAsFixed(2)} $_currency',
                              color: AppColors.error,
                            ),
                          if (totals.taxAmount.isPositive) ...[
                            _buildSummaryLine(
                              l10n.taxableBaseLabel,
                              '${totals.taxableBase.toStringAsFixed(2)} $_currency',
                            ),
                            _buildSummaryLine(
                              l10n.taxAmountLabel,
                              '+${totals.taxAmount.toStringAsFixed(2)} $_currency',
                            ),
                          ],
                          const Divider(thickness: 1.5),
                          _buildSummaryLine(
                            l10n.finalTotalLabel,
                            '${totals.finalTotal.toStringAsFixed(2)} $_currency',
                            isBold: true,
                            fontSize: 18,
                            color: AppColors.primary,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),

                  // Actions Buttons
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: OutlinedButton(
                          onPressed: () => _submit(isDraft: true),
                          child: Text(l10n.saveDraftBtn),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 16),
                      Expanded(
                        child: ElevatedButton(
                          onPressed: () => _submit(isDraft: false),
                          child: Text(l10n.issueInvoiceBtn),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSummaryLine(
    String label,
    String value, {
    bool isBold = false,
    double fontSize = 14,
    Color? color,
  }) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4.0),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: fontSize,
              fontWeight: isBold ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            value,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: fontSize,
              fontWeight: isBold ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
              color: color,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\invoices\presentation\pages\invoices_list_page.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/localization/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency_formatter.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/responsive/responsive_layout.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/presentation/cubit/invoices_cubit.dart';

import 'create_invoice_page.dart';
import 'invoice_detail_page.dart';

class InvoicesListPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const InvoicesListPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<InvoicesListPage> createState() => _InvoicesListPageState();
}

class _InvoicesListPageState extends State<InvoicesListPage> {
  final _searchController = TextEditingController();
  DocumentStatus? _selectedStatus;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    context.read<InvoicesCubit>().loadInvoices();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onFilterChanged() {
    context.read<InvoicesCubit>().loadInvoices(
      query: _searchController.text.trim(),
      documentStatus: _selectedStatus,
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(l10n.navInvoices),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
            tooltip: l10n.actionNewInvoice,
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.push(
                context,
                MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const CreateInvoicePage()),
              ).then((_) => _onFilterChanged());
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: ResponsiveContentContainer(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // Search and Status Filter Row
            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: TextField(
                    controller: _searchController,
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      hintText: l10n.searchHint,
                      prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search),
                      suffixIcon: _searchController.text.isNotEmpty
                          ? IconButton(
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.clear),
                              onPressed: () {
                                _searchController.clear();
                                _onFilterChanged();
                              },
                            )
                          : null,
                    ),
                    onChanged: (_) => _onFilterChanged(),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                DropdownButton<DocumentStatus?>(
                  value: _selectedStatus,
                  hint: Text(l10n.allLabel),
                  items: [
                    DropdownMenuItem(value: null, child: Text(l10n.allLabel)),
                    DropdownMenuItem(
                      value: DocumentStatus.issued,
                      child: Text(l10n.statusIssued),
                    ),
                    DropdownMenuItem(
                      value: DocumentStatus.draft,
                      child: Text(l10n.statusDraft),
                    ),
                    DropdownMenuItem(
                      value: DocumentStatus.cancelled,
                      child: Text(l10n.statusCancelled),
                    ),
                  ],
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    setState(() => _selectedStatus = val);
                    _onFilterChanged();
                  },
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            // Invoices List
            Expanded(
              child: BlocBuilder<InvoicesCubit, InvoicesState>(
                builder: (context, state) {
                  if (state is InvoicesLoading) {
                    return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
                  }
                  if (state is InvoicesError) {
                    return Center(child: Text(state.message));
                  }
                  if (state is InvoicesLoaded) {
                    final invoices = state.invoices;
                    if (invoices.isEmpty) {
                      return Center(
                        child: Column(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                          children: [
                            const Icon(
                              Icons.receipt_outlined,
                              size: 56,
                              color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 12),
                            Text(
                              l10n.noDataFound,
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 16),
                            ElevatedButton.icon(
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                              label: Text(l10n.actionNewInvoice),
                              onPressed: () {
                                Navigator.push(
                                  context,
                                  MaterialPageRoute(
                                    builder: (_) => const CreateInvoicePage(),
                                  ),
                                ).then((_) => _onFilterChanged());
                              },
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      );
                    }

                    return ListView.separated(
                      itemCount: invoices.length,
                      separatorBuilder: (context, index) =>
                          const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                        final invoice = invoices[index];
                        final dateStr = DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm')
                            .format(invoice.issueDate);

                        return Card(
                          child: InkWell(
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                            onTap: () {
                              Navigator.push(
                                context,
                                MaterialPageRoute(
                                  builder: (_) =>
                                      InvoiceDetailPage(invoiceId: invoice.id),
                                ),
                              ).then((_) => _onFilterChanged());
                            },
                            child: Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                              child: Row(
                                children: [
                                  Container(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: invoice.isCancelled
                                          ? AppColors.errorBackground
                                          : invoice.remainingBalance == 0
                                          ? AppColors.successBackground
                                          : const Color(0xFFEFF6FF),
                                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                                    ),
                                    child: Icon(
                                      invoice.isCancelled
                                          ? Icons.cancel_outlined
                                          : Icons.receipt,
                                      color: invoice.isCancelled
                                          ? AppColors.error
                                          : invoice.remainingBalance == 0
                                          ? AppColors.success
                                          : AppColors.primary,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 16),
                                  Expanded(
                                    child: Column(
                                      crossAxisAlignment:
                                          CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                      children: [
                                        Wrap(
                                          spacing: 8,
                                          runSpacing: 4,
                                          crossAxisAlignment:
                                              WrapCrossAlignment.center,
                                          children: [
                                            Text(
                                              invoice.invoiceNumber,
                                              style: const TextStyle(
                                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                                fontSize: 15,
                                              ),
                                            ),
                                            _buildStatusBadge(context, invoice),
                                          ],
                                        ),
                                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                        Text(
                                          invoice.customerName.isNotEmpty
                                              ? invoice.customerName
                                              : l10n.cashCustomer,
                                          style: const TextStyle(
                                            color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                                            fontSize: 13,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                        Text(
                                          dateStr,
                                          style: const TextStyle(
                                            color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                                            fontSize: 11,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  Column(
                                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                                    children: [
                                      Text(
                                        CurrencyFormatter.format(
                                          invoice.finalTotal,
                                          currency: invoice.currency,
                                          isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                                        ),
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                          fontSize: 15,
                                          color: invoice.isCancelled
                                              ? AppColors.error
                                              : AppColors.textPrimaryLight,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      if (!invoice.isCancelled &&
                                          invoice.remainingBalance > 0)
                                        Text(
                                          '${l10n.remainingBalanceLabel}: ${CurrencyFormatter.format(invoice.remainingBalance, currency: invoice.currency, isArabic: l10n.isArabic)}',
                                          style: const TextStyle(
                                            fontSize: 11,
                                            color: AppColors.warning,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                    );
                  }
                  return const SizedBox.shrink();
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatusBadge(BuildContext context, Invoice invoice) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final String label;
    final Color color;
    final Color bg;

    if (invoice.isCancelled) {
      label = l10n.statusCancelled;
      color = AppColors.error;
      bg = AppColors.errorBackground;
    } else if (invoice.isDraft) {
      label = l10n.statusDraft;
      color = AppColors.textSecondaryLight;
      bg = const Color(0xFFF1F5F9);
    } else if (invoice.remainingBalance == 0) {
      label = l10n.paymentStatusPaid;
      color = AppColors.success;
      bg = AppColors.successBackground;
    } else if (invoice.totalPaid > 0) {
      label = l10n.paymentStatusPartial;
      color = AppColors.warning;
      bg = AppColors.warningBackground;
    } else {
      label = l10n.paymentStatusUnpaid;
      color = AppColors.primary;
      bg = const Color(0xFFEFF6FF);
    }

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: bg,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
      ),
      child: Text(
        label,
        style: TextStyle(
          color: color,
          fontSize: 10,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\invoices\presentation\pages\invoice_detail_page.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'package:printing/printing.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/localization/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/pdf/invoice_pdf_generator.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency_formatter.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/responsive/responsive_layout.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/repositories/invoice_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/payments/domain/entities/payment.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/payments/presentation/cubit/payments_cubit.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/presentation/pages/create_return_page.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/presentation/cubit/invoices_cubit.dart';

class InvoiceDetailPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final String invoiceId;

  const InvoiceDetailPage({super.key, required this.invoiceId});

  @override
  State<InvoiceDetailPage> createState() => _InvoiceDetailPageState();
}

class _InvoiceDetailPageState extends State<InvoiceDetailPage> {
  Invoice? _invoice;
  bool _isLoading = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadInvoice();
  }

  Future<void> _loadInvoice() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    final repo = context.read<InvoiceRepository>();
    final inv = await repo.getInvoiceById(widget.invoiceId);
    if (mounted) {
      setState(() {
        _invoice = inv;
        _isLoading = false;
      });
      if (inv != null) {
        context.read<PaymentsCubit>().loadPayments(partyId: inv.customerId);
      }
    }
  }

  void _showRecordPaymentDialog() {
    if (_invoice == null) return;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final amountCtrl = TextEditingController(
      text: _invoice!.remainingBalance.toStringAsFixed(2),
    );
    final refCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final notesCtrl = TextEditingController();
    PaymentMethod method = PaymentMethod.cash;

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (context, setDialogState) => AlertDialog(
          title: Text(l10n.actionRecordPayment),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Text(
                  '${l10n.remainingBalanceLabel}: ${CurrencyFormatter.format(_invoice!.remainingBalance, currency: _invoice!.currency, isArabic: l10n.isArabic)}',
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: AppColors.primary,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                TextField(
                  controller: amountCtrl,
                  keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                    decimal: true,
                  ),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: l10n.paymentAmountLabel,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                DropdownButtonFormField<PaymentMethod>(
                  initialValue: method,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: l10n.paymentMethodLabel,
                  ),
                  items: [
                    DropdownMenuItem(
                      value: PaymentMethod.cash,
                      child: Text(l10n.methodCash),
                    ),
                    DropdownMenuItem(
                      value: PaymentMethod.bank,
                      child: Text(l10n.methodBank),
                    ),
                    DropdownMenuItem(
                      value: PaymentMethod.card,
                      child: Text(l10n.methodCard),
                    ),
                    DropdownMenuItem(
                      value: PaymentMethod.other,
                      child: Text(l10n.methodOther),
                    ),
                  ],
                  onChanged: (val) {
                    if (val != null) setDialogState(() => method = val);
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                TextField(
                  controller: refCtrl,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: l10n.paymentReferenceLabel,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                TextField(
                  controller: notesCtrl,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.notesLabel),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
              child: Text(l10n.cancelBtn),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () async {
                final amount = double.tryParse(amountCtrl.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;
                if (amount <= 0.0) return;

                final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
                final paymentsCubit = context.read<PaymentsCubit>();
                final inv = _invoice!;
                final ref = refCtrl.text.trim();
                final notes = notesCtrl.text.trim();

                Navigator.pop(ctx);
                final success = await paymentsCubit.recordPayment(
                  amount: amount,
                  method: method,
                  partyId: inv.customerId,
                  partyName: inv.customerName,
                  isCustomerPayment: true,
                  invoiceId: inv.id,
                  invoiceNumber: inv.invoiceNumber,
                  referenceNumber: ref,
                  notes: notes,
                  currency: inv.currency,
                );

                if (success && mounted) {
                  _loadInvoice();
                  messenger.showSnackBar(
                    SnackBar(content: Text(l10n.successMsg)),
                  );
                }
              },
              child: Text(l10n.saveBtn),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showCancelDialog() {
    if (_invoice == null) return;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final reasonCtrl = TextEditingController();

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(l10n.cancelInvoiceTitle),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              l10n.cancelInvoiceWarning,
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.error),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            TextField(
              controller: reasonCtrl,
              maxLines: 2,
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                labelText: l10n.cancelReasonLabel,
                border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
            child: Text(l10n.closeBtn),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.error),
            onPressed: () async {
              if (reasonCtrl.text.trim().isNotEmpty) {
                Navigator.pop(ctx);
                await context.read<InvoicesCubit>().cancelInvoice(
                  _invoice!.id,
                  reasonCtrl.text.trim(),
                );
                _loadInvoice();
              }
            },
            child: Text(l10n.confirmCancelBtn),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _previewOrPrintPdf({bool isArabic = true}) async {
    if (_invoice == null) return;
    final pdfBytes = await InvoicePdfGenerator.generateInvoicePdf(
      invoice: _invoice!,
      isArabic: isArabic,
    );

    await Printing.layoutPdf(
      onLayout: (format) async => pdfBytes,
      name: '${_invoice!.invoiceNumber}.pdf',
    );
  }

  Future<void> _shareInvoicePdf({bool isArabic = true}) async {
    if (_invoice == null) return;
    final pdfBytes = await InvoicePdfGenerator.generateInvoicePdf(
      invoice: _invoice!,
      isArabic: isArabic,
    );

    await Printing.sharePdf(
      bytes: pdfBytes,
      filename: '${_invoice!.invoiceNumber}.pdf',
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);

    if (_isLoading) {
      return const Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()));
    }
    if (_invoice == null) {
      return Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(),
        body: Center(child: Text(l10n.noDataFound)),
      );
    }

    final invoice = _invoice!;
    final dateStr = DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm').format(invoice.issueDate);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(invoice.invoiceNumber),
        actions: [
          // Print / Preview PDF
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.print_outlined),
            tooltip: l10n.previewPdfBtn,
            onPressed: () => _previewOrPrintPdf(isArabic: l10n.isArabic),
          ),
          // Share PDF
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.share_outlined),
            tooltip: l10n.shareDocumentBtn,
            onPressed: () => _shareInvoicePdf(isArabic: l10n.isArabic),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: ResponsiveContentContainer(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              // Header Card
              Card(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                invoice.shopName.isNotEmpty
                                    ? invoice.shopName
                                    : l10n.appName,
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  fontSize: 18,
                                  color: AppColors.primary,
                                ),
                              ),
                              if (invoice.shopPhone.isNotEmpty)
                                Text(
                                  invoice.shopPhone,
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                                    fontSize: 12,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              if (invoice.shopAddress.isNotEmpty)
                                Text(
                                  invoice.shopAddress,
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                                    fontSize: 12,
                                  ),
                                ),
                            ],
                          ),
                          _buildStatusBadge(context, invoice),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const Divider(height: 24),
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            '${l10n.invoiceDate}: $dateStr',
                            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            '${l10n.customerLabel}: ${invoice.customerName.isNotEmpty ? invoice.customerName : l10n.cashCustomer}',
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 13,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      if (invoice.isCancelled &&
                          invoice.cancellationReason != null) ...[
                        const SizedBox(height: 12),
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: AppColors.errorBackground,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                          ),
                          child: Text(
                            '${l10n.cancelReasonLabel}: ${invoice.cancellationReason}',
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              color: AppColors.error,
                              fontSize: 12,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // Items Breakdown Card
              Card(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        l10n.itemsSection,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          fontSize: 15,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const Divider(),
                      ...invoice.items.map(
                        (item) => Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6.0),
                          child: Row(
                            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                            children: [
                              Expanded(
                                flex: 4,
                                child: Text(
                                  item.description,
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              Expanded(
                                flex: 2,
                                child: Text(
                                  '${item.quantity} × ${item.unitPrice.toStringAsFixed(2)}',
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                                    fontSize: 12,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              Text(
                                CurrencyFormatter.format(
                                  item.lineTotal,
                                  currency: invoice.currency,
                                  isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                                ),
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),

              // Financial Totals Card
              Card(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      _buildRow(
                        l10n.subtotalLabel,
                        CurrencyFormatter.format(
                          invoice.subtotal,
                          currency: invoice.currency,
                          isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (invoice.discountAmount > 0)
                        _buildRow(
                          l10n.discountLabel,
                          '-${CurrencyFormatter.format(invoice.discountAmount, currency: invoice.currency, isArabic: l10n.isArabic)}',
                          color: AppColors.error,
                        ),
                      if (invoice.taxAmount > 0) ...[
                        _buildRow(
                          l10n.taxableBaseLabel,
                          CurrencyFormatter.format(
                            invoice.taxableBase,
                            currency: invoice.currency,
                            isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                          ),
                        ),
                        _buildRow(
                          '${l10n.taxAmountLabel} (${invoice.taxRate}%)',
                          '+${CurrencyFormatter.format(invoice.taxAmount, currency: invoice.currency, isArabic: l10n.isArabic)}',
                        ),
                      ],
                      const Divider(thickness: 1.5),
                      _buildRow(
                        l10n.finalTotalLabel,
                        CurrencyFormatter.format(
                          invoice.finalTotal,
                          currency: invoice.currency,
                          isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                        ),
                        isBold: true,
                        fontSize: 16,
                        color: AppColors.primary,
                      ),
                      _buildRow(
                        l10n.amountPaidLabel,
                        CurrencyFormatter.format(
                          invoice.totalPaid,
                          currency: invoice.currency,
                          isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                        ),
                        color: AppColors.success,
                      ),
                      _buildRow(
                        l10n.remainingBalanceLabel,
                        CurrencyFormatter.format(
                          invoice.remainingBalance,
                          currency: invoice.currency,
                          isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                        ),
                        isBold: true,
                        color: invoice.remainingBalance > 0
                            ? AppColors.warning
                            : AppColors.success,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 24),

              // Action Buttons
              if (!invoice.isCancelled) ...[
                Row(
                  children: [
                    if (invoice.remainingBalance > 0) ...[
                      Expanded(
                        child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.payment),
                          label: Text(l10n.actionRecordPayment),
                          onPressed: _showRecordPaymentDialog,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    ],
                    Expanded(
                      child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.assignment_return_outlined),
                        label: Text(l10n.actionNewReturn),
                        onPressed: () {
                          Navigator.push(
                            context,
                            MaterialPageRoute(
                              builder: (_) =>
                                  CreateReturnPage(originalInvoice: invoice),
                            ),
                          ).then((_) => _loadInvoice());
                        },
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                OutlinedButton.icon(
                  icon: const Icon(
                    Icons.cancel_outlined,
                    color: AppColors.error,
                  ),
                  label: Text(
                    l10n.cancelInvoiceTitle,
                    style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.error),
                  ),
                  onPressed: _showCancelDialog,
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRow(
    String label,
    String value, {
    bool isBold = false,
    double fontSize = 13,
    Color? color,
  }) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3.0),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: fontSize,
              fontWeight: isBold ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            value,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: fontSize,
              fontWeight: isBold ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
              color: color,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStatusBadge(BuildContext context, Invoice invoice) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final String label;
    final Color color;
    final Color bg;

    if (invoice.isCancelled) {
      label = l10n.statusCancelled;
      color = AppColors.error;
      bg = AppColors.errorBackground;
    } else if (invoice.remainingBalance == 0) {
      label = l10n.paymentStatusPaid;
      color = AppColors.success;
      bg = AppColors.successBackground;
    } else if (invoice.totalPaid > 0) {
      label = l10n.paymentStatusPartial;
      color = AppColors.warning;
      bg = AppColors.warningBackground;
    } else {
      label = l10n.paymentStatusUnpaid;
      color = AppColors.primary;
      bg = const Color(0xFFEFF6FF);
    }

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: bg,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
      ),
      child: Text(
        label,
        style: TextStyle(
          color: color,
          fontSize: 11,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\parties\data\repositories\party_repository_impl.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/hive_database.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/entities/party.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/repositories/party_repository.dart';

class PartyRepositoryImpl implements PartyRepository {
  final HiveDatabase database;

  PartyRepositoryImpl(this.database);

  @override
  Future<List<Party>> getCustomers({
    String? query,
    bool includeArchived = false,
  }) async {
    final box = database.partiesBox;
    final results = <Party>[];

    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final party = Party.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
        if (party.isCustomer && (includeArchived || !party.isArchived)) {
          if (query == null || query.trim().isEmpty) {
            results.add(party);
          } else {
            final q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
            if (party.name.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
                party.phone.contains(q) ||
                party.address.toLowerCase().contains(q)) {
              results.add(party);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    results.sort((a, b) => a.name.compareTo(b.name));
    return results;
  }

  @override
  Future<List<Party>> getSuppliers({
    String? query,
    bool includeArchived = false,
  }) async {
    final box = database.partiesBox;
    final results = <Party>[];

    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final party = Party.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
        if (party.isSupplier && (includeArchived || !party.isArchived)) {
          if (query == null || query.trim().isEmpty) {
            results.add(party);
          } else {
            final q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
            if (party.name.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
                party.phone.contains(q) ||
                party.address.toLowerCase().contains(q)) {
              results.add(party);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    results.sort((a, b) => a.name.compareTo(b.name));
    return results;
  }

  @override
  Future<Party?> getPartyById(String id) async {
    final box = database.partiesBox;
    final map = box.get(id);
    if (map == null) return null;
    return Party.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
  }

  @override
  Future<void> saveParty(Party party) async {
    final box = database.partiesBox;
    await box.put(party.id, party.toMap());
  }

  @override
  Future<void> archiveParty(String id) async {
    final party = await getPartyById(id);
    if (party != null) {
      await saveParty(
        party.copyWith(isArchived: true, updatedAt: DateTime.now()),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<double> getCustomerReceivableBalance(String customerId) async {
    final party = await getPartyById(customerId);
    var balance = party?.openingReceivable ?? 0.0;

    // Add remaining balance of all issued invoices for this customer
    final invoiceBox = database.invoicesBox;
    for (final key in invoiceBox.keys) {
      final map = invoiceBox.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final invoice = Invoice.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
        if (invoice.customerId == customerId && invoice.isIssued) {
          balance += invoice.remainingBalance;
        }
      }
    }

    return balance > 0.0 ? balance : 0.0;
  }

  @override
  Future<double> getSupplierPayableBalance(String supplierId) async {
    final party = await getPartyById(supplierId);
    var balance = party?.openingPayable ?? 0.0;

    // Add remaining payable of all issued purchases for this supplier
    final purchaseBox = database.purchasesBox;
    for (final key in purchaseBox.keys) {
      final map = purchaseBox.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final doc = map;
        if (doc['supplierId'] == supplierId &&
            doc['documentStatus'] == 'issued') {
          balance += (doc['remainingPayable'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
        }
      }
    }

    return balance > 0.0 ? balance : 0.0;
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\parties\domain\entities\party.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

enum PartyType { customer, supplier, both }

class Party extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String phone;
  final String address;
  final String email;
  final String notes;
  final bool isCustomer;
  final bool isSupplier;
  final double openingReceivable; // What the customer owes us initially
  final double openingPayable; // What we owe the supplier initially
  final bool isArchived;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  const Party({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    this.phone = '',
    this.address = '',
    this.email = '',
    this.notes = '',
    this.isCustomer = true,
    this.isSupplier = false,
    this.openingReceivable = 0.0,
    this.openingPayable = 0.0,
    this.isArchived = false,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  Party copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? name,
    String? phone,
    String? address,
    String? email,
    String? notes,
    bool? isCustomer,
    bool? isSupplier,
    double? openingReceivable,
    double? openingPayable,
    bool? isArchived,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return Party(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      phone: phone ?? this.phone,
      address: address ?? this.address,
      email: email ?? this.email,
      notes: notes ?? this.notes,
      isCustomer: isCustomer ?? this.isCustomer,
      isSupplier: isSupplier ?? this.isSupplier,
      openingReceivable: openingReceivable ?? this.openingReceivable,
      openingPayable: openingPayable ?? this.openingPayable,
      isArchived: isArchived ?? this.isArchived,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'phone': phone,
      'address': address,
      'email': email,
      'notes': notes,
      'isCustomer': isCustomer,
      'isSupplier': isSupplier,
      'openingReceivable': openingReceivable,
      'openingPayable': openingPayable,
      'isArchived': isArchived,
      'createdAt': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updatedAt': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory Party.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return Party(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? '',
      name: map['name'] as String? ?? '',
      phone: map['phone'] as String? ?? '',
      address: map['address'] as String? ?? '',
      email: map['email'] as String? ?? '',
      notes: map['notes'] as String? ?? '',
      isCustomer: map['isCustomer'] as bool? ?? true,
      isSupplier: map['isSupplier'] as bool? ?? false,
      openingReceivable: (map['openingReceivable'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      openingPayable: (map['openingPayable'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      isArchived: map['isArchived'] as bool? ?? false,
      createdAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['createdAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['updatedAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    id,
    name,
    phone,
    address,
    email,
    notes,
    isCustomer,
    isSupplier,
    openingReceivable,
    openingPayable,
    isArchived,
    createdAt,
    updatedAt,
  ];
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\parties\domain\repositories\party_repository.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/entities/party.dart';

abstract class PartyRepository {
  Future<List<Party>> getCustomers({
    String? query,
    bool includeArchived = false,
  });
  Future<List<Party>> getSuppliers({
    String? query,
    bool includeArchived = false,
  });
  Future<Party?> getPartyById(String id);
  Future<void> saveParty(Party party);
  Future<void> archiveParty(String id);
  Future<double> getCustomerReceivableBalance(String customerId);
  Future<double> getSupplierPayableBalance(String supplierId);
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\parties\presentation\cubit\parties_cubit.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/entities/party.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/repositories/party_repository.dart';

abstract class PartiesState extends Equatable {
  const PartiesState();
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class PartiesInitial extends PartiesState {}

class PartiesLoading extends PartiesState {}

class CustomersLoaded extends PartiesState {
  final List<Party> customers;
  final Map<String, double> balances;

  const CustomersLoaded({required this.customers, this.balances = const {}});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [customers, balances];
}

class SuppliersLoaded extends PartiesState {
  final List<Party> suppliers;
  final Map<String, double> balances;

  const SuppliersLoaded({required this.suppliers, this.balances = const {}});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [suppliers, balances];
}

class PartyOperationSuccess extends PartiesState {
  final String message;

  const PartyOperationSuccess(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class PartiesError extends PartiesState {
  final String message;

  const PartiesError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class PartiesCubit extends Cubit<PartiesState> {
  final PartyRepository repository;

  PartiesCubit(this.repository) : super(PartiesInitial());

  Future<void> loadCustomers({String? query}) async {
    emit(PartiesLoading());
    try {
      final customers = await repository.getCustomers(query: query);
      final balances = <String, double>{};
      for (final c in customers) {
        balances[c.id] = await repository.getCustomerReceivableBalance(c.id);
      }
      emit(CustomersLoaded(customers: customers, balances: balances));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PartiesError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> loadSuppliers({String? query}) async {
    emit(PartiesLoading());
    try {
      final suppliers = await repository.getSuppliers(query: query);
      final balances = <String, double>{};
      for (final s in suppliers) {
        balances[s.id] = await repository.getSupplierPayableBalance(s.id);
      }
      emit(SuppliersLoaded(suppliers: suppliers, balances: balances));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PartiesError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> saveParty({
    String? id,
    required String name,
    String phone = '',
    String address = '',
    String email = '',
    String notes = '',
    required bool isCustomer,
    required bool isSupplier,
    double openingReceivable = 0.0,
    double openingPayable = 0.0,
  }) async {
    if (name.trim().isEmpty) {
      emit(const PartiesError('Party name cannot be empty.'));
      return;
    }

    emit(PartiesLoading());
    try {
      final partyId = id ?? const Uuid().v4();
      final now = DateTime.now();

      final party = Party(
        id: partyId,
        name: name.trim(),
        phone: phone.trim(),
        address: address.trim(),
        email: email.trim(),
        notes: notes.trim(),
        isCustomer: isCustomer,
        isSupplier: isSupplier,
        openingReceivable: openingReceivable,
        openingPayable: openingPayable,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      );

      await repository.saveParty(party);
      emit(const PartyOperationSuccess('Party saved successfully'));

      if (isCustomer) {
        await loadCustomers();
      } else {
        await loadSuppliers();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PartiesError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> archiveParty(String id, bool isCustomer) async {
    emit(PartiesLoading());
    try {
      await repository.archiveParty(id);
      emit(const PartyOperationSuccess('Party archived successfully'));
      if (isCustomer) {
        await loadCustomers();
      } else {
        await loadSuppliers();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PartiesError(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\parties\presentation\pages\parties_page.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/localization/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency_formatter.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/responsive/responsive_layout.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/settings/app_settings_cubit.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/entities/party.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/presentation/cubit/parties_cubit.dart';

import 'party_detail_page.dart';

class PartiesPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const PartiesPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<PartiesPage> createState() => _PartiesPageState();
}

class _PartiesPageState extends State<PartiesPage>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late TabController _tabController;
  final _searchController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tabController = TabController(length: 2, vsync: this);
    _loadData();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabController.dispose();
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _loadData() {
    final query = _searchController.text.trim();
    context.read<PartiesCubit>().loadCustomers(query: query);
    context.read<PartiesCubit>().loadSuppliers(query: query);
  }

  void _showAddEditPartyDialog(
    BuildContext context, {
    Party? existingParty,
    bool isCustomerTab = true,
  }) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final nameCtrl = TextEditingController(text: existingParty?.name ?? '');
    final phoneCtrl = TextEditingController(text: existingParty?.phone ?? '');
    final addressCtrl = TextEditingController(
      text: existingParty?.address ?? '',
    );
    final emailCtrl = TextEditingController(text: existingParty?.email ?? '');
    final notesCtrl = TextEditingController(text: existingParty?.notes ?? '');
    final openingCtrl = TextEditingController(
      text: existingParty != null
          ? (isCustomerTab
                    ? existingParty.openingReceivable
                    : existingParty.openingPayable)
                .toString()
          : '0.0',
    );

    bool isCustomer = existingParty?.isCustomer ?? isCustomerTab;
    bool isSupplier = existingParty?.isSupplier ?? !isCustomerTab;

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => StatefulBuilder(
        builder: (context, setDialogState) => AlertDialog(
          title: Text(
            existingParty != null
                ? l10n.editBtn
                : (isCustomerTab
                      ? l10n.actionAddCustomer
                      : l10n.actionAddSupplier),
          ),
          content: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                TextField(
                  controller: nameCtrl,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.nameLabel),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                TextField(
                  controller: phoneCtrl,
                  keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.phoneLabel),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                TextField(
                  controller: addressCtrl,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.addressLabel),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                TextField(
                  controller: emailCtrl,
                  keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.emailLabel),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                TextField(
                  controller: openingCtrl,
                  keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                    decimal: true,
                  ),
                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                    labelText: l10n.openingBalanceLabel,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                TextField(
                  controller: notesCtrl,
                  decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.notesLabel),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                CheckboxListTile(
                  title: Text(l10n.customerLabel),
                  value: isCustomer,
                  onChanged: (val) =>
                      setDialogState(() => isCustomer = val ?? false),
                ),
                CheckboxListTile(
                  title: Text(l10n.supplierLabel),
                  value: isSupplier,
                  onChanged: (val) =>
                      setDialogState(() => isSupplier = val ?? false),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          actions: [
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
              child: Text(l10n.cancelBtn),
            ),
            ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () {
                final name = nameCtrl.text.trim();
                if (name.isEmpty) return;

                final opening = double.tryParse(openingCtrl.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;

                context.read<PartiesCubit>().saveParty(
                  id: existingParty?.id,
                  name: name,
                  phone: phoneCtrl.text.trim(),
                  address: addressCtrl.text.trim(),
                  email: emailCtrl.text.trim(),
                  notes: notesCtrl.text.trim(),
                  isCustomer: isCustomer,
                  isSupplier: isSupplier,
                  openingReceivable: isCustomer ? opening : 0.0,
                  openingPayable: isSupplier ? opening : 0.0,
                );
                Navigator.pop(ctx);
                _loadData();
              },
              child: Text(l10n.saveBtn),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text('${l10n.navCustomers} / ${l10n.navSuppliers}'),
        bottom: TabBar(
          controller: _tabController,
          labelColor: AppColors.primary,
          indicatorColor: AppColors.primary,
          tabs: [
            Tab(
              text: l10n.navCustomers,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.person_outline),
            ),
            Tab(
              text: l10n.navSuppliers,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.business_outlined),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
            onPressed: () => _showAddEditPartyDialog(
              context,
              isCustomerTab: _tabController.index == 0,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: ResponsiveContentContainer(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            TextField(
              controller: _searchController,
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                hintText: l10n.searchHint,
                prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search),
                suffixIcon: _searchController.text.isNotEmpty
                    ? IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.clear),
                        onPressed: () {
                          _searchController.clear();
                          _loadData();
                        },
                      )
                    : null,
              ),
              onChanged: (_) => _loadData(),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Expanded(
              child: TabBarView(
                controller: _tabController,
                children: [
                  _buildCustomersTab(context),
                  _buildSuppliersTab(context),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCustomersTab(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final currency = context.watch<AppSettingsCubit>().state.defaultCurrency;

    return BlocBuilder<PartiesCubit, PartiesState>(
      builder: (context, state) {
        if (state is PartiesLoading) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        }
        if (state is CustomersLoaded) {
          final customers = state.customers;
          if (customers.isEmpty) {
            return Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  const Icon(
                    Icons.people_outline,
                    size: 56,
                    color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Text(
                    l10n.noDataFound,
                    style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }

          return ListView.separated(
            itemCount: customers.length,
            separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(height: 8),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final c = customers[index];
              final balance = state.balances[c.id] ?? 0.0;

              return Card(
                child: ListTile(
                  leading: const CircleAvatar(
                    backgroundColor: Color(0xFFEFF6FF),
                    child: Icon(Icons.person, color: AppColors.primary),
                  ),
                  title: Text(
                    c.name,
                    style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                  ),
                  subtitle: Text(
                    c.phone.isNotEmpty
                        ? c.phone
                        : (c.address.isNotEmpty ? c.address : '-'),
                  ),
                  trailing: Column(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        CurrencyFormatter.format(
                          balance,
                          currency: currency,
                          isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                        ),
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: balance > 0
                              ? AppColors.warning
                              : AppColors.success,
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        l10n.currentBalanceLabel,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10,
                          color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.push(
                      context,
                      MaterialPageRoute(
                        builder: (_) =>
                            PartyDetailPage(party: c, isCustomer: true),
                      ),
                    ).then((_) => _loadData());
                  },
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        }
        return const SizedBox.shrink();
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSuppliersTab(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final currency = context.watch<AppSettingsCubit>().state.defaultCurrency;

    return BlocBuilder<PartiesCubit, PartiesState>(
      builder: (context, state) {
        if (state is PartiesLoading) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        }
        if (state is SuppliersLoaded) {
          final suppliers = state.suppliers;
          if (suppliers.isEmpty) {
            return Center(
              child: Column(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  const Icon(
                    Icons.business_outlined,
                    size: 56,
                    color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Text(
                    l10n.noDataFound,
                    style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }

          return ListView.separated(
            itemCount: suppliers.length,
            separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(height: 8),
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final s = suppliers[index];
              final balance = state.balances[s.id] ?? 0.0;

              return Card(
                child: ListTile(
                  leading: const CircleAvatar(
                    backgroundColor: Color(0xFFF5F3FF),
                    child: Icon(Icons.business, color: Color(0xFF8B5CF6)),
                  ),
                  title: Text(
                    s.name,
                    style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                  ),
                  subtitle: Text(
                    s.phone.isNotEmpty
                        ? s.phone
                        : (s.address.isNotEmpty ? s.address : '-'),
                  ),
                  trailing: Column(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        CurrencyFormatter.format(
                          balance,
                          currency: currency,
                          isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                        ),
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          color: balance > 0
                              ? AppColors.error
                              : AppColors.success,
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        l10n.currentBalanceLabel,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10,
                          color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  onTap: () {
                    Navigator.push(
                      context,
                      MaterialPageRoute(
                        builder: (_) =>
                            PartyDetailPage(party: s, isCustomer: false),
                      ),
                    ).then((_) => _loadData());
                  },
                ),
              );
            },
          );
        }
        return const SizedBox.shrink();
      },
    );
  }
}

class PartyDetailPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final Party party;
  final bool isCustomer;

  const PartyDetailPage({
    super.key,
    required this.party,
    required this.isCustomer,
  });

  @override
  State<PartyDetailPage> createState() => _PartyDetailPageState();
}

class _PartyDetailPageState extends State<PartyDetailPage> {
  double _balance = 0.0;
  List<Invoice> _invoices = [];
  List<PurchaseInvoice> _purchases = [];
  List<Payment> _payments = [];
  bool _isLoading = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadHistory();
  }

  Future<void> _loadHistory() async {
    setState(() => _isLoading = true);
    final partyRepo = context.read<PartyRepository>();
    final paymentRepo = context.read<PaymentRepository>();
    final invRepo = context.read<InvoiceRepository>();
    final purRepo = context.read<PurchaseRepository>();

    if (widget.isCustomer) {
      final bal = await partyRepo.getCustomerReceivableBalance(widget.party.id);
      final invs = await invRepo.getInvoices(customerId: widget.party.id);
      final pays = await paymentRepo.getPayments(
        partyId: widget.party.id,
        isCustomerPayment: true,
      );

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _balance = bal;
          _invoices = invs;
          _payments = pays;
          _isLoading = false;
        });
      }
    } else {
      final bal = await partyRepo.getSupplierPayableBalance(widget.party.id);
      final purs = await purRepo.getPurchases(supplierId: widget.party.id);
      final pays = await paymentRepo.getPayments(
        partyId: widget.party.id,
        isCustomerPayment: false,
      );

      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _balance = bal;
          _purchases = purs;
          _payments = pays;
          _isLoading = false;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final currency = context.watch<AppSettingsCubit>().state.defaultCurrency;
    final party = widget.party;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(party.name),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.description_outlined),
            tooltip: l10n.statementTitle,
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.push(
                context,
                MaterialPageRoute(
                  builder: (_) => ReportsPage(
                    initialReportType: widget.isCustomer
                        ? ReportType.customerStatement
                        : ReportType.supplierStatement,
                    initialPartyId: party.id,
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.archive_outlined),
            tooltip: l10n.deleteBtn,
            onPressed: () async {
              final cubit = context.read<PartiesCubit>();
              final navigator = Navigator.of(context);
              final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
                context: context,
                builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
                  title: Text(l10n.deleteBtn),
                  content: Text(l10n.cancelInvoiceWarning),
                  actions: [
                    TextButton(
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false),
                      child: Text(l10n.cancelBtn),
                    ),
                    ElevatedButton(
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                        backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
                      ),
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
                      child: Text(l10n.deleteBtn),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
              if (confirmed == true) {
                await cubit.archiveParty(party.id, widget.isCustomer);
                navigator.pop();
              }
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: _isLoading
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : ResponsiveContentContainer(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
