// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\payments\data\repositories\payment_repository_impl.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/hive_database.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/repositories/invoice_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/payments/domain/entities/payment.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/payments/domain/repositories/payment_repository.dart';

class PaymentRepositoryImpl implements PaymentRepository {
  final HiveDatabase database;
  final InvoiceRepository invoiceRepository;

  PaymentRepositoryImpl({
    required this.database,
    required this.invoiceRepository,
  });

  @override
  Future<List<Payment>> getPayments({
    String? partyId,
    bool? isCustomerPayment,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
  }) async {
    final box = database.paymentsBox;
    final results = <Payment>[];

    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final payment = Payment.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));

        if (partyId != null && payment.partyId != partyId) {
          continue;
        }
        if (isCustomerPayment != null &&
            payment.isCustomerPayment != isCustomerPayment) {
          continue;
        }
        if (fromDate != null &&
            payment.paymentDate.isBefore(
              DateTime(fromDate.year, fromDate.month, fromDate.day),
            )) {
          continue;
        }
        if (toDate != null &&
            payment.paymentDate.isAfter(
              DateTime(toDate.year, toDate.month, toDate.day, 23, 59, 59),
            )) {
          continue;
        }

        results.add(payment);
      }
    }

    results.sort((a, b) => b.paymentDate.compareTo(a.paymentDate));
    return results;
  }

  @override
  Future<Payment?> getPaymentById(String id) async {
    final box = database.paymentsBox;
    final map = box.get(id);
    if (map == null) return null;
    return Payment.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
  }

  @override
  Future<List<Payment>> getPaymentsForInvoice(String invoiceId) async {
    final box = database.paymentsBox;
    final results = <Payment>[];

    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final payment = Payment.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
        final hasAllocation = payment.allocations.any(
          (alloc) => alloc.invoiceId == invoiceId,
        );
        if (hasAllocation) {
          results.add(payment);
        }
      }
    }

    results.sort((a, b) => b.paymentDate.compareTo(a.paymentDate));
    return results;
  }

  @override
  Future<void> savePayment(Payment payment) async {
    // 1. Process allocations to invoices
    for (final alloc in payment.allocations) {
      if (alloc.amount > 0) {
        if (payment.isCustomerPayment) {
          await invoiceRepository.recordPaymentOnInvoice(
            invoiceId: alloc.invoiceId,
            paymentAmount: alloc.amount,
          );
        } else {
          // Supplier payment to purchase invoice
          final purchaseBox = database.purchasesBox;
          final purchaseMap = purchaseBox.get(alloc.invoiceId);
          if (purchaseMap != null) {
            final currentPaid =
                (purchaseMap['amountPaid'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
            final finalTotal =
                (purchaseMap['finalTotal'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
            final newPaid = currentPaid + alloc.amount;
            final newRemaining = (finalTotal - newPaid) > 0
                ? (finalTotal - newPaid)
                : 0.0;
            final newStatus = newRemaining == 0.0
                ? 'fullyPaid'
                : 'partiallyPaid';

            purchaseMap['amountPaid'] = newPaid;
            purchaseMap['remainingPayable'] = newRemaining;
            purchaseMap['paymentStatus'] = newStatus;
            purchaseMap['updatedAt'] = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
            await purchaseBox.put(alloc.invoiceId, purchaseMap);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. Persist the payment record
    final box = database.paymentsBox;
    await box.put(payment.id, payment.toMap());
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\payments\domain\entities\payment.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

enum PaymentMethod { cash, bank, card, other }

class PaymentAllocation extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String paymentId;
  final String invoiceId;
  final String invoiceNumber;
  final double amount;
  final DateTime allocatedAt;

  const PaymentAllocation({
    required this.id,
    required this.paymentId,
    required this.invoiceId,
    required this.invoiceNumber,
    required this.amount,
    required this.allocatedAt,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'paymentId': paymentId,
      'invoiceId': invoiceId,
      'invoiceNumber': invoiceNumber,
      'amount': amount,
      'allocatedAt': allocatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory PaymentAllocation.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return PaymentAllocation(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? '',
      paymentId: map['paymentId'] as String? ?? '',
      invoiceId: map['invoiceId'] as String? ?? '',
      invoiceNumber: map['invoiceNumber'] as String? ?? '',
      amount: (map['amount'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      allocatedAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['allocatedAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    id,
    paymentId,
    invoiceId,
    invoiceNumber,
    amount,
    allocatedAt,
  ];
}

class Payment extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final double amount;
  final String currency;
  final DateTime paymentDate;
  final PaymentMethod method;
  final String? partyId;
  final String partyName;
  final bool
  isCustomerPayment; // true = received from customer, false = paid to supplier
  final String referenceNumber;
  final String notes;
  final List<PaymentAllocation> allocations;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  const Payment({
    required this.id,
    required this.amount,
    this.currency = 'EGP',
    required this.paymentDate,
    this.method = PaymentMethod.cash,
    this.partyId,
    this.partyName = '',
    this.isCustomerPayment = true,
    this.referenceNumber = '',
    this.notes = '',
    this.allocations = const [],
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  double get totalAllocated =>
      allocations.fold(0.0, (sum, item) => sum + item.amount);

  double get unallocatedAmount =>
      (amount - totalAllocated) > 0.0 ? (amount - totalAllocated) : 0.0;

  Payment copyWith({
    String? id,
    double? amount,
    String? currency,
    DateTime? paymentDate,
    PaymentMethod? method,
    String? partyId,
    String? partyName,
    bool? isCustomerPayment,
    String? referenceNumber,
    String? notes,
    List<PaymentAllocation>? allocations,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return Payment(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      amount: amount ?? this.amount,
      currency: currency ?? this.currency,
      paymentDate: paymentDate ?? this.paymentDate,
      method: method ?? this.method,
      partyId: partyId ?? this.partyId,
      partyName: partyName ?? this.partyName,
      isCustomerPayment: isCustomerPayment ?? this.isCustomerPayment,
      referenceNumber: referenceNumber ?? this.referenceNumber,
      notes: notes ?? this.notes,
      allocations: allocations ?? this.allocations,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'amount': amount,
      'currency': currency,
      'paymentDate': paymentDate.toIso8601String(),
      'method': method.name,
      'partyId': partyId,
      'partyName': partyName,
      'isCustomerPayment': isCustomerPayment,
      'referenceNumber': referenceNumber,
      'notes': notes,
      'allocations': allocations.map((x) => x.toMap()).toList(),
      'createdAt': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updatedAt': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory Payment.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return Payment(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? '',
      amount: (map['amount'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      currency: map['currency'] as String? ?? 'EGP',
      paymentDate:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['paymentDate'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      method: PaymentMethod.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == map['method'],
        orElse: () => PaymentMethod.cash,
      ),
      partyId: map['partyId'] as String?,
      partyName: map['partyName'] as String? ?? '',
      isCustomerPayment: map['isCustomerPayment'] as bool? ?? true,
      referenceNumber: map['referenceNumber'] as String? ?? '',
      notes: map['notes'] as String? ?? '',
      allocations:
          (map['allocations'] as List<dynamic>?)
              ?.map(
                (x) => PaymentAllocation.fromMap(
                  Map<String, dynamic>.from(x as Map),
                ),
              )
              .toList() ??
          [],
      createdAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['createdAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['updatedAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    id,
    amount,
    currency,
    paymentDate,
    method,
    partyId,
    partyName,
    isCustomerPayment,
    allocations,
    createdAt,
  ];
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\payments\domain\repositories\payment_repository.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/features/payments/domain/entities/payment.dart';

abstract class PaymentRepository {
  Future<List<Payment>> getPayments({
    String? partyId,
    bool? isCustomerPayment,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
  });
  Future<Payment?> getPaymentById(String id);
  Future<List<Payment>> getPaymentsForInvoice(String invoiceId);
  Future<void> savePayment(Payment payment);
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\payments\presentation\cubit\payments_cubit.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/features/audit/domain/entities/audit_log.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/audit/domain/repositories/audit_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/repositories/invoice_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/payments/domain/entities/payment.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/payments/domain/repositories/payment_repository.dart';

abstract class PaymentsState extends Equatable {
  const PaymentsState();
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class PaymentsInitial extends PaymentsState {}

class PaymentsLoading extends PaymentsState {}

class PaymentsLoaded extends PaymentsState {
  final List<Payment> payments;

  const PaymentsLoaded(this.payments);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [payments];
}

class PaymentOperationSuccess extends PaymentsState {
  final String message;

  const PaymentOperationSuccess(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class PaymentsError extends PaymentsState {
  final String message;

  const PaymentsError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class PaymentsCubit extends Cubit<PaymentsState> {
  final PaymentRepository paymentRepository;
  final InvoiceRepository invoiceRepository;
  final AuditRepository auditRepository;

  PaymentsCubit({
    required this.paymentRepository,
    required this.invoiceRepository,
    required this.auditRepository,
  }) : super(PaymentsInitial());

  Future<void> loadPayments({
    String? partyId,
    bool? isCustomerPayment,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
  }) async {
    emit(PaymentsLoading());
    try {
      final list = await paymentRepository.getPayments(
        partyId: partyId,
        isCustomerPayment: isCustomerPayment,
        fromDate: fromDate,
        toDate: toDate,
      );
      emit(PaymentsLoaded(list));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PaymentsError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<bool> recordPayment({
    required double amount,
    required PaymentMethod method,
    String? partyId,
    String partyName = '',
    bool isCustomerPayment = true,
    String? invoiceId,
    String invoiceNumber = '',
    String referenceNumber = '',
    String notes = '',
    String currency = 'EGP',
  }) async {
    if (amount <= 0.0) {
      emit(const PaymentsError('Payment amount must be greater than zero.'));
      return false;
    }

    emit(PaymentsLoading());
    try {
      final paymentId = const Uuid().v4();
      final now = DateTime.now();

      final allocations = <PaymentAllocation>[];
      if (invoiceId != null && invoiceId.isNotEmpty) {
        // Prevent unintended overpayment
        if (isCustomerPayment) {
          final invoice = await invoiceRepository.getInvoiceById(invoiceId);
          if (invoice == null) {
            emit(const PaymentsError('Invoice not found.'));
            return false;
          }
          if (amount > invoice.remainingBalance + 0.001) {
            emit(
              PaymentsError(
                'Payment amount ($amount $currency) exceeds remaining balance (${invoice.remainingBalance} $currency).',
              ),
            );
            return false;
          }
        }

        allocations.add(
          PaymentAllocation(
            id: const Uuid().v4(),
            paymentId: paymentId,
            invoiceId: invoiceId,
            invoiceNumber: invoiceNumber,
            amount: amount,
            allocatedAt: now,
          ),
        );
      }

      final payment = Payment(
        id: paymentId,
        amount: amount,
        currency: currency,
        paymentDate: now,
        method: method,
        partyId: partyId,
        partyName: partyName.trim(),
        isCustomerPayment: isCustomerPayment,
        referenceNumber: referenceNumber.trim(),
        notes: notes.trim(),
        allocations: allocations,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      );

      await paymentRepository.savePayment(payment);

      await auditRepository.logAction(
        AuditLog(
          id: const Uuid().v4(),
          action: 'record_payment',
          documentType: 'payment',
          documentId: payment.id,
          description:
              'Recorded payment of $amount $currency via ${method.name}',
          timestamp: now,
        ),
      );

      emit(const PaymentOperationSuccess('Payment recorded successfully'));
      await loadPayments(isCustomerPayment: isCustomerPayment);
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PaymentsError(e.toString()));
      return false;
    }
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\products\data\repositories\product_repository_impl.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/hive_database.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/products/domain/entities/product.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/products/domain/repositories/product_repository.dart';

class ProductRepositoryImpl implements ProductRepository {
  final HiveDatabase database;

  ProductRepositoryImpl(this.database);

  @override
  Future<List<Product>> getProducts({
    String? query,
    String? category,
    bool includeArchived = false,
  }) async {
    final box = database.productsBox;
    final results = <Product>[];

    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final product = Product.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
        if (includeArchived || !product.isArchived) {
          if (category != null &&
              category.isNotEmpty &&
              product.category != category) {
            continue;
          }
          if (query == null || query.trim().isEmpty) {
            results.add(product);
          } else {
            final q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
            if (product.name.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
                product.sku.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
                product.description.toLowerCase().contains(q)) {
              results.add(product);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    results.sort((a, b) => a.name.compareTo(b.name));
    return results;
  }

  @override
  Future<Product?> getProductById(String id) async {
    final box = database.productsBox;
    final map = box.get(id);
    if (map == null) return null;
    return Product.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
  }

  @override
  Future<void> saveProduct(Product product) async {
    final box = database.productsBox;
    await box.put(product.id, product.toMap());
  }

  @override
  Future<void> archiveProduct(String id) async {
    final product = await getProductById(id);
    if (product != null) {
      await saveProduct(
        product.copyWith(isArchived: true, updatedAt: DateTime.now()),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<List<String>> getCategories() async {
    final box = database.productsBox;
    final categories = <String>{};

    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final cat = map['category'] as String?;
        if (cat != null && cat.trim().isNotEmpty) {
          categories.add(cat.trim());
        }
      }
    }

    final list = categories.toList()..sort();
    return list;
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\products\domain\entities\product.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

class Product extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String sku;
  final String description;
  final double defaultUnitPrice;
  final String category;
  final bool isArchived;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  const Product({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    this.sku = '',
    this.description = '',
    required this.defaultUnitPrice,
    this.category = '',
    this.isArchived = false,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  Product copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? name,
    String? sku,
    String? description,
    double? defaultUnitPrice,
    String? category,
    bool? isArchived,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return Product(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      sku: sku ?? this.sku,
      description: description ?? this.description,
      defaultUnitPrice: defaultUnitPrice ?? this.defaultUnitPrice,
      category: category ?? this.category,
      isArchived: isArchived ?? this.isArchived,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'sku': sku,
      'description': description,
      'defaultUnitPrice': defaultUnitPrice,
      'category': category,
      'isArchived': isArchived,
      'createdAt': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updatedAt': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory Product.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return Product(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? '',
      name: map['name'] as String? ?? '',
      sku: map['sku'] as String? ?? '',
      description: map['description'] as String? ?? '',
      defaultUnitPrice: (map['defaultUnitPrice'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      category: map['category'] as String? ?? '',
      isArchived: map['isArchived'] as bool? ?? false,
      createdAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['createdAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['updatedAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    id,
    name,
    sku,
    description,
    defaultUnitPrice,
    category,
    isArchived,
    createdAt,
    updatedAt,
  ];
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\products\domain\repositories\product_repository.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/features/products/domain/entities/product.dart';

abstract class ProductRepository {
  Future<List<Product>> getProducts({
    String? query,
    String? category,
    bool includeArchived = false,
  });
  Future<Product?> getProductById(String id);
  Future<void> saveProduct(Product product);
  Future<void> archiveProduct(String id);
  Future<List<String>> getCategories();
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\products\presentation\cubit\products_cubit.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/features/products/domain/entities/product.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/products/domain/repositories/product_repository.dart';

abstract class ProductsState extends Equatable {
  const ProductsState();
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class ProductsInitial extends ProductsState {}

class ProductsLoading extends ProductsState {}

class ProductsLoaded extends ProductsState {
  final List<Product> products;
  final List<String> categories;

  const ProductsLoaded({required this.products, required this.categories});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [products, categories];
}

class ProductOperationSuccess extends ProductsState {
  final String message;

  const ProductOperationSuccess(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class ProductsError extends ProductsState {
  final String message;

  const ProductsError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class ProductsCubit extends Cubit<ProductsState> {
  final ProductRepository repository;

  ProductsCubit(this.repository) : super(ProductsInitial());

  Future<void> loadProducts({String? query, String? category}) async {
    emit(ProductsLoading());
    try {
      final products = await repository.getProducts(
        query: query,
        category: category,
      );
      final categories = await repository.getCategories();
      emit(ProductsLoaded(products: products, categories: categories));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ProductsError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> saveProduct({
    String? id,
    required String name,
    String sku = '',
    String description = '',
    required double defaultUnitPrice,
    String category = '',
  }) async {
    if (name.trim().isEmpty) {
      emit(const ProductsError('Product name cannot be empty.'));
      return;
    }
    if (defaultUnitPrice < 0.0) {
      emit(const ProductsError('Unit price cannot be negative.'));
      return;
    }

    emit(ProductsLoading());
    try {
      final productId = id ?? const Uuid().v4();
      final now = DateTime.now();

      final product = Product(
        id: productId,
        name: name.trim(),
        sku: sku.trim(),
        description: description.trim(),
        defaultUnitPrice: defaultUnitPrice,
        category: category.trim(),
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      );

      await repository.saveProduct(product);
      emit(const ProductOperationSuccess('Product saved successfully'));
      await loadProducts();
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ProductsError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> archiveProduct(String id) async {
    emit(ProductsLoading());
    try {
      await repository.archiveProduct(id);
      emit(const ProductOperationSuccess('Product archived successfully'));
      await loadProducts();
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ProductsError(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

class ProductsPage extends StatefulWidget {

class _ProductsPageState extends State<ProductsPage> {

    context.read<ProductsCubit>().loadProducts();
  }

  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _showAddEditProductDialog(BuildContext context, [Product? existing]) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final nameCtrl = TextEditingController(text: existing?.name ?? '');
    final skuCtrl = TextEditingController(text: existing?.sku ?? '');

      text: existing != null ? existing.defaultUnitPrice.toString() : '0.0',
    );
    final catCtrl = TextEditingController(text: existing?.category ?? '');

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(existing != null ? l10n.editBtn : l10n.addProductBtn),
        content: SingleChildScrollView(
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              TextField(
                controller: nameCtrl,
                decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.itemDescription),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              TextField(
                controller: skuCtrl,
                decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.skuLabel),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              TextField(
                controller: priceCtrl,
                keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                  decimal: true,
                ),
                decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.defaultPriceLabel),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              TextField(
                controller: catCtrl,
                decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.categoryLabel),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              TextField(
                controller: descCtrl,
                decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: l10n.notesLabel),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
            child: Text(l10n.cancelBtn),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: () {
              final name = nameCtrl.text.trim();
              if (name.isEmpty) return;

              final price = double.tryParse(priceCtrl.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;

              context.read<ProductsCubit>().saveProduct(
                id: existing?.id,
                name: name,
                sku: skuCtrl.text.trim(),
                description: descCtrl.text.trim(),
                defaultUnitPrice: price,
                category: catCtrl.text.trim(),
              );
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
            },
            child: Text(l10n.saveBtn),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

          ),
        ],

      body: ResponsiveContentContainer(

                              icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                              label: Text(l10n.addProductBtn),
                              onPressed: () =>
                                  _showAddEditProductDialog(context),

                    return ListView.separated(

                              ),
                              child: const Icon(
                                Icons.inventory_2,
                                color: AppColors.primary,
                              ),
                            ),
                            title: Text(
                              p.name,
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              ),

                                      .read<ProductsCubit>()
                                      .archiveProduct(p.id),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        );
                      },

                  }
                  return const SizedBox.shrink();
                },
              ),

  }

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\purchases\data\repositories\purchase_repository_impl.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/hive_database.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/purchases/domain/entities/purchase_invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/purchases/domain/repositories/purchase_repository.dart';

class PurchaseRepositoryImpl implements PurchaseRepository {
  final HiveDatabase database;

  PurchaseRepositoryImpl(this.database);

  @override
  Future<List<PurchaseInvoice>> getPurchases({
    String? supplierId,
    DocumentStatus? documentStatus,
    PaymentStatus? paymentStatus,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
    String? query,
  }) async {
    final box = database.purchasesBox;
    final results = <PurchaseInvoice>[];

    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final purchase = PurchaseInvoice.fromMap(
          Map<String, dynamic>.from(map),
        );

        if (supplierId != null && purchase.supplierId != supplierId) {
          continue;
        }
        if (documentStatus != null &&
            purchase.documentStatus != documentStatus) {
          continue;
        }
        if (paymentStatus != null && purchase.paymentStatus != paymentStatus) {
          continue;
        }
        if (fromDate != null &&
            purchase.issueDate.isBefore(
              DateTime(fromDate.year, fromDate.month, fromDate.day),
            )) {
          continue;
        }
        if (toDate != null &&
            purchase.issueDate.isAfter(
              DateTime(toDate.year, toDate.month, toDate.day, 23, 59, 59),
            )) {
          continue;
        }
        if (query != null && query.trim().isNotEmpty) {
          final q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
          final matchesNumber = purchase.purchaseNumber.toLowerCase().contains(
            q,
          );
          final matchesSupplier = purchase.supplierName.toLowerCase().contains(
            q,
          );
          final matchesItem = purchase.items.any(
            (item) => item.description.toLowerCase().contains(q),
          );
          if (!matchesNumber && !matchesSupplier && !matchesItem) {
            continue;
          }
        }

        results.add(purchase);
      }
    }

    results.sort((a, b) => b.issueDate.compareTo(a.issueDate));
    return results;
  }

  @override
  Future<PurchaseInvoice?> getPurchaseById(String id) async {
    final box = database.purchasesBox;
    final map = box.get(id);
    if (map == null) return null;
    return PurchaseInvoice.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
  }

  @override
  Future<void> savePurchase(PurchaseInvoice purchase) async {
    final box = database.purchasesBox;
    await box.put(purchase.id, purchase.toMap());
  }

  @override
  Future<void> recordPaymentOnPurchase({
    required String purchaseId,
    required double paymentAmount,
  }) async {
    final purchase = await getPurchaseById(purchaseId);
    if (purchase == null) return;

    final newPaid = purchase.amountPaid + paymentAmount;
    final newRemaining = (purchase.finalTotal - newPaid) > 0.0
        ? (purchase.finalTotal - newPaid)
        : 0.0;

    final PaymentStatus newStatus;
    if (newRemaining == 0.0) {
      newStatus = PaymentStatus.fullyPaid;
    } else if (newPaid > 0.0) {
      newStatus = PaymentStatus.partiallyPaid;
    } else {
      newStatus = PaymentStatus.unpaid;
    }

    final updated = purchase.copyWith(
      amountPaid: newPaid,
      remainingPayable: newRemaining,
      paymentStatus: newStatus,
      updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
    );
    await savePurchase(updated);
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\purchases\domain\entities\purchase_invoice.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';

class PurchaseInvoiceItem extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String description;
  final double quantity;
  final double unitPrice;
  final double lineTotal;

  const PurchaseInvoiceItem({
    required this.id,
    required this.description,
    required this.quantity,
    required this.unitPrice,
    required this.lineTotal,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'description': description,
      'quantity': quantity,
      'unitPrice': unitPrice,
      'lineTotal': lineTotal,
    };
  }

  factory PurchaseInvoiceItem.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return PurchaseInvoiceItem(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? '',
      description: map['description'] as String? ?? '',
      quantity: (map['quantity'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 1.0,
      unitPrice: (map['unitPrice'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      lineTotal: (map['lineTotal'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [id, description, quantity, unitPrice, lineTotal];
}

class PurchaseInvoice extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String purchaseNumber;
  final String supplierId;
  final String supplierName;
  final String supplierPhone;
  final DateTime issueDate;
  final List<PurchaseInvoiceItem> items;
  final double subtotal;
  final String discountType;
  final double discountInput;
  final double discountAmount;
  final double taxableBase;
  final double taxRate;
  final double taxAmount;
  final double finalTotal;
  final double amountPaid;
  final double remainingPayable;
  final String currency;
  final String notes;
  final DocumentStatus documentStatus;
  final PaymentStatus paymentStatus;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  const PurchaseInvoice({
    required this.id,
    required this.purchaseNumber,
    required this.supplierId,
    required this.supplierName,
    this.supplierPhone = '',
    required this.issueDate,
    required this.items,
    required this.subtotal,
    this.discountType = 'fixed',
    this.discountInput = 0.0,
    this.discountAmount = 0.0,
    required this.taxableBase,
    this.taxRate = 0.0,
    this.taxAmount = 0.0,
    required this.finalTotal,
    this.amountPaid = 0.0,
    required this.remainingPayable,
    this.currency = 'EGP',
    this.notes = '',
    this.documentStatus = DocumentStatus.issued,
    this.paymentStatus = PaymentStatus.unpaid,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  PurchaseInvoice copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? purchaseNumber,
    String? supplierId,
    String? supplierName,
    String? supplierPhone,
    DateTime? issueDate,
    List<PurchaseInvoiceItem>? items,
    double? subtotal,
    String? discountType,
    double? discountInput,
    double? discountAmount,
    double? taxableBase,
    double? taxRate,
    double? taxAmount,
    double? finalTotal,
    double? amountPaid,
    double? remainingPayable,
    String? currency,
    String? notes,
    DocumentStatus? documentStatus,
    PaymentStatus? paymentStatus,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return PurchaseInvoice(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      purchaseNumber: purchaseNumber ?? this.purchaseNumber,
      supplierId: supplierId ?? this.supplierId,
      supplierName: supplierName ?? this.supplierName,
      supplierPhone: supplierPhone ?? this.supplierPhone,
      issueDate: issueDate ?? this.issueDate,
      items: items ?? this.items,
      subtotal: subtotal ?? this.subtotal,
      discountType: discountType ?? this.discountType,
      discountInput: discountInput ?? this.discountInput,
      discountAmount: discountAmount ?? this.discountAmount,
      taxableBase: taxableBase ?? this.taxableBase,
      taxRate: taxRate ?? this.taxRate,
      taxAmount: taxAmount ?? this.taxAmount,
      finalTotal: finalTotal ?? this.finalTotal,
      amountPaid: amountPaid ?? this.amountPaid,
      remainingPayable: remainingPayable ?? this.remainingPayable,
      currency: currency ?? this.currency,
      notes: notes ?? this.notes,
      documentStatus: documentStatus ?? this.documentStatus,
      paymentStatus: paymentStatus ?? this.paymentStatus,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'purchaseNumber': purchaseNumber,
      'supplierId': supplierId,
      'supplierName': supplierName,
      'supplierPhone': supplierPhone,
      'issueDate': issueDate.toIso8601String(),
      'items': items.map((x) => x.toMap()).toList(),
      'subtotal': subtotal,
      'discountType': discountType,
      'discountInput': discountInput,
      'discountAmount': discountAmount,
      'taxableBase': taxableBase,
      'taxRate': taxRate,
      'taxAmount': taxAmount,
      'finalTotal': finalTotal,
      'amountPaid': amountPaid,
      'remainingPayable': remainingPayable,
      'currency': currency,
      'notes': notes,
      'documentStatus': documentStatus.name,
      'paymentStatus': paymentStatus.name,
      'createdAt': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updatedAt': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory PurchaseInvoice.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return PurchaseInvoice(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? '',
      purchaseNumber: map['purchaseNumber'] as String? ?? '',
      supplierId: map['supplierId'] as String? ?? '',
      supplierName: map['supplierName'] as String? ?? '',
      supplierPhone: map['supplierPhone'] as String? ?? '',
      issueDate:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['issueDate'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      items:
          (map['items'] as List<dynamic>?)
              ?.map(
                (x) => PurchaseInvoiceItem.fromMap(
                  Map<String, dynamic>.from(x as Map),
                ),
              )
              .toList() ??
          [],
      subtotal: (map['subtotal'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      discountType: map['discountType'] as String? ?? 'fixed',
      discountInput: (map['discountInput'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      discountAmount: (map['discountAmount'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      taxableBase: (map['taxableBase'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      taxRate: (map['taxRate'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      taxAmount: (map['taxAmount'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      finalTotal: (map['finalTotal'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      amountPaid: (map['amountPaid'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      remainingPayable: (map['remainingPayable'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      currency: map['currency'] as String? ?? 'EGP',
      notes: map['notes'] as String? ?? '',
      documentStatus: DocumentStatus.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == map['documentStatus'],
        orElse: () => DocumentStatus.issued,
      ),
      paymentStatus: PaymentStatus.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == map['paymentStatus'],
        orElse: () => PaymentStatus.unpaid,
      ),
      createdAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['createdAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['updatedAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    id,
    purchaseNumber,
    supplierId,
    supplierName,
    finalTotal,
    amountPaid,
    remainingPayable,
    documentStatus,
    paymentStatus,
    createdAt,
  ];
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\purchases\domain\repositories\purchase_repository.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/purchases/domain/entities/purchase_invoice.dart';

abstract class PurchaseRepository {
  Future<List<PurchaseInvoice>> getPurchases({
    String? supplierId,
    DocumentStatus? documentStatus,
    PaymentStatus? paymentStatus,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
    String? query,
  });
  Future<PurchaseInvoice?> getPurchaseById(String id);
  Future<void> savePurchase(PurchaseInvoice purchase);
  Future<void> recordPaymentOnPurchase({
    required String purchaseId,
    required double paymentAmount,
  });
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\purchases\presentation\cubit\purchases_cubit.dart ===

import 'package:decimal/decimal.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/financial_calculator.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/money.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/sequence_manager.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/audit/domain/entities/audit_log.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/audit/domain/repositories/audit_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/purchases/domain/entities/purchase_invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/purchases/domain/repositories/purchase_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/shop_setup/domain/repositories/shop_profile_repository.dart';

abstract class PurchasesState extends Equatable {
  const PurchasesState();
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class PurchasesInitial extends PurchasesState {}

class PurchasesLoading extends PurchasesState {}

class PurchasesLoaded extends PurchasesState {
  final List<PurchaseInvoice> purchases;

  const PurchasesLoaded(this.purchases);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [purchases];
}

class PurchaseOperationSuccess extends PurchasesState {
  final String message;
  final PurchaseInvoice? purchase;

  const PurchaseOperationSuccess(this.message, {this.purchase});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message, purchase];
}

class PurchasesError extends PurchasesState {
  final String message;

  const PurchasesError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class PurchasesCubit extends Cubit<PurchasesState> {
  final PurchaseRepository purchaseRepository;
  final ShopProfileRepository shopProfileRepository;
  final SequenceManager sequenceManager;
  final AuditRepository auditRepository;

  PurchasesCubit({
    required this.purchaseRepository,
    required this.shopProfileRepository,
    required this.sequenceManager,
    required this.auditRepository,
  }) : super(PurchasesInitial());

  Future<void> loadPurchases({
    String? supplierId,
    DocumentStatus? documentStatus,
    PaymentStatus? paymentStatus,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
    String? query,
  }) async {
    emit(PurchasesLoading());
    try {
      final list = await purchaseRepository.getPurchases(
        supplierId: supplierId,
        documentStatus: documentStatus,
        paymentStatus: paymentStatus,
        fromDate: fromDate,
        toDate: toDate,
        query: query,
      );
      emit(PurchasesLoaded(list));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PurchasesError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<PurchaseInvoice?> createPurchase({
    required String supplierId,
    required String supplierName,
    String supplierPhone = '',
    required List<({String description, double quantity, double unitPrice})>
    rawItems,
    DiscountType discountType = DiscountType.fixed,
    double discountValue = 0.0,
    double taxRate = 0.0,
    String notes = '',
  }) async {
    if (rawItems.isEmpty) {
      emit(const PurchasesError('Purchase must contain at least one item.'));
      return null;
    }

    emit(PurchasesLoading());
    try {
      final shopProfile = await shopProfileRepository.getProfile();
      final currency = shopProfile?.defaultCurrency ?? 'EGP';

      final calcItems = rawItems
          .map(
            (item) => (
              quantity: Decimal.parse(item.quantity.toString()),
              unitPrice: Money.fromNum(item.unitPrice, currency),
            ),
          )
          .toList();

      final calcResult = FinancialCalculator.calculateInvoice(
        rawItems: calcItems,
        discountType: discountType,
        discountValue: Decimal.parse(discountValue.toString()),
        taxRatePercent: Decimal.parse(taxRate.toString()),
        currency: currency,
      );

      final now = DateTime.now();
      final purchaseNumber = await sequenceManager.getNextPurchaseNumber(now);
      final purchaseId = const Uuid().v4();

      final items = <PurchaseInvoiceItem>[];
      for (int i = 0; i < rawItems.length; i++) {
        final raw = rawItems[i];
        final calc = calcResult.items[i];
        items.add(
          PurchaseInvoiceItem(
            id: const Uuid().v4(),
            description: raw.description.trim(),
            quantity: calc.quantity.toDouble(),
            unitPrice: calc.unitPrice.toDouble(),
            lineTotal: calc.lineTotal.toDouble(),
          ),
        );
      }

      final purchase = PurchaseInvoice(
        id: purchaseId,
        purchaseNumber: purchaseNumber,
        supplierId: supplierId,
        supplierName: supplierName.trim(),
        supplierPhone: supplierPhone.trim(),
        issueDate: now,
        items: items,
        subtotal: calcResult.subtotal.toDouble(),
        discountType: discountType == DiscountType.fixed
            ? 'fixed'
            : 'percentage',
        discountInput: discountValue,
        discountAmount: calcResult.discountAmount.toDouble(),
        taxableBase: calcResult.taxableBase.toDouble(),
        taxRate: taxRate,
        taxAmount: calcResult.taxAmount.toDouble(),
        finalTotal: calcResult.finalTotal.toDouble(),
        amountPaid: 0.0,
        remainingPayable: calcResult.finalTotal.toDouble(),
        currency: currency,
        notes: notes.trim(),
        documentStatus: DocumentStatus.issued,
        paymentStatus: PaymentStatus.unpaid,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      );

      await purchaseRepository.savePurchase(purchase);

      await auditRepository.logAction(
        AuditLog(
          id: const Uuid().v4(),
          action: 'create_purchase',
          documentType: 'purchase_invoice',
          documentId: purchase.id,
          description:
              'Recorded purchase ${purchase.purchaseNumber} from $supplierName for ${purchase.finalTotal} $currency',
          timestamp: now,
        ),
      );

      emit(
        PurchaseOperationSuccess(
          'Purchase recorded successfully',
          purchase: purchase,
        ),
      );
      await loadPurchases();
      return purchase;
    } catch (e) {
      emit(PurchasesError(e.toString()));
      return null;
    }
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\purchases\presentation\pages\create_purchase_page.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/localization/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/responsive/responsive_layout.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/entities/party.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/presentation/cubit/parties_cubit.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/purchases/presentation/cubit/purchases_cubit.dart';

class _PurchaseItemRow {
  final TextEditingController descCtrl;
  final TextEditingController qtyCtrl;
  final TextEditingController priceCtrl;

  _PurchaseItemRow()
    : descCtrl = TextEditingController(),
      qtyCtrl = TextEditingController(text: '1'),
      priceCtrl = TextEditingController(text: '0.0');

  void dispose() {
    descCtrl.dispose();
    qtyCtrl.dispose();
    priceCtrl.dispose();
  }
}

class CreatePurchasePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const CreatePurchasePage({super.key});

  @override
  State<CreatePurchasePage> createState() => _CreatePurchasePageState();
}

class _CreatePurchasePageState extends State<CreatePurchasePage> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  Party? _selectedSupplier;
  final _items = <_PurchaseItemRow>[];

  final _discountController = TextEditingController(text: '0.0');
  final _taxRateController = TextEditingController(text: '0.0');
  final _notesController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    context.read<PartiesCubit>().loadSuppliers();
    _addItem();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    for (final i in _items) {
      i.dispose();
    }
    _discountController.dispose();
    _taxRateController.dispose();
    _notesController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _addItem() {
    setState(() => _items.add(_PurchaseItemRow()));
  }

  void _removeItem(int index) {
    if (_items.length > 1) {
      setState(() {
        final removed = _items.removeAt(index);
        removed.dispose();
      });
    }
  }

  void _submit() {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);

    if (_selectedSupplier == null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(l10n.setupRequiredField),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    final rawItems =
        <({String description, double quantity, double unitPrice})>[];
    for (final item in _items) {
      final desc = item.descCtrl.text.trim();
      final qty = double.tryParse(item.qtyCtrl.text.trim()) ?? 1.0;
      final price = double.tryParse(item.priceCtrl.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;
      if (desc.isNotEmpty && qty > 0) {
        rawItems.add((description: desc, quantity: qty, unitPrice: price));
      }
    }

    if (rawItems.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(l10n.setupRequiredField),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    final discountVal = double.tryParse(_discountController.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;
    final taxRateVal = double.tryParse(_taxRateController.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;

    context.read<PurchasesCubit>().createPurchase(
      supplierId: _selectedSupplier!.id,
      supplierName: _selectedSupplier!.name,
      supplierPhone: _selectedSupplier!.phone,
      rawItems: rawItems,
      discountValue: discountVal,
      taxRate: taxRateVal,
      notes: _notesController.text.trim(),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);

    return BlocListener<PurchasesCubit, PurchasesState>(
      listener: (context, state) {
        if (state is PurchaseOperationSuccess) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
              .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(state.message)));
          Navigator.pop(context);
        } else if (state is PurchasesError) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            SnackBar(
              content: Text(state.message),
              backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
            ),
          );
        }
      },
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.actionNewPurchase)),
        body: ResponsiveContentContainer(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
          child: Form(
            key: _formKey,
            child: SingleChildScrollView(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  // Supplier Card
                  Card(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            l10n.supplierLabel,
                            style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 12),
                          BlocBuilder<PartiesCubit, PartiesState>(
                            builder: (context, state) {
                              List<Party> suppliers = [];
                              if (state is SuppliersLoaded) {
                                suppliers = state.suppliers;
                              }
                              return DropdownButtonFormField<Party?>(
                                initialValue: _selectedSupplier,
                                decoration: InputDecoration(
                                  labelText: l10n.supplierLabel,
                                  prefixIcon: const Icon(
                                    Icons.business_outlined,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                items: suppliers
                                    .map(
                                      (s) => DropdownMenuItem(
                                        value: s,
                                        child: Text('${s.name} (${s.phone})'),
                                      ),
                                    )
                                    .toList(),
                                onChanged: (val) =>
                                    setState(() => _selectedSupplier = val),
                                validator: (v) =>
                                    v == null ? l10n.setupRequiredField : null,
                              );
                            },
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),

                  // Line Items
                  Card(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Row(
                            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                l10n.itemsSection,
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                ),
                              ),
                              TextButton.icon(
                                icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                                label: Text(l10n.addItemBtn),
                                onPressed: _addItem,
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                          const Divider(),
                          ..._items.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                            final idx = entry.key;
                            final item = entry.value;

                            return Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12.0),
                              child: Row(
                                children: [
                                  Expanded(
                                    flex: 4,
                                    child: TextFormField(
                                      controller: item.descCtrl,
                                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                                        labelText: l10n.itemDescription,
                                        isDense: true,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                  Expanded(
                                    flex: 2,
                                    child: TextFormField(
                                      controller: item.qtyCtrl,
                                      keyboardType:
                                          const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                                            decimal: true,
                                          ),
                                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                                        labelText: l10n.itemQuantity,
                                        isDense: true,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                                  Expanded(
                                    flex: 2,
                                    child: TextFormField(
                                      controller: item.priceCtrl,
                                      keyboardType:
                                          const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                                            decimal: true,
                                          ),
                                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                                        labelText: l10n.itemUnitPrice,
                                        isDense: true,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  IconButton(
                                    icon: const Icon(
                                      Icons.delete_outline,
                                      color: AppColors.error,
                                    ),
                                    onPressed: _items.length > 1
                                        ? () => _removeItem(idx)
                                        : null,
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            );
                          }),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),

                  // Notes & Discounts
                  Card(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                      child: Column(
                        children: [
                          Row(
                            children: [
                              Expanded(
                                child: TextFormField(
                                  controller: _discountController,
                                  keyboardType:
                                      const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                                        decimal: true,
                                      ),
                                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                                    labelText: l10n.discountLabel,
                                    isDense: true,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: 12),
                              Expanded(
                                child: TextFormField(
                                  controller: _taxRateController,
                                  keyboardType:
                                      const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                                        decimal: true,
                                      ),
                                  decoration: InputDecoration(
                                    labelText: l10n.taxRateLabel,
                                    isDense: true,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 12),
                          TextFormField(
                            controller: _notesController,
                            maxLines: 2,
                            decoration: InputDecoration(
                              labelText: l10n.notesLabel,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 24),

                  ElevatedButton(onPressed: _submit, child: Text(l10n.saveBtn)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
