import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/localization/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency_formatter.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/responsive/responsive_layout.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/purchases/presentation/cubit/purchases_cubit.dart';

import 'create_purchase_page.dart';

class PurchasesPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const PurchasesPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<PurchasesPage> createState() => _PurchasesPageState();
}

class _PurchasesPageState extends State<PurchasesPage> {
  final _searchController = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    context.read<PurchasesCubit>().loadPurchases();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _onSearch() {
    context.read<PurchasesCubit>().loadPurchases(
      query: _searchController.text.trim(),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(l10n.navPurchases),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
            tooltip: l10n.actionNewPurchase,
            onPressed: () {
              Navigator.push(
                context,
                MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const CreatePurchasePage()),
              ).then((_) => _onSearch());
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: ResponsiveContentContainer(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            TextField(
              controller: _searchController,
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                hintText: l10n.searchHint,
                prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search),
                suffixIcon: _searchController.text.isNotEmpty
                    ? IconButton(
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.clear),
                        onPressed: () {
                          _searchController.clear();
                          _onSearch();
                        },
                      )
                    : null,
              ),
              onChanged: (_) => _onSearch(),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Expanded(
              child: BlocBuilder<PurchasesCubit, PurchasesState>(
                builder: (context, state) {
                  if (state is PurchasesLoading) {
                    return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
                  }
                  if (state is PurchasesLoaded) {
                    final purchases = state.purchases;
                    if (purchases.isEmpty) {
                      return Center(
                        child: Column(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                          children: [
                            const Icon(
                              Icons.shopping_bag_outlined,
                              size: 56,
                              color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 12),
                            Text(
                              l10n.noDataFound,
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 16),
                            ElevatedButton.icon(
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                              label: Text(l10n.actionNewPurchase),
                              onPressed: () {
                                Navigator.push(
                                  context,
                                  MaterialPageRoute(
                                    builder: (_) => const CreatePurchasePage(),
                                  ),
                                ).then((_) => _onSearch());
                              },
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      );
                    }

                    return ListView.separated(
                      itemCount: purchases.length,
                      separatorBuilder: (context, index) =>
                          const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                        final pur = purchases[index];
                        final dateStr = DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd')
                            .format(pur.issueDate);

                        return Card(
                          child: Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                            child: Row(
                              children: [
                                Container(
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    color: const Color(0xFFF5F3FF),
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                                  ),
                                  child: const Icon(
                                    Icons.shopping_bag,
                                    color: Color(0xFF8B5CF6),
                                  ),
                                ),
                                const SizedBox(width: 16),
                                Expanded(
                                  child: Column(
                                    crossAxisAlignment:
                                        CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                    children: [
                                      Text(
                                        pur.purchaseNumber,
                                        style: const TextStyle(
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                          fontSize: 15,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                      Text(
                                        '${l10n.supplierLabel}: ${pur.supplierName} • $dateStr',
                                        style: const TextStyle(
                                          color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                                          fontSize: 13,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                ),
                                Column(
                                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                                  children: [
                                    Text(
                                      CurrencyFormatter.format(
                                        pur.finalTotal,
                                        currency: pur.currency,
                                        isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                                      ),
                                      style: const TextStyle(
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                        fontSize: 15,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    if (pur.remainingPayable > 0)
                                      Text(
                                        '${l10n.remainingBalanceLabel}: ${CurrencyFormatter.format(pur.remainingPayable, currency: pur.currency, isArabic: l10n.isArabic)}',
                                        style: const TextStyle(
                                          fontSize: 11,
                                          color: AppColors.error,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                    );
                  }
                  return const SizedBox.shrink();
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\reports\presentation\cubit\reports_cubit.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/repositories/invoice_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/entities/party.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/repositories/party_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/payments/domain/entities/payment.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/payments/domain/repositories/payment_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/purchases/domain/entities/purchase_invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/purchases/domain/repositories/purchase_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/domain/entities/sales_return.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/domain/repositories/sales_return_repository.dart';

enum ReportType {
  periodSales,
  collectionsAndDebts,
  customerDebts,
  supplierPayables,
  purchases,
  returnsAndRefunds,
  cancelledInvoices,
  customerStatement,
  supplierStatement,
}

class StatementEntry extends Equatable {
  final DateTime date;
  final String documentType; // 'Invoice', 'Payment', 'Return', 'Purchase'
  final String documentNumber;
  final double debit; // Increases what is owed
  final double credit; // Decreases what is owed
  final double runningBalance;
  final String notes;

  const StatementEntry({
    required this.date,
    required this.documentType,
    required this.documentNumber,
    required this.debit,
    required this.credit,
    required this.runningBalance,
    this.notes = '',
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    date,
    documentNumber,
    debit,
    credit,
    runningBalance,
  ];
}

class ReportData extends Equatable {
  final ReportType type;
  final DateTime fromDate;
  final DateTime toDate;
  final double totalGross;
  final double totalNet;
  final double totalCollected;
  final double totalRefunded;
  final List<Invoice> invoices;
  final List<PurchaseInvoice> purchases;
  final List<Payment> payments;
  final List<SalesReturn> returns;
  final List<StatementEntry> statementEntries;
  final Party? selectedParty;
  final String currency;

  const ReportData({
    required this.type,
    required this.fromDate,
    required this.toDate,
    this.totalGross = 0.0,
    this.totalNet = 0.0,
    this.totalCollected = 0.0,
    this.totalRefunded = 0.0,
    this.invoices = const [],
    this.purchases = const [],
    this.payments = const [],
    this.returns = const [],
    this.statementEntries = const [],
    this.selectedParty,
    this.currency = 'EGP',
  });

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    type,
    fromDate,
    toDate,
    totalGross,
    totalNet,
    totalCollected,
    totalRefunded,
    invoices,
    purchases,
    payments,
    returns,
    statementEntries,
    selectedParty,
  ];
}

abstract class ReportsState extends Equatable {
  const ReportsState();
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class ReportsInitial extends ReportsState {}

class ReportsLoading extends ReportsState {}

class ReportsLoaded extends ReportsState {
  final ReportData data;

  const ReportsLoaded(this.data);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [data];
}

class ReportsError extends ReportsState {
  final String message;

  const ReportsError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class ReportsCubit extends Cubit<ReportsState> {
  final InvoiceRepository invoiceRepository;
  final PurchaseRepository purchaseRepository;
  final PaymentRepository paymentRepository;
  final SalesReturnRepository returnRepository;
  final PartyRepository partyRepository;

  ReportsCubit({
    required this.invoiceRepository,
    required this.purchaseRepository,
    required this.paymentRepository,
    required this.returnRepository,
    required this.partyRepository,
  }) : super(ReportsInitial());

  Future<void> generateReport({
    required ReportType type,
    required DateTime fromDate,
    required DateTime toDate,
    String? partyId,
    String defaultCurrency = 'EGP',
  }) async {
    emit(ReportsLoading());
    try {
      final start = DateTime(fromDate.year, fromDate.month, fromDate.day);
      final end = DateTime(toDate.year, toDate.month, toDate.day, 23, 59, 59);

      switch (type) {
        case ReportType.periodSales:
          final all = await invoiceRepository.getInvoices(
            fromDate: start,
            toDate: end,
          );
          final active = all.where((i) => i.isIssued).toList();
          final gross = active.fold(0.0, (sum, i) => sum + i.finalTotal);
          final paid = active.fold(0.0, (sum, i) => sum + i.totalPaid);

          emit(
            ReportsLoaded(
              ReportData(
                type: type,
                fromDate: start,
                toDate: end,
                totalGross: gross,
                totalNet: gross,
                totalCollected: paid,
                invoices: active,
                currency: defaultCurrency,
              ),
            ),
          );
          break;

        case ReportType.cancelledInvoices:
          final all = await invoiceRepository.getInvoices(
            documentStatus: DocumentStatus.cancelled,
            fromDate: start,
            toDate: end,
          );
          final gross = all.fold(0.0, (sum, i) => sum + i.finalTotal);

          emit(
            ReportsLoaded(
              ReportData(
                type: type,
                fromDate: start,
                toDate: end,
                totalGross: gross,
                invoices: all,
                currency: defaultCurrency,
              ),
            ),
          );
          break;

        case ReportType.purchases:
          final all = await purchaseRepository.getPurchases(
            fromDate: start,
            toDate: end,
          );
          final active = all
              .where((p) => p.documentStatus == DocumentStatus.issued)
              .toList();
          final gross = active.fold(0.0, (sum, p) => sum + p.finalTotal);
          final paid = active.fold(0.0, (sum, p) => sum + p.amountPaid);

          emit(
            ReportsLoaded(
              ReportData(
                type: type,
                fromDate: start,
                toDate: end,
                totalGross: gross,
                totalCollected: paid,
                purchases: active,
                currency: defaultCurrency,
              ),
            ),
          );
          break;

        case ReportType.returnsAndRefunds:
          final returns = await returnRepository.getReturns(
            fromDate: start,
            toDate: end,
          );
          final refunded = returns.fold(
            0.0,
            (sum, r) => sum + r.totalRefundAmount,
          );

          emit(
            ReportsLoaded(
              ReportData(
                type: type,
                fromDate: start,
                toDate: end,
                totalRefunded: refunded,
                returns: returns,
                currency: defaultCurrency,
              ),
            ),
          );
          break;

        case ReportType.customerStatement:
          if (partyId == null) {
            emit(
              const ReportsError(
                'Customer must be selected for customer statement.',
              ),
            );
            return;
          }
          final party = await partyRepository.getPartyById(partyId);
          if (party == null) {
            emit(const ReportsError('Selected customer not found.'));
            return;
          }

          final invoices = await invoiceRepository.getInvoices(
            customerId: partyId,
            documentStatus: DocumentStatus.issued,
          );
          final payments = await paymentRepository.getPayments(
            partyId: partyId,
            isCustomerPayment: true,
          );
          final returns = await returnRepository.getReturns(
            customerId: partyId,
          );

          final entries = <StatementEntry>[];
          var running = party.openingReceivable;

          // 1. Invoices (Debit)
          for (final inv in invoices) {
            running += inv.finalTotal;
            entries.add(
              StatementEntry(
                date: inv.issueDate,
                documentType: 'Sales Invoice',
                documentNumber: inv.invoiceNumber,
                debit: inv.finalTotal,
                credit: 0.0,
                runningBalance: running,
              ),
            );
          }

          // 2. Payments (Credit)
          for (final pay in payments) {
            running -= pay.amount;
            entries.add(
              StatementEntry(
                date: pay.paymentDate,
                documentType: 'Payment Received',
                documentNumber: pay.referenceNumber.isNotEmpty
                    ? pay.referenceNumber
                    : pay.id.substring(0, 8),
                debit: 0.0,
                credit: pay.amount,
                runningBalance: running,
              ),
            );
          }

          // 3. Returns (Credit)
          for (final ret in returns) {
            running -= ret.totalRefundAmount;
            entries.add(
              StatementEntry(
                date: ret.returnDate,
                documentType: 'Sales Return',
                documentNumber: ret.returnNumber,
                debit: 0.0,
                credit: ret.totalRefundAmount,
                runningBalance: running,
              ),
            );
          }

          // Sort entries by date
          entries.sort((a, b) => a.date.compareTo(b.date));

          emit(
            ReportsLoaded(
              ReportData(
                type: type,
                fromDate: start,
                toDate: end,
                totalNet: running,
                statementEntries: entries,
                selectedParty: party,
                currency: defaultCurrency,
              ),
            ),
          );
          break;

        case ReportType.supplierStatement:
          if (partyId == null) {
            emit(
              const ReportsError(
                'Supplier must be selected for supplier statement.',
              ),
            );
            return;
          }
          final party = await partyRepository.getPartyById(partyId);
          if (party == null) {
            emit(const ReportsError('Selected supplier not found.'));
            return;
          }

          final purchases = await purchaseRepository.getPurchases(
            supplierId: partyId,
            documentStatus: DocumentStatus.issued,
          );
          final payments = await paymentRepository.getPayments(
            partyId: partyId,
            isCustomerPayment: false,
          );

          final entries = <StatementEntry>[];
          var running = party.openingPayable;

          for (final pur in purchases) {
            running += pur.finalTotal;
            entries.add(
              StatementEntry(
                date: pur.issueDate,
                documentType: 'Purchase Bill',
                documentNumber: pur.purchaseNumber,
                debit: pur.finalTotal,
                credit: 0.0,
                runningBalance: running,
              ),
            );
          }

          for (final pay in payments) {
            running -= pay.amount;
            entries.add(
              StatementEntry(
                date: pay.paymentDate,
                documentType: 'Supplier Payment',
                documentNumber: pay.referenceNumber.isNotEmpty
                    ? pay.referenceNumber
                    : pay.id.substring(0, 8),
                debit: 0.0,
                credit: pay.amount,
                runningBalance: running,
              ),
            );
          }

          entries.sort((a, b) => a.date.compareTo(b.date));

          emit(
            ReportsLoaded(
              ReportData(
                type: type,
                fromDate: start,
                toDate: end,
                totalNet: running,
                statementEntries: entries,
                selectedParty: party,
                currency: defaultCurrency,
              ),
            ),
          );
          break;

        case ReportType.collectionsAndDebts:
        case ReportType.customerDebts:
        case ReportType.supplierPayables:
          final allInvoices = await invoiceRepository.getInvoices(
            documentStatus: DocumentStatus.issued,
          );
          final allPurchases = await purchaseRepository.getPurchases(
            documentStatus: DocumentStatus.issued,
          );
          final allPayments = await paymentRepository.getPayments(
            fromDate: start,
            toDate: end,
          );

          final totalDebts = allInvoices.fold(
            0.0,
            (sum, i) => sum + i.remainingBalance,
          );
          final totalPayables = allPurchases.fold(
            0.0,
            (sum, p) => sum + p.remainingPayable,
          );
          final totalColl = allPayments
              .where((p) => p.isCustomerPayment)
              .fold(0.0, (sum, p) => sum + p.amount);

          emit(
            ReportsLoaded(
              ReportData(
                type: type,
                fromDate: start,
                toDate: end,
                totalGross: totalDebts,
                totalNet: totalPayables,
                totalCollected: totalColl,
                invoices: allInvoices,
                purchases: allPurchases,
                payments: allPayments,
                currency: defaultCurrency,
              ),
            ),
          );
          break;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ReportsError(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\reports\presentation\pages\reports_page.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/localization/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/responsive/responsive_layout.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/domain/entities/party.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/parties/presentation/cubit/parties_cubit.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/reports/presentation/cubit/reports_cubit.dart';

class ReportsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final ReportType? initialReportType;
  final String? initialPartyId;

  const ReportsPage({super.key, this.initialReportType, this.initialPartyId});

  @override
  State<ReportsPage> createState() => _ReportsPageState();
}

class _ReportsPageState extends State<ReportsPage> {
  late ReportType _selectedReport;
  DateTime _fromDate = DateTime.now().subtract(const Duration(days: 30));
  DateTime _toDate = DateTime.now();
  String? _selectedPartyId;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _selectedReport = widget.initialReportType ?? ReportType.periodSales;
    _selectedPartyId = widget.initialPartyId;

    context.read<PartiesCubit>().loadCustomers();
    context.read<PartiesCubit>().loadSuppliers();

    _generate();
  }

  void _generate() {
    context.read<ReportsCubit>().generateReport(
      type: _selectedReport,
      fromDate: _fromDate,
      toDate: _toDate,
      partyId: _selectedPartyId,
    );
  }

  Future<void> _pickDateRange() async {
    final picked = await showDateRangePicker(
      context: context,
      firstDate: DateTime(2020),
      lastDate: DateTime(2035),
      initialDateRange: DateTimeRange(start: _fromDate, end: _toDate),
    );
    if (picked != null) {
      setState(() {
        _fromDate = picked.start;
        _toDate = picked.end;
      });
      _generate();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final dateRangeStr =
        '${DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd').format(_fromDate)} - ${DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd').format(_toDate)}';

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.navReports)),
      body: ResponsiveContentContainer(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // Controls Card
            Card(
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        // Report Type selector
                        Expanded(
                          flex: 3,
                          child: DropdownButtonFormField<ReportType>(
                            initialValue: _selectedReport,
                            decoration: InputDecoration(
                              labelText: l10n.navReports,
                              isDense: true,
                            ),
                            items: [
                              DropdownMenuItem(
                                value: ReportType.periodSales,
                                child: Text(l10n.reportPeriodSales),
                              ),
                              DropdownMenuItem(
                                value: ReportType.purchases,
                                child: Text(l10n.reportPurchases),
                              ),
                              DropdownMenuItem(
                                value: ReportType.collectionsAndDebts,
                                child: Text(l10n.reportCollections),
                              ),
                              DropdownMenuItem(
                                value: ReportType.returnsAndRefunds,
                                child: Text(l10n.reportReturns),
                              ),
                              DropdownMenuItem(
                                value: ReportType.cancelledInvoices,
                                child: Text(l10n.reportCancelled),
                              ),
                              DropdownMenuItem(
                                value: ReportType.customerStatement,
                                child: Text('${l10n.statementTitle} (عميل)'),
                              ),
                              DropdownMenuItem(
                                value: ReportType.supplierStatement,
                                child: Text('${l10n.statementTitle} (مورد)'),
                              ),
                            ],
                            onChanged: (val) {
                              if (val != null) {
                                setState(() => _selectedReport = val);
                                _generate();
                              }
                            },
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 12),
                        // Date picker button
                        Expanded(
                          flex: 2,
                          child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.date_range, size: 16),
                            label: Text(
                              dateRangeStr,
                              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11),
                            ),
                            onPressed: _pickDateRange,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    if (_selectedReport == ReportType.customerStatement ||
                        _selectedReport == ReportType.supplierStatement) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      BlocBuilder<PartiesCubit, PartiesState>(
                        builder: (context, state) {
                          List<Party> parties = [];
                          if (_selectedReport == ReportType.customerStatement &&
                              state is CustomersLoaded) {
                            parties = state.customers;
                          } else if (_selectedReport ==
                                  ReportType.supplierStatement &&
                              state is SuppliersLoaded) {
                            parties = state.suppliers;
                          }

                          return DropdownButtonFormField<String?>(
                            initialValue: _selectedPartyId,
                            decoration: InputDecoration(
                              labelText:
                                  _selectedReport ==
                                      ReportType.customerStatement
                                  ? l10n.customerLabel
                                  : l10n.supplierLabel,
                              isDense: true,
                            ),
                            items: parties
                                .map(
                                  (p) => DropdownMenuItem(
                                    value: p.id,
                                    child: Text(p.name),
                                  ),
                                )
                                .toList(),
                            onChanged: (val) {
                              setState(() => _selectedPartyId = val);
                              _generate();
                            },
                          );
                        },
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            // Report Output
            Expanded(
              child: BlocBuilder<ReportsCubit, ReportsState>(
                builder: (context, state) {
                  if (state is ReportsLoading) {
                    return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
                  }
                  if (state is ReportsError) {
                    return Center(child: Text(state.message));
                  }
                  if (state is ReportsLoaded) {
                    final data = state.data;
                    return _buildReportContent(context, data);
                  }
                  return const SizedBox.shrink();
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildReportContent(BuildContext context, ReportData data) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);

    if (data.type == ReportType.customerStatement ||
        data.type == ReportType.supplierStatement) {
      // Ledger Statement Table
      return Card(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    '${l10n.statementTitle}: ${data.selectedParty?.name ?? ''}',
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      fontSize: 16,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Text(
                    '${l10n.currentBalanceLabel}: ${data.totalNet.toStringAsFixed(2)} ${data.currency}',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      fontSize: 16,
                      color: data.totalNet > 0
                          ? AppColors.warning
                          : AppColors.success,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const Divider(height: 24),
              Expanded(
                child: data.statementEntries.isEmpty
                    ? Center(child: Text(l10n.noDataFound))
                    : ListView.separated(
                        itemCount: data.statementEntries.length,
                        separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const Divider(),
                        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                          final entry = data.statementEntries[index];
                          final dateStr = DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd')
                              .format(entry.date);

                          return ListTile(
                            title: Text(
                              '${entry.documentType} #${entry.documentNumber}',
                            ),
                            subtitle: Text(dateStr),
                            trailing: Column(
                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                              children: [
                                Text(
                                  entry.debit > 0
                                      ? '+${entry.debit.toStringAsFixed(2)}'
                                      : '-${entry.credit.toStringAsFixed(2)}',
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                    color: entry.debit > 0
                                        ? AppColors.error
                                        : AppColors.success,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                Text(
                                  'الرصيد: ${entry.runningBalance.toStringAsFixed(2)}',
                                  style: const TextStyle(
                                    fontSize: 11,
                                    color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    // Period Invoices / Purchases Report
    return Card(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Text(
                  '${l10n.dashboardTotalSales}: ${data.totalGross.toStringAsFixed(2)} ${data.currency}',
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 15,
                  ),
                ),
                if (data.totalCollected > 0)
                  Text(
                    '${l10n.dashboardCollected}: ${data.totalCollected.toStringAsFixed(2)} ${data.currency}',
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: AppColors.success,
                    ),
                  ),
                if (data.totalRefunded > 0)
                  Text(
                    'المسترد: ${data.totalRefunded.toStringAsFixed(2)} ${data.currency}',
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: AppColors.error,
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
            const Divider(height: 24),
            Expanded(
              child:
                  (data.invoices.isEmpty &&
                      data.purchases.isEmpty &&
                      data.returns.isEmpty)
                  ? Center(child: Text(l10n.noDataFound))
                  : ListView.separated(
                      itemCount: data.invoices.isNotEmpty
                          ? data.invoices.length
                          : (data.purchases.isNotEmpty
                                ? data.purchases.length
                                : data.returns.length),
                      separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const Divider(),
                      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                        if (data.invoices.isNotEmpty) {
                          final inv = data.invoices[index];
                          return ListTile(
                            title: Text(inv.invoiceNumber),
                            subtitle: Text(
                              DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd').format(inv.issueDate),
                            ),
                            trailing: Text(
                              '${inv.finalTotal.toStringAsFixed(2)} ${inv.currency}',
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        } else if (data.purchases.isNotEmpty) {
                          final pur = data.purchases[index];
                          return ListTile(
                            title: Text(pur.purchaseNumber),
                            subtitle: Text(
                              '${pur.supplierName} • ${DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd').format(pur.issueDate)}',
                            ),
                            trailing: Text(
                              '${pur.finalTotal.toStringAsFixed(2)} ${pur.currency}',
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        } else {
                          final ret = data.returns[index];
                          return ListTile(
                            title: Text(ret.returnNumber),
                            subtitle: Text(
                              '${ret.customerName} • ${DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd').format(ret.returnDate)}',
                            ),
                            trailing: Text(
                              '${ret.totalRefundAmount.toStringAsFixed(2)} ${ret.currency}',
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                color: AppColors.error,
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        }
                      },
                    ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\returns\data\repositories\sales_return_repository_impl.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/hive_database.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/repositories/invoice_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/domain/entities/sales_return.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/domain/repositories/sales_return_repository.dart';

class SalesReturnRepositoryImpl implements SalesReturnRepository {
  final HiveDatabase database;
  final InvoiceRepository invoiceRepository;

  SalesReturnRepositoryImpl({
    required this.database,
    required this.invoiceRepository,
  });

  @override
  Future<List<SalesReturn>> getReturns({
    String? originalInvoiceId,
    String? customerId,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
  }) async {
    final box = database.salesReturnsBox;
    final results = <SalesReturn>[];

    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final ret = SalesReturn.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));

        if (originalInvoiceId != null &&
            ret.originalInvoiceId != originalInvoiceId) {
          continue;
        }
        if (customerId != null && ret.customerId != customerId) {
          continue;
        }
        if (fromDate != null &&
            ret.returnDate.isBefore(
              DateTime(fromDate.year, fromDate.month, fromDate.day),
            )) {
          continue;
        }
        if (toDate != null &&
            ret.returnDate.isAfter(
              DateTime(toDate.year, toDate.month, toDate.day, 23, 59, 59),
            )) {
          continue;
        }

        results.add(ret);
      }
    }

    results.sort((a, b) => b.returnDate.compareTo(a.returnDate));
    return results;
  }

  @override
  Future<SalesReturn?> getReturnById(String id) async {
    final box = database.salesReturnsBox;
    final map = box.get(id);
    if (map == null) return null;
    return SalesReturn.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
  }

  @override
  Future<double> getReturnedQuantityForItem(String invoiceItemId) async {
    final box = database.salesReturnsBox;
    var totalReturned = 0.0;

    for (final key in box.keys) {
      final map = box.get(key);
      if (map != null) {
        final ret = SalesReturn.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
        for (final item in ret.items) {
          if (item.invoiceItemId == invoiceItemId) {
            totalReturned += item.returnedQuantity;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    return totalReturned;
  }

  @override
  Future<void> saveReturn(SalesReturn salesReturn) async {
    // If refund method is account credit, apply credit on the original invoice balance
    if (salesReturn.refundMethod == RefundMethod.credit) {
      await invoiceRepository.recordReturnCreditOnInvoice(
        invoiceId: salesReturn.originalInvoiceId,
        creditAmount: salesReturn.totalRefundAmount,
      );
    }

    final box = database.salesReturnsBox;
    await box.put(salesReturn.id, salesReturn.toMap());
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\returns\domain\entities\sales_return.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

enum RefundMethod { cash, credit }

class SalesReturnItem extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String invoiceItemId;
  final String description;
  final double returnedQuantity;
  final double unitPrice;
  final double refundAmount;

  const SalesReturnItem({
    required this.id,
    required this.invoiceItemId,
    required this.description,
    required this.returnedQuantity,
    required this.unitPrice,
    required this.refundAmount,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'invoiceItemId': invoiceItemId,
      'description': description,
      'returnedQuantity': returnedQuantity,
      'unitPrice': unitPrice,
      'refundAmount': refundAmount,
    };
  }

  factory SalesReturnItem.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return SalesReturnItem(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? '',
      invoiceItemId: map['invoiceItemId'] as String? ?? '',
      description: map['description'] as String? ?? '',
      returnedQuantity: (map['returnedQuantity'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 1.0,
      unitPrice: (map['unitPrice'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      refundAmount: (map['refundAmount'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    id,
    invoiceItemId,
    description,
    returnedQuantity,
    unitPrice,
    refundAmount,
  ];
}

class SalesReturn extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String returnNumber;
  final DateTime returnDate;
  final String originalInvoiceId;
  final String originalInvoiceNumber;
  final String? customerId;
  final String customerName;
  final List<SalesReturnItem> items;
  final double totalRefundAmount;
  final String returnReason;
  final RefundMethod refundMethod;
  final String notes;
  final String currency;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  const SalesReturn({
    required this.id,
    required this.returnNumber,
    required this.returnDate,
    required this.originalInvoiceId,
    required this.originalInvoiceNumber,
    this.customerId,
    this.customerName = '',
    required this.items,
    required this.totalRefundAmount,
    required this.returnReason,
    this.refundMethod = RefundMethod.cash,
    this.notes = '',
    this.currency = 'EGP',
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'returnNumber': returnNumber,
      'returnDate': returnDate.toIso8601String(),
      'originalInvoiceId': originalInvoiceId,
      'originalInvoiceNumber': originalInvoiceNumber,
      'customerId': customerId,
      'customerName': customerName,
      'items': items.map((x) => x.toMap()).toList(),
      'totalRefundAmount': totalRefundAmount,
      'returnReason': returnReason,
      'refundMethod': refundMethod.name,
      'notes': notes,
      'currency': currency,
      'createdAt': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updatedAt': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory SalesReturn.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return SalesReturn(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? '',
      returnNumber: map['returnNumber'] as String? ?? '',
      returnDate:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['returnDate'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      originalInvoiceId: map['originalInvoiceId'] as String? ?? '',
      originalInvoiceNumber: map['originalInvoiceNumber'] as String? ?? '',
      customerId: map['customerId'] as String?,
      customerName: map['customerName'] as String? ?? '',
      items:
          (map['items'] as List<dynamic>?)
              ?.map(
                (x) => SalesReturnItem.fromMap(
                  Map<String, dynamic>.from(x as Map),
                ),
              )
              .toList() ??
          [],
      totalRefundAmount: (map['totalRefundAmount'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0,
      returnReason: map['returnReason'] as String? ?? '',
      refundMethod: RefundMethod.values.firstWhere(
        (e) => e.name == map['refundMethod'],
        orElse: () => RefundMethod.cash,
      ),
      notes: map['notes'] as String? ?? '',
      currency: map['currency'] as String? ?? 'EGP',
      createdAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['createdAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['updatedAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    id,
    returnNumber,
    returnDate,
    originalInvoiceId,
    items,
    totalRefundAmount,
    returnReason,
    refundMethod,
    createdAt,
  ];
}

import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/domain/entities/sales_return.dart';

abstract class SalesReturnRepository {
  Future<List<SalesReturn>> getReturns({
    String? originalInvoiceId,
    String? customerId,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
  });
  Future<SalesReturn?> getReturnById(String id);
  Future<void> saveReturn(SalesReturn salesReturn);
  Future<double> getReturnedQuantityForItem(String invoiceItemId);
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\returns\presentation\cubit\returns_cubit.dart ===

import 'package:decimal/decimal.dart';
import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/financial_calculator.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/money.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/sequence_manager.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/audit/domain/entities/audit_log.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/audit/domain/repositories/audit_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/repositories/invoice_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/domain/entities/sales_return.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/domain/repositories/sales_return_repository.dart';

abstract class ReturnsState extends Equatable {
  const ReturnsState();
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class ReturnsInitial extends ReturnsState {}

class ReturnsLoading extends ReturnsState {}

class ReturnsLoaded extends ReturnsState {
  final List<SalesReturn> returns;

  const ReturnsLoaded(this.returns);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [returns];
}

class ReturnOperationSuccess extends ReturnsState {
  final String message;
  final SalesReturn? salesReturn;

  const ReturnOperationSuccess(this.message, {this.salesReturn});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message, salesReturn];
}

class ReturnsError extends ReturnsState {
  final String message;

  const ReturnsError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class ReturnsCubit extends Cubit<ReturnsState> {
  final SalesReturnRepository returnRepository;
  final InvoiceRepository invoiceRepository;
  final SequenceManager sequenceManager;
  final AuditRepository auditRepository;

  ReturnsCubit({
    required this.returnRepository,
    required this.invoiceRepository,
    required this.sequenceManager,
    required this.auditRepository,
  }) : super(ReturnsInitial());

  Future<void> loadReturns({
    String? originalInvoiceId,
    String? customerId,
    DateTime? fromDate,
    DateTime? toDate,
  }) async {
    emit(ReturnsLoading());
    try {
      final list = await returnRepository.getReturns(
        originalInvoiceId: originalInvoiceId,
        customerId: customerId,
        fromDate: fromDate,
        toDate: toDate,
      );
      emit(ReturnsLoaded(list));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ReturnsError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<bool> createReturn({
    required String originalInvoiceId,
    required List<({InvoiceItem invoiceItem, double returnedQuantity})>
    itemsToReturn,
    required String returnReason,
    RefundMethod refundMethod = RefundMethod.cash,
    String notes = '',
  }) async {
    if (itemsToReturn.isEmpty) {
      emit(const ReturnsError('Must select at least one item to return.'));
      return false;
    }
    if (returnReason.trim().isEmpty) {
      emit(const ReturnsError('Return reason is required.'));
      return false;
    }

    emit(ReturnsLoading());
    try {
      final invoice = await invoiceRepository.getInvoiceById(originalInvoiceId);
      if (invoice == null) {
        emit(const ReturnsError('Original invoice not found.'));
        return false;
      }
      if (invoice.isCancelled) {
        emit(
          const ReturnsError(
            'Cannot process returns against a cancelled invoice.',
          ),
        );
        return false;
      }

      final returnItems = <SalesReturnItem>[];
      var totalRefund = 0.0;

      for (final itemReq in itemsToReturn) {
        if (itemReq.returnedQuantity <= 0) continue;

        // Verify remaining returnable quantity
        final alreadyReturned = await returnRepository
            .getReturnedQuantityForItem(itemReq.invoiceItem.id);
        final maxReturnable = itemReq.invoiceItem.quantity - alreadyReturned;

        if (itemReq.returnedQuantity > maxReturnable + 0.0001) {
          emit(
            ReturnsError(
              'Cannot return ${itemReq.returnedQuantity} of "${itemReq.invoiceItem.description}". Max returnable: $maxReturnable',
            ),
          );
          return false;
        }

        // Authoritative proportional refund calculation
        final refundMoney = FinancialCalculator.calculateReturnRefundAmount(
          returnedQuantity: Decimal.parse(itemReq.returnedQuantity.toString()),
          originalSoldQuantity: Decimal.parse(
            itemReq.invoiceItem.quantity.toString(),
          ),
          originalLineTotal: Money.fromNum(
            itemReq.invoiceItem.lineTotal,
            invoice.currency,
          ),
          originalInvoiceSubtotal: Money.fromNum(
            invoice.subtotal,
            invoice.currency,
          ),
          originalInvoiceFinalTotal: Money.fromNum(
            invoice.finalTotal,
            invoice.currency,
          ),
        );

        final itemRefund = refundMoney.toDouble();
        totalRefund += itemRefund;

        returnItems.add(
          SalesReturnItem(
            id: const Uuid().v4(),
            invoiceItemId: itemReq.invoiceItem.id,
            description: itemReq.invoiceItem.description,
            returnedQuantity: itemReq.returnedQuantity,
            unitPrice: itemReq.invoiceItem.unitPrice,
            refundAmount: itemRefund,
          ),
        );
      }

      final now = DateTime.now();
      final returnNumber = await sequenceManager.getNextReturnNumber(now);
      final returnId = const Uuid().v4();

      final salesReturn = SalesReturn(
        id: returnId,
        returnNumber: returnNumber,
        returnDate: now,
        originalInvoiceId: invoice.id,
        originalInvoiceNumber: invoice.invoiceNumber,
        customerId: invoice.customerId,
        customerName: invoice.customerName,
        items: returnItems,
        totalRefundAmount: totalRefund,
        returnReason: returnReason.trim(),
        refundMethod: refundMethod,
        notes: notes.trim(),
        currency: invoice.currency,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      );

      await returnRepository.saveReturn(salesReturn);

      await auditRepository.logAction(
        AuditLog(
          id: const Uuid().v4(),
          action: 'create_return',
          documentType: 'sales_return',
          documentId: salesReturn.id,
          description:
              'Processed return ${salesReturn.returnNumber} for invoice ${invoice.invoiceNumber}. Refund: $totalRefund ${invoice.currency}',
          timestamp: now,
        ),
      );

      emit(
        ReturnOperationSuccess(
          'Return processed successfully',
          salesReturn: salesReturn,
        ),
      );
      await loadReturns();
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ReturnsError(e.toString()));
      return false;
    }
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\returns\presentation\pages\create_return_page.dart ===

import 'package:decimal/decimal.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/localization/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/financial_calculator.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/money.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/responsive/responsive_layout.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/invoices/domain/entities/invoice.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/domain/entities/sales_return.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/domain/repositories/sales_return_repository.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/presentation/cubit/returns_cubit.dart';

class CreateReturnPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final Invoice originalInvoice;

  const CreateReturnPage({super.key, required this.originalInvoice});

  @override
  State<CreateReturnPage> createState() => _CreateReturnPageState();
}

class _CreateReturnPageState extends State<CreateReturnPage> {
  final _reasonController = TextEditingController();
  final _notesController = TextEditingController();
  RefundMethod _refundMethod = RefundMethod.cash;

  final Map<String, TextEditingController> _returnQtyControllers = {};
  final Map<String, double> _maxReturnable = {};
  bool _isLoadingLimits = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadLimits();
  }

  Future<void> _loadLimits() async {
    final returnRepo = context.read<SalesReturnRepository>();
    for (final item in widget.originalInvoice.items) {
      final returned = await returnRepo.getReturnedQuantityForItem(item.id);
      final max = (item.quantity - returned) > 0
          ? (item.quantity - returned)
          : 0.0;
      _maxReturnable[item.id] = max;
      _returnQtyControllers[item.id] = TextEditingController(text: '0');
    }
    if (mounted) {
      setState(() => _isLoadingLimits = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _reasonController.dispose();
    _notesController.dispose();
    for (final ctrl in _returnQtyControllers.values) {
      ctrl.dispose();
    }
    super.dispose();
  }

  double _calculateEstimatedRefund() {
    var totalRefund = 0.0;
    final inv = widget.originalInvoice;

    for (final item in inv.items) {
      final ctrl = _returnQtyControllers[item.id];
      if (ctrl != null) {
        final qty = double.tryParse(ctrl.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;
        if (qty > 0) {
          final refund = FinancialCalculator.calculateReturnRefundAmount(
            returnedQuantity: Decimal.parse(qty.toString()),
            originalSoldQuantity: Decimal.parse(item.quantity.toString()),
            originalLineTotal: Money.fromNum(item.lineTotal, inv.currency),
            originalInvoiceSubtotal: Money.fromNum(inv.subtotal, inv.currency),
            originalInvoiceFinalTotal: Money.fromNum(
              inv.finalTotal,
              inv.currency,
            ),
          );
          totalRefund += refund.toDouble();
        }
      }
    }
    return totalRefund;
  }

  void _submit() {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final reason = _reasonController.text.trim();
    if (reason.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(l10n.setupRequiredField),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    final itemsToReturn =
        <({InvoiceItem invoiceItem, double returnedQuantity})>[];
    for (final item in widget.originalInvoice.items) {
      final ctrl = _returnQtyControllers[item.id];
      if (ctrl != null) {
        final qty = double.tryParse(ctrl.text.trim()) ?? 0.0;
        if (qty > 0) {
          itemsToReturn.add((invoiceItem: item, returnedQuantity: qty));
        }
      }
    }

    if (itemsToReturn.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(l10n.setupRequiredField),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    context.read<ReturnsCubit>().createReturn(
      originalInvoiceId: widget.originalInvoice.id,
      itemsToReturn: itemsToReturn,
      returnReason: reason,
      refundMethod: _refundMethod,
      notes: _notesController.text.trim(),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);
    final inv = widget.originalInvoice;

    return BlocListener<ReturnsCubit, ReturnsState>(
      listener: (context, state) {
        if (state is ReturnOperationSuccess) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
              .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(state.message)));
          Navigator.pop(context);
        } else if (state is ReturnsError) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            SnackBar(
              content: Text(state.message),
              backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
            ),
          );
        }
      },
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.returnTitle)),
        body: _isLoadingLimits
            ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
            : ResponsiveContentContainer(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: [
                      // Header reference card
                      Card(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                '${l10n.originalInvoiceLabel}: ${inv.invoiceNumber}',
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  fontSize: 16,
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 4),
                              Text(
                                '${l10n.customerLabel}: ${inv.customerName.isNotEmpty ? inv.customerName : l10n.cashCustomer}',
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Returnable items
                      Card(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                l10n.itemsSection,
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  fontSize: 15,
                                ),
                              ),
                              const Divider(),
                              ...inv.items.map((item) {
                                final max = _maxReturnable[item.id] ?? 0.0;
                                final ctrl = _returnQtyControllers[item.id];

                                return Padding(
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                    vertical: 8.0,
                                  ),
                                  child: Row(
                                    children: [
                                      Expanded(
                                        flex: 4,
                                        child: Column(
                                          crossAxisAlignment:
                                              CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                          children: [
                                            Text(
                                              item.description,
                                              style: const TextStyle(
                                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                              ),
                                            ),
                                            Text(
                                              '${l10n.maxReturnableQty}: $max / ${item.quantity}',
                                              style: const TextStyle(
                                                fontSize: 11,
                                                color: AppColors
                                                    .textSecondaryLight,
                                              ),
                                            ),
                                          ],
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      const SizedBox(width: 12),
                                      Expanded(
                                        flex: 2,
                                        child: TextFormField(
                                          controller: ctrl,
                                          keyboardType:
                                              const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                                                decimal: true,
                                              ),
                                          decoration: InputDecoration(
                                            labelText: l10n.returnQtyLabel,
                                            isDense: true,
                                          ),
                                          onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                );
                              }),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Refund Method & Reason
                      Card(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                          child: Column(
                            children: [
                              DropdownButtonFormField<RefundMethod>(
                                initialValue: _refundMethod,
                                decoration: InputDecoration(
                                  labelText: l10n.refundMethodLabel,
                                ),
                                items: [
                                  DropdownMenuItem(
                                    value: RefundMethod.cash,
                                    child: Text(l10n.refundCash),
                                  ),
                                  DropdownMenuItem(
                                    value: RefundMethod.credit,
                                    child: Text(l10n.refundCredit),
                                  ),
                                ],
                                onChanged: (val) {
                                  if (val != null) {
                                    setState(() => _refundMethod = val);
                                  }
                                },
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 12),
                              TextField(
                                controller: _reasonController,
                                decoration: InputDecoration(
                                  labelText: l10n.returnReasonLabel,
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 12),
                              TextField(
                                controller: _notesController,
                                decoration: InputDecoration(
                                  labelText: l10n.notesLabel,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Total Refund Preview
                      Card(
                        color: AppColors.errorBackground,
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                          child: Row(
                            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                l10n.calculatedRefundAmount,
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                ),
                              ),
                              Text(
                                '${_calculateEstimatedRefund().toStringAsFixed(2)} ${inv.currency}',
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  fontSize: 18,
                                  color: AppColors.error,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 24),

                      ElevatedButton(
                        onPressed: _submit,
                        child: Text(l10n.confirmReturnBtn),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\returns\presentation\pages\returns_page.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/localization/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency_formatter.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/responsive/responsive_layout.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/returns/presentation/cubit/returns_cubit.dart';

class ReturnsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const ReturnsPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<ReturnsPage> createState() => _ReturnsPageState();
}

class _ReturnsPageState extends State<ReturnsPage> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    context.read<ReturnsCubit>().loadReturns();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: Text(l10n.navReturns)),
      body: ResponsiveContentContainer(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: BlocBuilder<ReturnsCubit, ReturnsState>(
          builder: (context, state) {
            if (state is ReturnsLoading) {
              return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
            }
            if (state is ReturnsLoaded) {
              final returns = state.returns;
              if (returns.isEmpty) {
                return Center(
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      const Icon(
                        Icons.assignment_return_outlined,
                        size: 56,
                        color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      Text(
                        l10n.noDataFound,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                );
              }

              return ListView.separated(
                itemCount: returns.length,
                separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(height: 8),
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  final ret = returns[index];
                  final dateStr = DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm')
                      .format(ret.returnDate);

                  return Card(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Row(
                            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                ret.returnNumber,
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  fontSize: 15,
                                  color: AppColors.primary,
                                ),
                              ),
                              Text(
                                CurrencyFormatter.format(
                                  ret.totalRefundAmount,
                                  currency: ret.currency,
                                  isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                                ),
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                  fontSize: 16,
                                  color: AppColors.error,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 4),
                          Text(
                            '${l10n.originalInvoiceLabel}: ${ret.originalInvoiceNumber} • ${ret.customerName.isNotEmpty ? ret.customerName : l10n.cashCustomer}',
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 13,
                              color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                            ),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            dateStr,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 11,
                              color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                            ),
                          ),
                          if (ret.returnReason.isNotEmpty) ...[
                            const SizedBox(height: 6),
                            Text(
                              '${l10n.returnReasonLabel}: ${ret.returnReason}',
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                fontSize: 12,
                                fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                          const Divider(height: 16),
                          ...ret.items.map(
                            (item) => Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                vertical: 2.0,
                              ),
                              child: Row(
                                mainAxisAlignment:
                                    MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                                children: [
                                  Text(
                                    '${item.description} (x${item.returnedQuantity})',
                                  ),
                                  Text(
                                    CurrencyFormatter.format(
                                      item.refundAmount,
                                      currency: ret.currency,
                                      isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              );
            }
            return const SizedBox.shrink();
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

                  subtitle,

                    fontSize: 12,
                    color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

  Widget _buildUnconfiguredCard(bool isAr) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 0,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
        side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.borderLight),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20.0),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.grey.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
              ),
              child: const Icon(
                Icons.person_off_outlined,
                size: 40,
                color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
              isAr
                  ? DeveloperConfig.unconfiguredTitleAr

              ),

            const SizedBox(height: 12),

              isAr ? 'الاسم' : 'Name',
              DeveloperConfig.developerName,
            ),

              _buildSpecRow(
                isAr ? 'البريد الإلكتروني' : 'Email',
                DeveloperConfig.developerEmail,
              ),

              const Divider(height: 16),
              _buildSpecRow(
                isAr ? 'تيليجرام' : 'Telegram',
                DeveloperConfig.developerTelegram,
              ),
            ],
            if (DeveloperConfig.developerWebsite.isNotEmpty) ...[
              const Divider(height: 16),
              _buildSpecRow(
                isAr ? 'الموقع الإلكتروني' : 'Website',
                DeveloperConfig.developerWebsite,
              ),
            ],
            if (DeveloperConfig.githubUrl.isNotEmpty) ...[
              const Divider(height: 16),
              _buildSpecRow(
                isAr ? 'المستودع البرمجي' : 'Repository',
                DeveloperConfig.githubUrl,
              ),
            ],
            if (DeveloperConfig.supportContact.isNotEmpty &&
                DeveloperConfig.supportContact !=
                    DeveloperConfig.developerTelegram) ...[
              const Divider(height: 16),
              _buildSpecRow(
                isAr ? 'قناة الدعم' : 'Support Contact',
                DeveloperConfig.supportContact,
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSpecRow(String label, String value) {
    return Row(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
      children: [

          label,
          style: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 13,
            color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,

        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 16),
        Flexible(
          child: Text(
            value,
            textAlign: TextAlign.end,
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 13,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
