// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\shop_setup\data\repositories\shop_profile_repository_impl.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/core/storage/hive_database.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/shop_setup/domain/entities/shop_profile.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/shop_setup/domain/repositories/shop_profile_repository.dart';

class ShopProfileRepositoryImpl implements ShopProfileRepository {
  final HiveDatabase database;

  ShopProfileRepositoryImpl(this.database);

  static const String _key = 'current_profile';

  @override
  Future<ShopProfile?> getProfile() async {
    final box = database.shopProfileBox;
    final map = box.get(_key);
    if (map == null) return null;
    return ShopProfile.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic>.from(map));
  }

  @override
  Future<void> saveProfile(ShopProfile profile) async {
    final box = database.shopProfileBox;
    await box.put(_key, profile.toMap());
  }

  @override
  Future<bool> hasCompletedSetup() async {
    final profile = await getProfile();
    return profile != null && profile.name.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\shop_setup\domain\entities\shop_profile.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';

class ShopProfile extends Equatable {
  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String businessType;
  final String phone;
  final String address;
  final String defaultCurrency;
  final String taxNumber;
  final bool defaultTaxEnabled;
  final double defaultTaxRate;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  const ShopProfile({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.businessType,
    this.phone = '',
    this.address = '',
    this.defaultCurrency = 'EGP',
    this.taxNumber = '',
    this.defaultTaxEnabled = false,
    this.defaultTaxRate = 15.0,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  ShopProfile copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? name,
    String? businessType,
    String? phone,
    String? address,
    String? defaultCurrency,
    String? taxNumber,
    bool? defaultTaxEnabled,
    double? defaultTaxRate,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return ShopProfile(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      businessType: businessType ?? this.businessType,
      phone: phone ?? this.phone,
      address: address ?? this.address,
      defaultCurrency: defaultCurrency ?? this.defaultCurrency,
      taxNumber: taxNumber ?? this.taxNumber,
      defaultTaxEnabled: defaultTaxEnabled ?? this.defaultTaxEnabled,
      defaultTaxRate: defaultTaxRate ?? this.defaultTaxRate,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toMap() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'businessType': businessType,
      'phone': phone,
      'address': address,
      'defaultCurrency': defaultCurrency,
      'taxNumber': taxNumber,
      'defaultTaxEnabled': defaultTaxEnabled,
      'defaultTaxRate': defaultTaxRate,
      'createdAt': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updatedAt': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    };
  }

  factory ShopProfile.fromMap(Map<String, dynamic> map) {
    return ShopProfile(
      id: map['id'] as String? ?? 'default_shop',
      name: map['name'] as String? ?? '',
      businessType: map['businessType'] as String? ?? '',
      phone: map['phone'] as String? ?? '',
      address: map['address'] as String? ?? '',
      defaultCurrency: map['defaultCurrency'] as String? ?? 'EGP',
      taxNumber: map['taxNumber'] as String? ?? '',
      defaultTaxEnabled: map['defaultTaxEnabled'] as bool? ?? false,
      defaultTaxRate: (map['defaultTaxRate'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 15.0,
      createdAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['createdAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt:
          DateTime.tryParse(map['updatedAt'] as String? ?? '') ??
          DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [
    id,
    name,
    businessType,
    phone,
    address,
    defaultCurrency,
    taxNumber,
    defaultTaxEnabled,
    defaultTaxRate,
    createdAt,
    updatedAt,
  ];
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\shop_setup\domain\repositories\shop_profile_repository.dart ===

import 'package:fatoura/features/shop_setup/domain/entities/shop_profile.dart';

abstract class ShopProfileRepository {
  Future<ShopProfile?> getProfile();
  Future<void> saveProfile(ShopProfile profile);
  Future<bool> hasCompletedSetup();
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\shop_setup\presentation\cubit\shop_setup_cubit.dart ===

import 'package:equatable/equatable.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/features/shop_setup/domain/entities/shop_profile.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/shop_setup/domain/repositories/shop_profile_repository.dart';

abstract class ShopSetupState extends Equatable {
  const ShopSetupState();
  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [];
}

class ShopSetupInitial extends ShopSetupState {}

class ShopSetupLoading extends ShopSetupState {}

class ShopSetupLoaded extends ShopSetupState {
  final ShopProfile? profile;
  final bool isCompleted;

  const ShopSetupLoaded({this.profile, required this.isCompleted});

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [profile, isCompleted];
}

class ShopSetupSuccess extends ShopSetupState {
  final ShopProfile profile;

  const ShopSetupSuccess(this.profile);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [profile];
}

class ShopSetupError extends ShopSetupState {
  final String message;

  const ShopSetupError(this.message);

  @override
  List<Object?> get props => [message];
}

class ShopSetupCubit extends Cubit<ShopSetupState> {
  final ShopProfileRepository repository;

  ShopSetupCubit(this.repository) : super(ShopSetupInitial());

  Future<void> loadProfile() async {
    emit(ShopSetupLoading());
    try {
      final profile = await repository.getProfile();
      final isCompleted = await repository.hasCompletedSetup();
      emit(ShopSetupLoaded(profile: profile, isCompleted: isCompleted));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ShopSetupError(e.toString()));
    }
  }

  Future<void> saveProfile({
    required String name,
    required String businessType,
    String phone = '',
    String address = '',
    String defaultCurrency = 'EGP',
    String taxNumber = '',
    bool defaultTaxEnabled = false,
    double defaultTaxRate = 15.0,
  }) async {
    if (name.trim().isEmpty) {
      emit(const ShopSetupError('Shop name cannot be empty.'));
      return;
    }
    if (businessType.trim().isEmpty) {
      emit(const ShopSetupError('Business type cannot be empty.'));
      return;
    }

    emit(ShopSetupLoading());
    try {
      final existing = await repository.getProfile();
      final now = DateTime.now();

      final profile = ShopProfile(
        id: existing?.id ?? 'shop_primary',
        name: name.trim(),
        businessType: businessType.trim(),
        phone: phone.trim(),
        address: address.trim(),
        defaultCurrency: defaultCurrency.trim().toUpperCase(),
        taxNumber: taxNumber.trim(),
        defaultTaxEnabled: defaultTaxEnabled,
        defaultTaxRate: defaultTaxRate,
        createdAt: existing?.createdAt ?? now,
        updatedAt: now,
      );

      await repository.saveProfile(profile);
      emit(ShopSetupSuccess(profile));
    } catch (e) {
      emit(ShopSetupError(e.toString()));
    }
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\features\shop_setup\presentation\pages\shop_setup_page.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';

import 'package:fatoura/core/localization/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/precision/currency_formatter.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/core/widgets/app_scaffold.dart';
import 'package:fatoura/features/shop_setup/presentation/cubit/shop_setup_cubit.dart';

class ShopSetupPage extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool isInitialSetup;

  const ShopSetupPage({super.key, this.isInitialSetup = true});

  @override
  State<ShopSetupPage> createState() => _ShopSetupPageState();
}

class _ShopSetupPageState extends State<ShopSetupPage> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _typeController = TextEditingController();
  final _phoneController = TextEditingController();
  final _addressController = TextEditingController();
  final _taxNumberController = TextEditingController();
  final _taxRateController = TextEditingController(text: '15.0');

  String _selectedCurrency = AppCurrencies.defaultCurrency;
  bool _taxEnabled = false;

  final List<String> _currencies = AppCurrencies.supportedCurrencyCodes;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final cubit = context.read<ShopSetupCubit>();
    if (cubit.state is ShopSetupLoaded) {
      final profile = (cubit.state as ShopSetupLoaded).profile;
      if (profile != null) {
        _nameController.text = profile.name;
        _typeController.text = profile.businessType;
        _phoneController.text = profile.phone;
        _addressController.text = profile.address;
        _taxNumberController.text = profile.taxNumber;
        _taxRateController.text = profile.defaultTaxRate.toString();
        _selectedCurrency = profile.defaultCurrency;
        _taxEnabled = profile.defaultTaxEnabled;
      }
    } else {
      cubit.loadProfile();
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _typeController.dispose();
    _phoneController.dispose();
    _addressController.dispose();
    _taxNumberController.dispose();
    _taxRateController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _submit() {
    if (_formKey.currentState?.validate() ?? false) {
      context.read<ShopSetupCubit>().saveProfile(
        name: _nameController.text,
        businessType: _typeController.text,
        phone: _phoneController.text,
        address: _addressController.text,
        defaultCurrency: _selectedCurrency,
        taxNumber: _taxNumberController.text,
        defaultTaxEnabled: _taxEnabled,
        defaultTaxRate: double.tryParse(_taxRateController.text) ?? 15.0,
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final l10n = AppLocalizations.of(context);

    return BlocConsumer<ShopSetupCubit, ShopSetupState>(
      listener: (context, state) {
        if (state is ShopSetupSuccess) {
          if (widget.isInitialSetup) {
            Navigator.of(context).pushReplacement(
              MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const AppScaffold()),
            );
          } else {
            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
                .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(l10n.successMsg)));
            Navigator.of(context).pop();
          }
        } else if (state is ShopSetupError) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            SnackBar(
              content: Text(state.message),
              backgroundColor: AppColors.error,
            ),
          );
        } else if (state is ShopSetupLoaded && state.profile != null) {
          final p = state.profile!;
          _nameController.text = p.name;
          _typeController.text = p.businessType;
          _phoneController.text = p.phone;
          _addressController.text = p.address;
          _taxNumberController.text = p.taxNumber;
          _taxRateController.text = p.defaultTaxRate.toString();
          setState(() {
            _selectedCurrency = p.defaultCurrency;
            _taxEnabled = p.defaultTaxEnabled;
          });
        }
      },
      builder: (context, state) {
        return Scaffold(
          appBar: AppBar(
            title: Text(l10n.setupTitle),
            automaticallyImplyLeading: !widget.isInitialSetup,
          ),
          body: Center(
            child: ConstrainedBox(
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 600),
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
                child: Form(
                  key: _formKey,
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: [
                      Center(
                        child: ClipRRect(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                          child: Image.asset(
                            'assets/images/logo.png',
                            width: 72,
                            height: 72,
                            errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) =>
                                Container(
                                  width: 72,
                                  height: 72,
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    color: AppColors.primary,
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                                  ),
                                  child: const Icon(
                                    Icons.receipt_long,
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    size: 40,
                                  ),
                                ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Text(
                        l10n.setupTitle,
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 22,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Text(
                        l10n.setupSubtitle,
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 13,
                          color: AppColors.textSecondaryLight,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 32),

                      // Shop Name
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _nameController,
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          labelText: l10n.shopNameLabel,
                          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.storefront_outlined),
                        ),
                        validator: (value) =>
                            value == null || value.trim().isEmpty
                            ? l10n.setupRequiredField
                            : null,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Business Type
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _typeController,
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          labelText: l10n.shopTypeLabel,
                          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.business_outlined),
                        ),
                        validator: (value) =>
                            value == null || value.trim().isEmpty
                            ? l10n.setupRequiredField
                            : null,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Phone Number
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _phoneController,
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          labelText: l10n.shopPhoneLabel,
                          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.phone_outlined),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Address
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _addressController,
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          labelText: l10n.shopAddressLabel,
                          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.location_on_outlined),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Default Currency Dropdown
                      DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                        initialValue: _currencies.contains(_selectedCurrency)
                            ? _selectedCurrency
                            : AppCurrencies.defaultCurrency,
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          labelText: l10n.defaultCurrencyLabel,
                          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.attach_money_outlined),
                        ),
                        items: _currencies
                            .map(
                              (c) => DropdownMenuItem(
                                value: c,
                                child: Text(
                                  CurrencyFormatter.getLocalizedDisplayName(
                                    c,
                                    isArabic: l10n.isArabic,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            )
                            .toList(),
                        onChanged: (val) {
                          if (val != null) {
                            setState(() => _selectedCurrency = val);
                          }
                        },
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Tax Number
                      TextFormField(
                        controller: _taxNumberController,
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          labelText: l10n.taxNumberLabel,
                          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.tag_outlined),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      // Default Tax Toggle
                      SwitchListTile(
                        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                        title: Text(l10n.taxEnabledLabel),
                        value: _taxEnabled,
                        activeThumbColor: AppColors.primary,
                        onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _taxEnabled = val),
                      ),

                      if (_taxEnabled) ...[
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        TextFormField(
                          controller: _taxRateController,
                          keyboardType: const TextInputType.numberWithOptions(
                            decimal: true,
                          ),
                          decoration: InputDecoration(
                            labelText: l10n.taxRateLabel,
                            prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.percent_outlined),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],

                      const SizedBox(height: 32),

                      ElevatedButton(
                        onPressed: state is ShopSetupLoading ? null : _submit,
                        child: state is ShopSetupLoading
                            ? const SizedBox(
                                width: 20,
                                height: 20,
                                child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                                  color: Colors.white,
                                  strokeWidth: 2,
                                ),
                              )
                            : Text(
                                widget.isInitialSetup
                                    ? l10n.completeSetupBtn
                                    : l10n.saveBtn,
                              ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_bloc/flutter_bloc.dart';
import 'package:flutter_localizations/flutter_localizations.dart';

import 'bootstrap.dart';
import 'core/localization/app_localizations.dart';
import 'core/settings/app_settings_cubit.dart';
import 'core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'core/widgets/app_scaffold.dart';
import 'features/backup/presentation/cubit/backup_cubit.dart';
import 'features/dashboard/presentation/cubit/dashboard_cubit.dart';
import 'features/invoices/presentation/cubit/invoices_cubit.dart';
import 'features/parties/presentation/cubit/parties_cubit.dart';
import 'features/payments/presentation/cubit/payments_cubit.dart';
import 'features/products/presentation/cubit/products_cubit.dart';
import 'features/purchases/presentation/cubit/purchases_cubit.dart';
import 'features/returns/presentation/cubit/returns_cubit.dart';
import 'features/reports/presentation/cubit/reports_cubit.dart';
import 'features/shop_setup/presentation/cubit/shop_setup_cubit.dart';
import 'features/shop_setup/presentation/pages/shop_setup_page.dart';

class FatouraApp extends StatelessWidget {
  final AppDependencies dependencies;

  const FatouraApp({super.key, required this.dependencies});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MultiRepositoryProvider(
      providers: [
        RepositoryProvider.value(value: dependencies.shopProfileRepository),
        RepositoryProvider.value(value: dependencies.partyRepository),
        RepositoryProvider.value(value: dependencies.productRepository),
        RepositoryProvider.value(value: dependencies.invoiceRepository),
        RepositoryProvider.value(value: dependencies.paymentRepository),
        RepositoryProvider.value(value: dependencies.returnRepository),
        RepositoryProvider.value(value: dependencies.purchaseRepository),
        RepositoryProvider.value(value: dependencies.auditRepository),
      ],
      child: MultiBlocProvider(
        providers: [
          BlocProvider.value(value: dependencies.appSettingsCubit),
          BlocProvider(
            create: (_) =>
                ShopSetupCubit(dependencies.shopProfileRepository)
                  ..loadProfile(),
          ),
          BlocProvider(
            create: (_) => DashboardCubit(
              invoiceRepository: dependencies.invoiceRepository,
              purchaseRepository: dependencies.purchaseRepository,
              paymentRepository: dependencies.paymentRepository,
              partyRepository: dependencies.partyRepository,
            ),
          ),
          BlocProvider(
            create: (_) => InvoicesCubit(
              invoiceRepository: dependencies.invoiceRepository,
              shopProfileRepository: dependencies.shopProfileRepository,
              sequenceManager: dependencies.sequenceManager,
              auditRepository: dependencies.auditRepository,
            ),
          ),
          BlocProvider(
            create: (_) => PaymentsCubit(
              paymentRepository: dependencies.paymentRepository,
              invoiceRepository: dependencies.invoiceRepository,
              auditRepository: dependencies.auditRepository,
            ),
          ),
          BlocProvider(
            create: (_) => PartiesCubit(dependencies.partyRepository),
          ),
          BlocProvider(
            create: (_) => ProductsCubit(dependencies.productRepository),
          ),
          BlocProvider(
            create: (_) => PurchasesCubit(
              purchaseRepository: dependencies.purchaseRepository,
              shopProfileRepository: dependencies.shopProfileRepository,
              sequenceManager: dependencies.sequenceManager,
              auditRepository: dependencies.auditRepository,
            ),
          ),
          BlocProvider(
            create: (_) => ReturnsCubit(
              returnRepository: dependencies.returnRepository,
              invoiceRepository: dependencies.invoiceRepository,
              sequenceManager: dependencies.sequenceManager,
              auditRepository: dependencies.auditRepository,
            ),
          ),
          BlocProvider(
            create: (_) => ReportsCubit(
              invoiceRepository: dependencies.invoiceRepository,
              purchaseRepository: dependencies.purchaseRepository,
              paymentRepository: dependencies.paymentRepository,
              returnRepository: dependencies.returnRepository,
              partyRepository: dependencies.partyRepository,
            ),
          ),
          BlocProvider(create: (_) => BackupCubit(dependencies.backupService)),
        ],
        child: BlocBuilder<AppSettingsCubit, AppSettingsState>(
          builder: (context, settingsState) {
            return MaterialApp(
              title: 'فاتورة - Fatoura',
              debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
              themeMode: settingsState.themeMode,
              theme: AppTheme.lightTheme,
              darkTheme: AppTheme.darkTheme,
              locale: settingsState.locale,
              supportedLocales: const [Locale('ar'), Locale('en')],
              localizationsDelegates: const [
                AppLocalizations.delegate,
                GlobalMaterialLocalizations.delegate,
                GlobalWidgetsLocalizations.delegate,
                GlobalCupertinoLocalizations.delegate,
              ],
              home: FutureBuilder<bool>(
                future: dependencies.shopProfileRepository.hasCompletedSetup(),
                builder: (context, snapshot) {
                  if (snapshot.connectionState == ConnectionState.waiting) {
                    return const Scaffold(
                      body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                    );
                  }
                  final hasCompleted = snapshot.data ?? false;
                  if (hasCompleted) {
                    return const AppScaffold();
                  }
                  return const ShopSetupPage(isInitialSetup: true);
                },
              ),
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\bootstrap.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import 'core/backup/backup_service.dart';
import 'core/settings/app_settings_cubit.dart';
import 'core/storage/hive_database.dart';
import 'core/storage/sequence_manager.dart';
import 'features/audit/data/repositories/audit_repository_impl.dart';
import 'features/audit/domain/repositories/audit_repository.dart';
import 'features/invoices/data/repositories/invoice_repository_impl.dart';
import 'features/invoices/domain/repositories/invoice_repository.dart';
import 'features/parties/data/repositories/party_repository_impl.dart';
import 'features/parties/domain/repositories/party_repository.dart';
import 'features/payments/data/repositories/payment_repository_impl.dart';
import 'features/payments/domain/repositories/payment_repository.dart';
import 'features/products/data/repositories/product_repository_impl.dart';
import 'features/products/domain/repositories/product_repository.dart';
import 'features/purchases/data/repositories/purchase_repository_impl.dart';
import 'features/purchases/domain/repositories/purchase_repository.dart';
import 'features/returns/data/repositories/sales_return_repository_impl.dart';
import 'features/returns/domain/repositories/sales_return_repository.dart';
import 'features/shop_setup/data/repositories/shop_profile_repository_impl.dart';
import 'features/shop_setup/domain/repositories/shop_profile_repository.dart';

class AppDependencies {
  final SharedPreferences sharedPreferences;
  final HiveDatabase hiveDatabase;
  final SequenceManager sequenceManager;
  final BackupService backupService;

  final ShopProfileRepository shopProfileRepository;
  final PartyRepository partyRepository;
  final ProductRepository productRepository;
  final InvoiceRepository invoiceRepository;
  final PaymentRepository paymentRepository;
  final SalesReturnRepository returnRepository;
  final PurchaseRepository purchaseRepository;
  final AuditRepository auditRepository;

  final AppSettingsCubit appSettingsCubit;

  AppDependencies({
    required this.sharedPreferences,
    required this.hiveDatabase,
    required this.sequenceManager,
    required this.backupService,
    required this.shopProfileRepository,
    required this.partyRepository,
    required this.productRepository,
    required this.invoiceRepository,
    required this.paymentRepository,
    required this.returnRepository,
    required this.purchaseRepository,
    required this.auditRepository,
    required this.appSettingsCubit,
  });
}

Future<AppDependencies> bootstrap() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();

  // 1. SharedPreferences
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();

  // 2. Hive Database
  final hiveDatabase = HiveDatabase.instance;
  await hiveDatabase.init();

  // 3. Infrastructure
  final sequenceManager = SequenceManager(hiveDatabase);
  final backupService = BackupService(hiveDatabase);

  // 4. Repositories
  final shopProfileRepository = ShopProfileRepositoryImpl(hiveDatabase);
  final partyRepository = PartyRepositoryImpl(hiveDatabase);
  final productRepository = ProductRepositoryImpl(hiveDatabase);
  final invoiceRepository = InvoiceRepositoryImpl(hiveDatabase);
  final auditRepository = AuditRepositoryImpl(hiveDatabase);
  final paymentRepository = PaymentRepositoryImpl(
    database: hiveDatabase,
    invoiceRepository: invoiceRepository,
  );
  final returnRepository = SalesReturnRepositoryImpl(
    database: hiveDatabase,
    invoiceRepository: invoiceRepository,
  );
  final purchaseRepository = PurchaseRepositoryImpl(hiveDatabase);

  // 5. Settings Cubit
  final appSettingsCubit = AppSettingsCubit(prefs);

  return AppDependencies(
    sharedPreferences: prefs,
    hiveDatabase: hiveDatabase,
    sequenceManager: sequenceManager,
    backupService: backupService,
    shopProfileRepository: shopProfileRepository,
    partyRepository: partyRepository,
    productRepository: productRepository,
    invoiceRepository: invoiceRepository,
    paymentRepository: paymentRepository,
    returnRepository: returnRepository,
    purchaseRepository: purchaseRepository,
    auditRepository: auditRepository,
    appSettingsCubit: appSettingsCubit,
  );
}

// === FILE: D:\Projects\fatoura\lib\main.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'app.dart';
import 'bootstrap.dart';

void main() async {
  final dependencies = await bootstrap();
  runApp(FatouraApp(dependencies: dependencies));
}
